Motivation: 现有基于图像拼接或视频稳像的多相机视频对齐方法受限于两相机或相机固定线性排序的强假设，无法处理任意、时变的视角相交图与快速相机运动。

Method: 采用受空间变换器启发的网络在短时窗内联合处理所有视角帧，输出per-view/per-time单应矩阵；优化分两阶段：几何引导对齐 + 基于外观的细化，两阶段均施加多视角与时间一致性约束；无需场景特定超参调优，支持任意及时变视角相交图。

Result: 在标准双视角视频基准上达到或超过SOTA；并构建了含两台以上自由移动相机的标注数据集，建立了该场景下的首个benchmark。

Conclusion: MViS提供了轻量、免调参、鲁棒的多视角视频几何对齐方案，并首次将多相机自由运动视频对齐问题形式化与基准化。

Abstract: We address geometric video alignment for the same scene captured by multiple freely moving cameras. Existing methods, which are based on image stitching or video stabilization, are typically limited to two cameras or must assume the restrictive setting of a fixed linear ordering of the cameras. We propose MViS, a lightweight test-time optimization framework for joint multi-view alignment with temporal consistency. MViS uses a spatial-transformer-inspired net to jointly process frames from all views within a short temporal window and predict per-view, per-time homographies. Optimization proceeds in two stages: geometry-guided alignment followed by appearance-based refinement, with both stages enforcing multi-view and temporal consistency across the sequence. The method requires no scene-specific hyperparameter tuning, handles arbitrary and time-varying view-intersection graphs, and remains robust under rapid camera motion. On a standard two-view video benchmark, MViS matches or surpasses the state of the art. To facilitate evaluation in more complex scenarios, we introduce an annotated dataset with more than two freely moving cameras and establish the first benchmark for this setting. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [73] [MSR-ABC: Complementary Feature Fusion for Clear Cell Renal Cell Carcinoma Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09583)
*Yuan Liang,Sourav Bhattacharjee,Abraham Campbell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MSR-ABC全局—局部框架，融合MedVAE、MedSigLIP与放射组学三源表征，通过跨表征注意力、放射组学辅助监督与门控残差融合完成ccRCC CT分类，内部AUROC 0.806、外部0.879。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过CT对透明细胞肾细胞癌(ccRCC)分型具有临床价值，但简单拼接重建特征、图文嵌入与放射组学描述符难以充分利用三者的互补信息。

Method: 构建全局—局部融合框架：空间匹配的局部图像描述符经跨表征注意力交互；放射组学辅助监督约束池化后的局部表征保留定量肿瘤属性；门控残差机制将局部信息与全局摘要融合；辅以面积感知池化与视觉/软文本提示。在396例KiTS23患者级划分与111例TCGA/TCIA外部队列上评估，报告三个训练种子的平均AUROC及患者自助法95%置信区间。

Result: 内部平均AUROC 0.806，外部0.879；内部较MedVAE—放射组学全局模型与三源拼接分别提升3.35与3.61个百分点。移除任一来源均在两队列上降低AUROC，其中移除放射组学降幅最大；单独移除跨表征注意力、辅助监督或门控融合亦降低内部AUROC。

Conclusion: 结果与"集成架构内各组件贡献互补"的解释一致，支持进一步研究结构化表征融合用于无创肾肿瘤表征。

Abstract: Clear cell renal cell carcinoma (ccRCC) classification from computed tomography (CT) may benefit from representations that capture different aspects of tumour phenotype. However, simply concatenating reconstruction-derived features, image--text embeddings, and radiomic descriptors may not fully exploit their complementary information. We propose MSR-ABC, a global--local framework integrating MedVAE, MedSigLIP, and radiomics. Spatially matched local image descriptors interact through cross-representation attention, while radiomic auxiliary supervision encourages the pooled local representation to retain quantitative tumour attributes. A gated residual mechanism integrates local information with global summaries. Area-aware pooling and visual and soft-text prompting complete the framework. We evaluate discrimination using a patient-level split of 396 KiTS23 cases and an external cohort of 111 TCGA/TCIA cases, reporting mean AUROC over three training seeds with 95% patient-bootstrap confidence intervals. MSR-ABC achieves mean AUROCs of 0.806 internally and 0.879 externally. Internally, it exceeds global MedVAE--radiomics and three-source concatenation by 3.35 and 3.61 AUROC percentage points, respectively. Removing any source lowers mean AUROC in both cohorts, with the largest decreases following radiomics removal. Individual removal of cross-representation attention, auxiliary supervision, or gated fusion also lowers internal mean AUROC. These findings are consistent with complementary contributions within the integrated architecture and support further investigation of structured representation fusion for non-invasive renal tumour characterisation.

</details>


### [74] [Dual-Region Slice Aggregation for CT-Based Renal Tumor Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09599)
*Yuan Liang,Sourav Bhattacharjee,Abraham Campbell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出区域感知架构：从冻结的DINOv3特征图中分别池化肿瘤区与0–10mm瘤周上下文，共享位置感知切片聚合器后接仅训练期PCA，再用两个标量门控与影像组学融合，用于CT的ccRCC分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ccRCC是成人最常见肾癌，需在CT上做良性/恶性或分级分类；现有冻结视觉特征缺乏区域与切片维度上的针对性适配。

Method: 双区域（肿瘤+0–10mm瘤周）分别池化共享DINOv3特征；共享位置感知切片聚合器；固定“仅训练”PCA降维；两标量特征门与影像组学融合。含单肿瘤读出、early/late-PCA与重构对照消融。

Result: KiTS23内部测试AUC 0.8078±0.0154，111例外部队列AUC 0.9094±0.0015；相对单肿瘤读出分别提升1.50与2.34个百分点；early-PCA与重构对照内部AUC更低，外部AUC早/晚PCA相近。

Conclusion: 区域读出与切片聚合共同决定冻结视觉特征适配于肾脏CT的效果，瘤周上下文带来稳定增益。

Abstract: Clear cell renal cell carcinoma (ccRCC) is the most common kidney cancer in adults. We present a region-aware architecture for CT-based ccRCC classification. Tumor and 0--10 mm peritumoral context are pooled separately from shared frozen DINOv3 feature maps. A shared position-aware slice aggregator precedes fixed training-only principal component analysis (PCA). The resulting representations are fused with radiomics using two scalar feature gates. Evaluation on KiTS23 and 111 external cases yields internal test and external AUCs of 0.8078\pm0.0154 and 0.9094\pm0.0015. Compared with matched single-tumor readout, mean AUC increases by 1.50 and 2.34 percentage points, respectively. Early-PCA and reconstruction controls yield lower internal mean AUCs, while external AUC is similar for early and late PCA. These findings highlight the joint role of regional readout and slice aggregation in adapting frozen visual features to renal CT.

</details>


### [75] [CRTrack-Cap: A New Benchmark for Cross-View Dense Video Object Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09617)
*Xiangqun Zhang,Likai Wang,Zekun Qian,Rui Zhou,Ruize Han,Wei Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出跨视角稠密视频目标描述（CVDVOC）任务，构建CRTrack-Cap数据集，并给出融合VECA轨迹关联与CEG跨视角证据门控的参考框架及CV-CHOTA评测指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DVOC逐相机独立处理，同一人物在不同视角产生分离的局部轨迹与字幕，缺乏统一描述，且跨视角互补外观证据未被利用。

Method: 形式化跨视角DVOC任务；由CRTrack审计文本-身份链接并转化为规范字幕，构建CRTrack-Cap（425条全局轨迹、838条局部轨迹、13个场景、335个身份规范字幕）；将CHOTA推广为CV-CHOTA联合评估跟踪与描述；框架由View-Exclusive Cross-Camera Association（VECA）分组局部轨迹，并用Conflict-Evidence Gating（CEG）按跨视角可靠性/分歧融合字幕证据。

Result: 对比三种经MvMHAT适配的单视角方法，框架在场景不相交的域内与跨域测试上CV-CHOTA分别为83.43与33.48。

Conclusion: CRTrack-Cap与所提框架为跨相机视角的一致人物描述提供了基准与强基线，但跨域泛化明显不足。

Abstract: Dense video object captioning (DVOC) localizes, tracks, and describes object trajectories within a video. Existing methods process each camera view independently, producing separate camera-local tracklets and captions for the same person across cameras. These outputs lack a unified description and leave complementary appearance evidence across views unused. We therefore formulate cross-view DVOC (CVDVOC), which associates these tracklets into scene-level global trajectories and generates one caption for each trajectory from synchronized views. To support this task, we construct CRTrack-Cap from CRTrack by auditing text-identity links and converting usable referring expressions into canonical captions. It retains 425 global trajectories and 838 local tracklets across 13 scenes, with canonical captions for 335 identities. We adapt CHOTA to the cross-view setting as CV-CHOTA to jointly evaluate tracking and caption quality. Our reference framework combines View-Exclusive Cross-Camera Association (VECA) for grouping tracklets with Conflict-Evidence Gating (CEG) for integrating caption evidence using its reliability and disagreement across views. Against three single-view methods adapted with MvMHAT for cross-view association, our framework achieves CV-CHOTA scores of 83.43 and 33.48 on the scene-disjoint in-domain and cross-domain tests, respectively. Together, CRTrack-Cap and our framework provide a benchmark and a strong baseline for describing the same person consistently across camera views.

</details>


### [76] [Breaking the Privacy Promise: Gradient Inversion Attacks on Federated Low-Rank Adapted Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09652)
*Ritik K. Badiya,Siladittya Manna,Ashmit Sinha,M Yashwanth,Anirban Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究了LoRA适配的ViT在联邦学习中仍存在隐私泄漏风险，并提出"空间锚定生成反演"框架：在客户端微调时引入服务器指定的可训练位置嵌入，利用位置梯度作为逐层空间锚点，结合生成先验提升反演重建质量。CIFAR-10上PSNR提升10.14 dB、SSIM提升0.45、LPIPS提升0.098；CelebA和CARS分别提升7.83/11.74 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PEFT（尤其LoRA）因低计算与通信开销被广泛用于联邦学习，但人们普遍假设其低秩更新会削弱隐私泄漏风险。作者希望验证这一假设：LoRA梯度信息减少与冻结位置嵌入导致空间线索缺失两种耦合模糊性，会限制梯度反演攻击的重建质量。

Method: 提出空间锚定生成反演框架：在客户端微调阶段将服务器指定的可训练位置嵌入注入ViT各块，使位置参数产生梯度，作为逐层空间锚点补充位置信息；同时引入生成先验将重建约束在真实图像流形上，抑制重复纹理与patch置换伪影。该方法作为模块加载于GIAS攻击之上，在FedSGD与FedAvg设定下评测。

Result: FedSGD设定下CIFAR-10的PSNR提升10.14 dB、SSIM提升0.45、LPIPS提升0.098；高分辨率CelebA和CARS数据集PSNR分别提升7.83 dB和11.74 dB；在更大batch size及FedAvg设定下增益依然保持。

Conclusion: LoRA类PEFT本身并不能阻止联邦学习中的数据泄漏，LoRA梯度仍足以支撑高质量重建；精心设计的位置嵌入注入可显著增强隐私暴露程度，提示FL系统设计需将PEFT与结构设计联合考虑以防泄漏。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has been increasingly adopted to fine-tune pretrained models with low computational cost and is therefore also preferred in the federated learning paradigm to reduce computational and communication overhead. In this work, we investigate the sparsely explored problem of the risk of private data leakage in LoRA-adapted Vision Transformers (ViTs), where low-rank updates weaken the gradient signal available to a server with access to individual client updates. We show that quality of reconstruction is limited by two coupled ambiguities: reduced information in LoRA gradients and missing spatial cues from frozen positional embeddings. To address this, we propose a spatially anchored generative inversion framework that incorporates server-prescribed trainable positional embeddings into ViT blocks during fine-tuning at clients. The resulting positional gradients act as layer-wise spatial anchors, providing additional positional cues, while the generative prior keeps reconstructions on a realistic image manifold, reducing duplicate-data and patch-permutation artifacts, {which produce similar gradients as those produced when the target client images are used}. Adding our proposed spatial anchoring to GIAS improves PSNR by 10.14 dB, SSIM by 0.45, and LPIPS by 0.098 on CIFAR-10, as well as PSNR gains of 7.83 and 11.74 dB on high-resolution CelebA and CARS datasets, respectively, in the FedSGD setting. Such gains in reconstructed image quality metrics are also maintained at higher batch sizes and in FedAvg settings. These results indicate that LoRA-based PEFT does not, by itself, prevent data leakage in FL, and carefully incorporated positional embeddings can help in exposing private information.

</details>


### [77] [Decoupled Mode Connectivity for Base-to-Novel Generalization in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09655)
*Imad Eddine Marouf,Khalid Oublal,Enzo Tartaglione,Stéphane Lathuilière*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DMC将提示学习中的基类专业化与泛化目标解耦为两个提示，通过文本特征空间的线性模式连通(LMC)与Visual Anchor KL正则保持零样本相似度分布，测试时以固定系数合并，推理开销等同单提示。在11个数据集上对CoOp/KgCoOp均超越MergeTune，且插值曲线能突破单提示工作点、外推基类-新类前沿。论文还提出类置换不变性(CPI)约束，并用Fano不等式说明违反CPI会以互信息项下界化新类精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交叉熵目标使提示偏向基类专业化，而泛化到未见类需贴近CLIP零样本特征空间；二者作用于同一提示导致梯度冲突，实验表明单提示损失设计难以超越cosine基线。因此需要解耦两类目标。

Method: 从基类训练提示出发，分别训练专业化提示与泛化提示；测试时按固定系数合并文本特征（单提示推理成本）；引入文本特征空间的LMC走廊使端点间插值分类损失保持较低；加入Visual Anchor KL项在专业化过程中保持每张图像的零样本类相似度分布；提出正则器应满足类置换不变性并用Fano不等式分析其必要性。

Result: 11个数据集上，DMC在CoOp与KgCoOp设置下基类/新类精度调和平均值均优于MergeTune；插值曲线越过单提示工作点，说明是移动前沿而非沿前沿折中。

Conclusion: 提示学习中的目标冲突可通过提示级解耦与模式连通性合并缓解，配合CPI兼容正则可在不增加推理成本下同时提升基类与新类精度。

Abstract: Prompt learning adapts vision-language models such as CLIP by optimizing a short learnable prompt. The cross-entropy objective pulls this prompt toward base-class specialization, whereas generalization to unseen classes benefits from staying close to CLIP's zero-shot feature space. Because both objectives act on the same prompt, their gradients conflict at convergence, and in our experiments no single-prompt loss design clearly improves on the cosine baseline. We propose Decoupled Mode Connectivity (DMC), which gives each objective its own prompt. Starting from a base-trained prompt, DMC trains a specialization prompt and a generalization prompt, and merges their text features with a fixed coefficient at test time, at the inference cost of a single prompt. A linear mode connectivity (LMC) corridor in text-feature space keeps the classification loss low for every classifier interpolated between the two endpoints, and a Visual Anchor KL term preserves CLIP's zero-shot class-similarity distribution of each image during specialization. We further argue that such regularizers should satisfy class-permutation invariance (CPI), and show via Fano's inequality, in an idealized model, that violating CPI lower-bounds the drop in novel accuracy by a term proportional to the mutual information between the prompt and the base-class labels. Across 11 datasets, DMC improves the average harmonic mean of base and novel accuracy over MergeTune for both CoOp and KgCoOp, and its interpolation curves pass beyond the single-prompt operating point, shifting the base-novel frontier rather than trading along it. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [78] [Video2Asset: Online Construction of Persistent 3D Street-Scene Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09661)
*Zihan Zheng,Chunlei Cai,Zhenlong Wu,Linjun Li,Yichen Gong,Xiaoyun Zhang,Qiang Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Video2Asset：以occupancy为中间表示，通过历史载体引导的实例解码与身份编码器构建身份感知观测，并用累积证据关联（身份/几何/外观）实现多视角视频下的在线持久3D资产构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线3D街景资产构建需在运动与遮挡下累积间歇性部分观测，避免目标身份碎片化与跨物体证据混淆；现有方法难以在大范围内维持一致的实例身份。

Method: 以occupancy为中间表示：Historical Carrier Guidance引导实例解码，identity encoder融合实例对齐的3D特征与多视角外观；Accumulated-Evidence Association在资产记录内比对身份、几何与外观证据以判定身份继承并更新世界坐标状态；由资产库实例化网格，并与occupancy导出的静态背景合成，支持网格替换、物体删除与轨迹编辑。

Result: 在多个数据集上，跨多种occupancy模型均提升了时间身份一致性与资产质量，nuScenes（SGE）上资产碎片化降低15.4%。

Conclusion: 说明了Occupancy驱动的在线身份关联与资产化流程对动态街景的可编辑重建有效，但实验限于自驾街景数据集且主要提升为碎片化指标，缺乏与生成式/前馈重建强基线的对比。

Abstract: Online 3D street-scene asset construction turns multi-view video into a continually updated scene of independently editable objects. The central challenge is to accumulate partial, intermittent observations under motion and occlusion without fragmenting identities or mixing evidence across objects. To address this challenge, we propose Video2Asset, an online framework for constructing persistent 3D assets from multi-view video. Using occupancy as an intermediate representation, we construct identity-aware observations with Historical Carrier Guidance for instance decoding and an identity encoder fusing instance-aligned 3D features with multi-view appearance. Accumulated-Evidence Association compares current observations with identity, geometric, and appearance evidence accumulated within asset records to determine identity inheritance and update world-coordinate states. We instantiate meshes from an asset library using these states and combine them with an occupancy-derived static background to support scene visualization, mesh replacement, object deletion, and trajectory editing. Experiments on several datasets demonstrate improvements in temporal identity consistency and asset quality across multiple occupancy models, including a 15.4% reduction in asset fragmentation on nuScenes with SGE.

</details>


### [79] [Dual Architecture Fusion Vehicle Lidar 3D Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09664)
*Muyun Li,Weizheng Jin,Yuan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出融合3D稀疏卷积分支与Transformer分支的混合骨干网络SpconvTransformer，在3D检测任务上达到81.45%/88.09%精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D卷积神经网络感受野有限，难以建模体素间长程关系；需融合局部特征与全局上下文以提升3D检测性能。

Method: 双分支骨干：卷积分支用3D稀疏卷积与子流形卷积提取局部体素特征与语义；Transformer分支用自注意力建模体素间远程依赖、借助大感受野捕获上下文；两分支特征融合形成局部+全局表征。

Result: 3D目标检测平均精度81.45%，鸟瞰图检测88.09%，平均方向相似度91.10%，优于现有算法。

Conclusion: 卷积-Transformer混合骨干可有效突破3D CNN感受野限制，是提升点云3D检测性能的可行架构方案。

Abstract: In this paper, a 3D backbone network (SpconvTransformer) based on the fusion of convolutional architecture branch and Transformer architecture branch is proposed. The convolution branch uses 3D sparse convolution and submanifold convolution to learn local voxel features and semantic information, and the Transformer branch uses the self-attention mechanism to model the remote relationship between voxels, uses the broad receptive field to capture rich context information, and complementing the advantages of the two architectures by fusing the local features extracted from the two branches and the global features solves the problem of limited receptive fields of 3D convolutional neural networks. The experimental results show that the average accuracy of SpconvTransformer in 3D object detection and bird's-eye view detection tasks reaches 81.45% and 88.09%, respectively, and the average directional similarity reaches 91.10%, which is significantly better than other existing algorithms.

</details>


### [80] [PoseRieFlow: Riemannian Flow Matching for Whole-Body Text-to-Pose Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09692)
*Jan Spindler,Bardia Ardakanian,Patrick Stotko,Reinhard Klein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PoseRieFlow：21.6M参数的流匹配框架，在SMPL-H关节旋转乘积流形上直接做文本条件全身3D姿态生成，用部位token化DiT聚合52关节为8个身体部位token（双手各专设token），沿黎曼测地线从SO(3)高斯先验输运到数据分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到姿态生成中，现有方法要么忽略手指关节，要么在欧氏空间建模本属于非欧几何的旋转。作者希望在旋转流形上直接生成，同时以紧凑模型实现包含精细手部关节的全身姿态，兼顾运动学精度与计算规模。

Method: (1) 在SMPL-H 52个关节旋转的乘积流形上定义流匹配，回归text-conditioned向量场，将样本从等方SO(3)高斯先验沿黎曼测地线输运到数据分布；(2) 部位token化Diffusion Transformer：52关节聚合为8个身体部位token，每只手分配独立token与非线性投影器；(3) 部位专用解码器恢复逐关节分辨率，缩短序列长度并提升运动学保真度。

Result: 仅21.6M可训练参数即可生成带精细手部关节的SMPL-H姿态，远小于近期LLM式生成器；在PoseScript上取得所有可生成手部方法中最低的全身运动学误差；每个实际推理预算下黎曼形式均优于欧氏对应方案，分布与检索指标二者相当。

Conclusion: 在旋转流形上做条件流匹配，配合部位级token化与解码器，可以用极小模型规模获得高运动学精度的全身姿态生成；流形建模的收益主要体现在运动学正确性而非分布/检索指标上。不足：仅在PoseScript上验证，缺少与下游驱动/重定向和真实系统集成的证据。

Abstract: We present PoseRieFlow, a compact flow-matching framework for text-conditioned generation of whole-body 3D human poses. Prior text-to-pose methods either neglect articulated hands or model rotations in Euclidean space despite their non-Euclidean geometry. PoseRieFlow instead formulates generation directly on the product manifold of SMPL-H joint rotations, learning a text-conditioned vector field that transports samples from an isotropic $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ Gaussian prior to the data distribution along Riemannian geodesics. A part-tokenized Diffusion Transformer aggregates the 52 joints into eight body-part tokens, allocating a dedicated token and nonlinear projector to each hand, and restores per-joint resolution through part-specific decoders, shortening the sequence while improving kinematic fidelity over per-joint tokenization. With only 21.6M trainable parameters, PoseRieFlow supports expressive SMPL-H generation with detailed hand articulation at a fraction of the scale of recent LLM-based generators. On PoseScript, it attains the lowest whole-body kinematic error among all hand-producing methods, with consistent kinematic gains from the Riemannian formulation over its Euclidean counterpart at every practical inference budget, while distributional and retrieval metrics remain comparable between them.

</details>


### [81] [Direct Regression and Geometric Recovery for Event-based Spacecraft Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09717)
*Pruthvi Nandan Janga,Kashyap Hegde Kota,Bharatesh Chakravarthi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文对两种独立开发的合成到真实（sim-to-real）事件相机航天器 6-DoF 位姿估计系统进行对比系统研究：模块化方案（关键点检测+无标签目标域自适应+几何位姿恢复+时序平滑）与直接回归方案（全局/裁剪事件表示+表观尺度线索+物理启发式合成退化+特征一致性正则）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具备高时间分辨率与高动态范围，适合非合作航天器位姿估计，但合成数据训练的模型须泛化到真实传感器的噪声、背景活动与对比度变化；需厘清不同系统设计在真实事件数据上的误差特性差异。

Method: 在统一 SPARK 2026 隐藏测试协议下，对 SPADES 真实事件划分进行全帧预测，比较两套系统；结合归档配置与一个定性失败案例解释误差分布差异（未做消融隔离因果）。

Result: 出现"指标级反转"：直接回归将姿态（方向）误差降低 22.7%（1.1767→0.9101 rad），综合分低 13.4%；模块化流程将相对平移误差降低 78.7%（0.1335→0.0285）。

Conclusion: 两类误差轮廓互补，聚合挑战分数掩盖了分项取舍；据此提出可检验的混合设计——直接回归姿态方向 + 几何约束平移恢复。

Abstract: Event cameras offer high temporal resolution and dynamic range for uncooperative spacecraft pose estimation, but models trained on synthetic event streams must generalize to the noise, background activity, and contrast variation of physical sensors. We present a comparative systems study of two independently developed approaches to synthetic-to-real, event-based 6-DoF spacecraft pose estimation. The first is a modular pipeline that combines learned keypoint detection and unlabeled target-domain adaptation with geometry-based pose recovery and temporal smoothing. The second directly regresses translation and rotation from global and cropped event representations using an apparent-scale cue, physics-motivated synthetic corruptions, and feature-consistency regularization. We evaluate both systems under the same SPARK 2026 hidden-test protocol on the real-event split of SPADES, with predictions for all test frames. The results reveal a component-wise reversal concealed by the aggregate challenge score: direct regression reduces orientation error by 22.7% (1.1767 to 0.9101 rad) and obtains a 13.4% lower composite score, whereas the modular pipeline reduces relative translation error by 78.7% (0.1335 to 0.0285). Archived configurations and a qualitative failure case identify plausible explanations for these complementary error profiles, without isolating their causes. These findings motivate a testable hybrid design that combines direct orientation estimation with geometry-constrained translation recovery.

</details>


### [82] [Evaluating Anterior Cruciate Ligament Risk of Injury Using a Graph Convolutional Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09724)
*Athanasios Babouras,Paul A. Martineau,Thomas Fevens*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 基于多层图卷积自编码器的无监督模型，在智能手机端用姿态估计数据判断DVJ与SLH两项测试中的前交叉韧带损伤高风��状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ACL损伤风险评估依赖昂贵动作捕捉实验室或专家主观评分，缺乏低成本、可普及的实时筛查手段。

Method: 以姿态估计关键点序列为输入，构建多层图卷积网络的自编码器进行无监督异常/风险表征学习，模型完全在手机端推理，分别针对Drop Vertical Jump与Single Leg Hop两项运动应力测试输出风险判定。

Result: DVJ测试平均敏感度78%、特异度78%；SLH测试敏感度75%、特异度74%。

Conclusion: 结果支持开发普及化的手机端ACL风险实时筛查方案，但样本规模与真值标注方式未在摘要中说明，临床落地仍需更大规模验证。

Abstract: Current solutions for assessing the risk of anterior cruciate ligament (ACL) injury typically rely on expensive motion-capture labs that require substantial resource investment or subjective evaluations of specific athletic stress tests by experts. The Drop Vertical Jump (DVJ) and the Single Leg Hop (SLH) are two examples of such tests typically used to track progress in ACL rehabilitation. In this paper, we develop an unsupervised learning model that runs entirely on a smartphone for two tasks, based on an autoencoder architecture with multiple layers of graph convolutional networks, which utilizes pose estimation data to determine a person's injury risk status. Our model accurately predicts athletes at high risk of injury for both athletic stress tests, with average sensitivities and specificities of 78% and 78% for the DVJ, and 75% and 74% for the SLH. Our findings support the development of a widely accessible smartphone solution for real-time screening of ACL injury risk.

</details>


### [83] [TriNetra: Thermal-Reliability-Guided RGB-Thermal Fusion and Tracking for Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09725)
*Sadique Khatib,Aditya Ahirrao,Vedant Sonawane,Disha Kapse,Y. D. Bhise*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TriNetra提出可靠性感知RGB-T监控框架：用5个无标注图像线索计算帧级热红外可靠性系数TRC，门控冻结骨干的双分支YOLOv8m热特征，并将同一分数传入ByteTrack与规则行为分析器；M3FD上mAP@50 70.0%，热流损坏时自适应门控保持43.6%（固定门控38.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见光相机夜间失效、热红外在饱和/模糊/低对比下失效，而主流RGB-T检测器以固定权重融合两路特征，无法感知某一模态何时不可靠，缺乏模态可靠性度量。

Method: 从五个免标签图像线索计算帧级Thermal Reliability Coefficient（TRC）；双分支YOLOv8m检测器（可见光分支、neck、head冻结且COCO预训练，仅热分支17.0M/42.9M参数可训练）；TRC用于门控热特征，并共享给ByteTrack关联与规则式行为分析器。

Result: M3FD（2940/630/630）测试集：P 87.6%、R 79.4%、mAP@50 70.0%、mAP@50:95 47.8%。TRC在干净帧约0.78（σ=0.012），随热饱和/模糊加剧降至0.53/0.69，对RGB-T错位不敏感。热流损坏时自适应门控43.6% mAP@50，优于固定门控38.4%、全热接纳29.2%、闭门25.0%，增益5.1点（95% bootstrap CI +3.6~+6.5）。跟踪上与ByteTrack持平，热饱和下略不稳定，故收益仅体现在检测端。

Conclusion: 提出无需标注的模态可靠性度量并实现检测-跟踪一体化门控，在检测端取得稳健增益；但跟踪增益未证实，评估局限已如实报告。属于监控域RGB-T融合的工程性改进，非物理AI/具身智能或3D重建核心议题。

Abstract: Visible cameras fail at night and thermal sensors fail under saturation, blur or loss of contrast, yet most RGB-thermal (RGB-T) detectors mix the two streams with fixed weights and cannot tell when one modality has become unreliable. TriNetra is a reliability-aware surveillance framework built around this problem. The proposed solution computes a frame-level Thermal Reliability Coefficient (TRC) from five label-free image cues, uses it to gate the thermal features of a dual-branch YOLOv8m detector whose visible branch, neck and head are frozen and COCO-pretrained, and passes the same score to a ByteTrack-based tracker that feeds a rule-based behaviour analyser. On the M3FD benchmark (2,940/630/630 split) the detector reaches 87.6% precision, 79.4% recall, 70.0% mAP@50 and 47.8% mAP@50:95 on the test split, with 17.0 M of its 42.9 M parameters trained. TRC stays near 0.78 on clean frames (standard deviation 0.012), decreases with the severity of thermal saturation and blur, reaching 0.53 and 0.69 at full severity, and is insensitive to RGB-thermal misalignment. With identical weights, the adaptive gate matches a fixed gate on clean data (70.0% versus 69.8% mAP@50) but keeps 43.6% mAP@50 when the thermal stream is corrupted, against 38.4%, 29.2% and 25.0% for a fixed gate, full thermal admission and a closed gate; the 5.1-point gain over the fixed gate has a 95% bootstrap confidence interval of +3.6 to +6.5 points. In tracking, the TRC-gated association performs on par with ByteTrack on a synthetic benchmark with exact ground truth and on 14 M3FD clips, and is slightly less stable under thermal saturation, so its measured benefit is confined to the detector. The limitations of the current evaluation are reported together with the results.

</details>


### [84] [Zero-Shot Benchmarking of Unified Multimodal Models for Shallow-Water UAV Image Distortion Assessment and Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09740)
*Dario Scilla,Omar A. Lopez,Mattia Rosso,Kasper Johansen,Matthew F. McCabe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首个系统的零样本基准，评估五个冻结权重的统一多模态大模型在浅水无人机图像的水致畸变识别与复原两类任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浅水UAV图像可提供厘米级底栖观测，但水致畸变（散射、吸收、折射）使解释困难；现有方法多为分离的复原或评估模块，缺乏在同一模型框架下检验"识别畸变"与"物理复原"能力的统一评测。

Method: 构建1000对图像基准：600对半合成（对真实浅水UAV图施加合成水致畸变）+400对全合成透水场景；三类独立人工标注覆盖五种畸变类型。评测5个开源统一MLLM（冻结权重、无配对参考、无真实海底描述）；设计4种评估提示（P0-P3，从直接指令到结构化任务分解，1000图全量）与4种诊断引导复原提示（R0-R3，100图子集）；并对比InstructIR、PromptFix两个纯复原基线。

Result: 评估性能随模型与提示显著波动，存在系统性预测偏置，畸变存在性判断的最佳汇总平衡准确率仅0.654；最佳汇总PSNR增益+0.255 dB，无统一MLLM改善汇总LPIPS，部分输出改变场景结构或产生幻觉内容；InstructIR汇总PSNR增益仅+0.040 dB，PromptFix在所有提示下四项汇总指标均下降。

Conclusion: 识别浅水退化并不等于具备可靠的物理复原能力；当前统一MLLM在该任务上明显不足，本文提供了可复现的零样本基线供后续研究参照。

Abstract: Shallow-water UAV imagery provides centimeter-scale observations of benthic environments, but water-induced distortions complicate interpretation. To our knowledge, we present the first systematic zero-shot benchmark of unified multimodal large language models (MLLMs) for both assessing water-induced distortions and restoring shallow-water imagery within the same model framework. We evaluate five open-weight unified MLLMs with frozen pretrained weights and without access to paired reference images or descriptions of the true seabed. The benchmark contains 1,000 image pairs: 600 semi-synthetic UAV scenes created by applying synthetic water distortions to real shallow-water imagery and 400 fully synthetic through-water scenes. Three independent human annotations cover five distortion types. We evaluate four assessment prompts (P0-P3), from direct instruction to structured task decomposition, on all 1,000 images and four diagnosis-guided restoration prompts (R0-R3) on a common 100-image subset. Assessment varies substantially by model and prompt, with systematic prediction biases and a best pooled balanced accuracy of 0.654 for distortion presence. The best pooled PSNR gain is +0.255 dB, no unified MLLM improves pooled LPIPS, and some outputs alter scene structure or hallucinate content. Two restoration-only baselines also show limited transfer: InstructIR achieves a pooled PSNR gain of +0.040 dB, while PromptFix degrades all four pooled metrics under every prompt. These results show that recognizing shallow-water degradation does not imply reliable physical restoration and provide a reproducible baseline for future work.

</details>


### [85] [A lightweight convolutional neural network for target detection in an edge environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09756)
*Hughes Perreault,Ola Ahmad,Daniel Czuboka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向边缘部署的轻量级CNN，结合实时热点检测算法，实现约20fps推理与400米以上热成像探测距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备算力受限，需在热成像态势感知场景下快速、准确地检测与分类潜在目标（威胁/目标），并扩展安全边界。

Method: 设计轻量级CNN分类检测网络，配套实时热点检测算法以加速信息筛选与处理，强调低参数量与易部署性。

Result: 推理速度最高达20 fps，配合典型热传感器探测距离超过400米；实验显示高准确率、高灵敏度与低误报率。

Conclusion: 该轻量CNN可作为边缘设备可靠的目标检测方案，适用于监视、安防与目标识别等应用。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel, lightweight convolutional neural network (CNN) designed for edge device deployment. The CNN is developed to recognize and classify threats or targets of interest. The model is equipped with a real-time hotspot detection algorithm, which enables it to process information quickly and accurately, resulting in improved inference time of up to 20 fps and a detection range of more than 400 meters with typical thermal situational awareness sensors. The proposed CNN has been rigorously tested and validated to demonstrate its high accuracy, sensitivity, and low false positive rate, providing a reliable solution for edge devices to detect potential targets. Additionally, the lightweight design of the CNN allows for easy deployment, making it an ideal solution for extending the security bubble for edge devices. The proposed model can be used for a variety of applications, including surveillance, security, and object recognition.

</details>


### [86] [AR-Edit: Training-Free Autoregressive Video Editing without Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09795)
*Hovhannes Margaryan,Quentin Bammey,Christian Sandor,Vicky Kalogeiton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AR-Edit：基于DMD蒸馏自回归视频扩散模型自注意力非对称性，实现无需训练、无需反演的实时流式视频编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式应用中需在无未来帧的流式条件下实时编辑视频；现有无训练方法需全序列离线访问，流式方法则依赖额外训练或局限于特定编辑技术。

Method: 分析DMD蒸馏AR视频扩散模型的自注意力动态，发现query/key在各去噪步稳定且与源对齐、value则发散并编码演化内容（继承自非蒸馏双向教师，高噪声处最强）。据此仅单次前向即可在value特征中捕获源身份，从纯高斯噪声出发注入源value即可高保真重建输入，省去反演；AR-Edit缓存一次源value并在去噪中按需选择性注入。

Result: 源重建保真度匹配或超过基于反演的方法；在多个AR骨干上实现实时流式编辑，源保持与色调保真更优，额外开销极小。

Conclusion: 注意力非对称性为流式视频编辑提供了免训练、免反演的简洁机制，可推广至多种AR视频扩散骨干。

Abstract: Editing a video stream in real time, without access to future frames, is essential for interactive applications. Existing training-free video editing methods, however, assume full offline access to the entire sequence, while streaming approaches either rely on additional training or are limited to specific editing techniques. We show that this limitation can be removed by analyzing the self-attention dynamics of DMD-distilled autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models. We uncover a key asymmetry: queries and keys remain stable and aligned with their source counterparts across denoising timesteps, while values diverge and encode the evolving content. Inherited from the non-distilled bidirectional teacher, this asymmetry is strongest at high noise, where few-step streaming models operate. The resulting mechanism is simple: a single forward pass suffices to capture source identity in the value features, which can be reused throughout generation. Starting from pure Gaussian noise and injecting only these source values reconstructs the input with fidelity matching or surpassing inversion-based methods, effectively eliminating inversion. Based on this insight, we introduce AR-Edit, a training-free, inversion-free method that caches source values once and selectively injects them during denoising. Across multiple AR backbones, AR-Edit enables real-time streaming editing with superior source preservation and tonal fidelity at minimal overhead.

</details>


### [87] [BEV3SR: Evidence-Aware 3D Spatial Reasoning from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09816)
*Fan Zhu,Rui Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BEV3SR：无需训练、backbone 无关的 RGB-D 视频 3D 空间推理框架，将视频观测转为可查询的度量 BEV 场景记忆，并按查询的证据需求分流到确定性几何推理器或视觉证据对齐器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频只提供序列化、视角局部的观测，缺乏持久度量场景记录；许多问题需在查询指定视角而非相机视角下回答，固定 RGB 视图提示可能漏掉稀疏或被遮挡线索。

Method: 训练无关框架：Spatial Task Formalizer 解析查询的参考系、空间约束、目标与证据需求（几何/视觉），决定推理路径；几何类查询由 Geometric Reasoner 在查询参考系下用度量 BEV 记录确定性计算；视觉类查询由 Visual Evidence Aligner 借助 BEV 索引的物体记录及支撑视图检索物体一致与场景上下文 RGB 帧供 VLM grounding；总帧预算 16 帧。

Result: 在多个 3D 场景 QA 基准上一致提升不同 VLM backbone，空间密集问题上增益显著，开放式场景 QA 亦有明显提升。

Conclusion: BEV3SR 为视频显式 3D 空间推理提供了有效、高效且可解释的框架。

Abstract: 3D spatial reasoning from video remains challenging for VLMs because videos provide sequential, view-local observations rather than persistent metric scene records. Many questions must be answered in a query-specified perspective rather than the observed camera viewpoint, and prompting with a fixed set of RGB views may miss sparse or occluded visual cues. To address these gaps, we introduce BEV3SR, a training-free and backbone-agnostic framework for RGB-D videos with camera parameters. BEV3SR turns video observations into queryable metric bird's-eye-view (BEV) scene memory and formalizes each query into an evidence-aware spatial task. The Spatial Task Formalizer specifies the query's reference frame, spatial constraints, objective, and evidence requirement. The evidence requirement determines the appropriate reasoning pathway. A query treated as requiring geometric evidence is handled by the Geometric Reasoner, which deterministically computes the answer using the metric BEV records in the query-specified reference frame. Otherwise, a query treated as requiring visual evidence is passed to the Visual Evidence Aligner, which uses BEV-indexed object records and their associated supporting views to retrieve object-consistent and scene-context RGB frames for VLM grounding. Within a standard 16-frame budget, BEV3SR consistently improves different VLM backbones across multiple 3D scene QA benchmarks, delivering large gains on spatially intensive questions and significant improvements on open-ended scene QA. These results demonstrate that BEV3SR offers an effective, efficient, and interpretable framework for explicit 3D spatial reasoning from video.

</details>


### [88] [Health-System-Wide Mapping of Demographic Information in Routine Clinical Histopathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09851)
*Shengjia Chen,Ruchika Verma,Kevin Clare,Paula Restrepo,Jannes Jegminat,Eugenia Alleva,Kuan-lin Huang,Aryeh Stock,Brandon Veremis,Thomas Fuchs,Gabriele Campanella*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: A large-scale study using pathology foundation models and multiple-instance learning over 1.3M H&E whole-slide images (257,322 patients, 25 organs) maps how well sex, age group, and self-reported race are recoverable from routine histopathology, with pan-organ AUROCs of 0.828/0.733/0.662.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: It is unclear how broadly demographic-associated information is detectable across organs and disease states in routine clinical histopathology, and whether such signals may induce shortcut learning in computational pathology models.

Method: Pathology foundation-model representations plus multiple-instance learning on 1.3M H&E whole-slide images from 257,322 patients across 25 organs; 69 organ-demographic combinations; demographic-class balancing and diagnosis-text-cluster evaluation as robustness checks; literature-guided interpretation of top settings.

Result: Demographic attributes are broadly recoverable (sex AUROC 0.828, age group 0.733, race 0.662 pan-organ), with substantial organ-wise variation; class balancing yields small changes and diagnosis-cluster evaluation shows modest attenuation; signals persist under diagnosis-based grouping (e.g., sex in gastric 0.865, age group in breast 0.816, race in skin 0.728).

Conclusion: The study provides a systematic map of demographic-associated information in routine histopathology, supporting investigation of its sources, assessment of shortcut learning, and development of more interpretable computational pathology models.

Abstract: The extent to which demographic-associated information is detectable across organs and disease states in routine clinical histopathology remains unclear. Here, we analyzed 1.3 million hematoxylin and eosin whole-slide images from 257,322 patients across 25 organs, using pathology foundation-model representations and multiple-instance learning to systematically map information associated with sex, age group, and self-reported race. Across 69 organ-demographic combinations, demographic attributes were broadly recoverable, with pan-organ mean AUROCs of 0.828 for sex, 0.733 for age group, and 0.662 for self-reported race, and substantial variation across organs. Demographic-class balancing produced relatively small changes in performance, while evaluation within diagnosis-text clusters showed modest attenuation. Under training with diagnosis-based grouping, demographic-associated signals remained detectable across multiple organs, including sex in gastric tissue (AUROC 0.865), age group in breast (0.816), and self-reported race in skin (0.728). Literature-guided analysis of these settings generated candidate histomorphological and contextual explanations for further investigation. Together, these findings provide a systematic map of demographic-associated information in routine histopathology, informing investigations of its sources, assessments of potential shortcut learning, and the development of more interpretable computational pathology models.

</details>


### [89] [GRAC: Graph-Based Retrieval-Augmented Captioning for Surgical Scene Description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09866)
*Rochak Dhakal,Chen Zhao,Soup Cobb,Xin Qian,Xiaofeng Yang,Weihua Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRAC：将手术场景表示为捕捉器械-动作-组织关系的场景图，通过检索增强（检索相似图-文本对并用器械-组织交互比较特征条件化生成）实现手术场景自动描述，在EndoVis-18与TORS上显著超越SGT及Transformer基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术报告信息提取需大量人工审阅与临床专业知识；利用手术活动的重复性与先验知识自动生成手术场景描述可减轻负担。

Method: 图基检索增强描述框架（GRAC）：输入场景图检索训练集中相关图-文本对；对检索样例与输入场景的器械-组织交互进行结构化比较，将比较特征用于条件化检索到的描述表示以驱动生成。

Result: EndoVis-18上CIDEr-D 5.88（SGT 4.23，最强Transformer基线3.36）；器械-动作-目标三元组集合精确率52.6%（SGT 32.7%，基线26.2%）。TORS上CIDEr-D 5.06（最强基线3.80）。

Conclusion: 结构化手术场景图表示结合检索样例可提升手术场景描述的生成质量与事实一致性，输出更具上下文意义的描述。

Abstract: Purpose: Surgical operative reports support procedural documentation, surgical training, performance assessment, and workflow review. However, extracting meaningful information often requires extensive manual review and clinical expertise. Automated surgical scene descriptions could reduce this burden by leveraging recurring surgical activities and prior knowledge. Methods: We propose GRAC (Graph-Based Retrieval-Augmented Captioning), a framework that represents surgical scenes as graphs capturing relationships among instruments, actions, and anatomical structures. Given an input scene graph, GRAC learns to retrieve relevant graph-caption pairs from training data. It compares instrument-tissue interactions between retrieved examples and the input scene, using comparison features to condition retrieved-caption representations during generation. Results: On the EndoVis-18 benchmark, GRAC achieved a CIDEr-D of 5.88, compared with 4.23 for SGT and 3.36 for the strongest conventional Transformer baseline. Exact instrument-action-target triplet-set accuracy reached 52.6%, compared with 32.7% for SGT and 26.2% for the strongest conventional baseline. These results demonstrate improvements in language quality and factual agreement. On TORS, GRAC achieved a CIDEr-D of 5.06, compared with 3.80 for the strongest baseline. Conclusion: GRAC demonstrates that combining structured surgical scene representations with retrieved examples improves surgical scene captioning and produces more contextually meaningful descriptions.

</details>


### [90] [Graded convolutional networks: scale invariance without the scale and robustness to unknown blur](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09878)
*Tanush Shaska*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文用“高斯 jet”上的分级代数刻画尺度不变局部特征：局部特征对膨胀不变当且仅当它经由尺度归一化 jet 或经由加权球面收缩(retraction)分解；收缩对未知高斯模糊只差一个尺度变换，而尺度归一化 jet 会残留无法被径向归一化消除的分级因子。基于收缩构造的网络对不变特征具有通用性，在 MNIST 上抗模糊精度显著优于尺度归一化导数网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尺度归一化导数（scale-normalized derivatives）是经典尺度空间理论中实现尺度不变性的标准做法，但作者怀疑它在未知高斯模糊下仍持有残余的分级因子，导致不变性不彻底；同时需要从理论上完备地刻画“全部”膨胀不变的局部特征，而不是依赖经验性归一化。

Method: 把图像高斯 jet 视为分级向量空间（λ 膨胀使 k 阶导数乘 λ^{-k}）；证明局部分级卷积恰为齐次微分算子；证明膨胀不变局部特征 ⇔ 可经由尺度归一化 jet 分解，或（去掉尺度后）经由到分级加权球面的收缩映射分解。分析两种构造在未知高斯模糊下的行为：收缩仅改变尺度，尺度归一化 jet 产生不可消除的分级因子。在零 jet 处，收缩可分离径向归一化与连续分类器无法分离的图像族；并证明基于收缩的网络对不变特征具有通用性。最后在 MNIST 与 Lindeberg 高斯导数网络上做模糊鲁棒性对比实验。

Result: MNIST 上基于收缩的网络在两像素模糊下保持 88.4% 精度，同结构尺度归一化导数网络仅 66.2%；在 Lindeberg 高斯导数网络中引入收缩使该模糊下精度从 91.5% 提升至 95.1%。理论上给出膨胀不变局部特征的完备分解定理与零 jet 处的可分离性结论。

Conclusion: 尺度不变性应通过加权球面收缩（分级归一化）而非仅尺度归一化导数来实现；收缩提供对未知模糊鲁棒且对不变特征通用的表示，可作为尺度不变前端模块替换经典尺度归一化导数层。

Abstract: The Gaussian jet of an image is a graded vector space: a dilation by $\lambda$ multiplies derivatives of order $k$ by $\lambda^{-k}$. Local graded convolutions are exactly homogeneous differential operators, and a local feature is dilation-invariant if and only if it factors through the scale-normalized jet or, without the scale, through the retraction onto the weighted sphere of the grading. Under unknown Gaussian blur the retraction changes only by a change of scale, whereas the scale-normalized jet acquires a grading factor that no radial normalization removes. At the zero jet the retraction separates image families that radial normalizations and continuous classifiers cannot. Networks built on the retraction are universal for invariant features. On MNIST such a network keeps 88.4% accuracy under a two-pixel blur, against 66.2% for the same network on scale-normalized derivatives; in Lindeberg's Gaussian derivative network the retraction raises accuracy at that blur from 91.5% to 95.1%.

</details>


### [91] [ZeoBench: A Benchmark for Self-Supervised Learning on 3D Zeolite Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09880)
*Aaron Sun,Yachan Liu,Ping Yang,Peng Bai,Subhransu Maji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZeoBench：用于全硅沸石三维稠密表示的自监督学习基准，在六个吸附性质预测任务上比较体素3D自监督预训练、2D图像预训练网络的3D适配、域外3D ConvNet预训练与手工描述符，发现3D自监督表示在少标注与跨域（MOF）设定下达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沸石结构库庞大但性质标签昂贵、仅覆盖有限结构-分子对；已有3D神经表示可预测性质，但在材料发现所对应的低数据条件下，何种表示学习策略最有效尚不清楚。

Method: 构建ZeoBench基准：对所有全硅沸石的三维稠密体素表示做自监督预训练；对比（1）体素数据上的3D自监督预训练、（2）经多视图与通道膨胀适配到3D的2D ConvNet/ViT、（3）在域外体素数据上预训练的3D ConvNet，并与手工描述符及监督基线比较；在六个吸附性质预测任务、多档训练集规模下评估。

Result: 自监督3D表示优于已有方法与手工描述符，在域内各标签量档位及未见域MOF上均达SOTA；卷积架构在此设定下普遍优于视觉Transformer。

Conclusion: ZeoBench为沸石3D表示学习提供了实用基准，并为数据受限的吸附性质预测给出选型指导：优先采用3D自监督+卷积架构。

Abstract: Zeolites are an important class of porous crystalline materials with broad applications in gas storage, separations, and catalysis. Although hundreds of thousands of experimentally realized and hypothetical zeolite structures are known, identifying materials optimized for a target application remains challenging because property labels are expensive to obtain and available only for limited structure-molecule pairs. While prior work has demonstrated that 3D neural representations can predict zeolite properties effectively, it remains unclear which representation-learning strategies are most effective in the low-data regime relevant to materials discovery. We present ZeoBench, a benchmark for self-supervised learning on dense 3D representations of all-silica zeolites, and evaluate the resulting features on six existing downstream adsorption-property prediction tasks, across a range of training set sizes. Our study compares self-supervised 3D pretraining on volumetric zeolite data, adaptations of image-pretrained 2D ConvNets and vision transformers to 3D via multiview and channel-inflation strategies, and 3D ConvNets pretrained on out-of-domain volumetric datasets, alongside hand-crafted descriptors and supervised baselines. Using our pretraining and evaluation framework, we find self-supervised 3D representations outperform existing methods and achieve state-of-the-art performance across both in-domain label regimes and for the unseen domain of metal organic frameworks. Learned representations consistently outperform hand-crafted descriptors, and convolutional architectures are generally more effective than vision transformers in this setting. These results establish ZeoBench as a practical benchmark for 3D representation learning in zeolites and provide guidance for data-limited adsorption property prediction.

</details>


### [92] [FlowMoP: Stochastic Multi-Person Motion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09882)
*Aadya Agrawal,Ho Kei Cheng,Alexander Schwing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）框架，联合生成多人多样化的未来3D运动，同时建模全局轨迹与根相对姿态；用B样条重参数化压缩生成状态空间并保证时序平滑，用双流Transformer以邻居观测运动为条件实现交互感知预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人未来运动本质上是随机的：同一观测历史在意图、协作与社会语境下存在多种合理延续。然而当前多人3D运动预测主流仍是确定性范式，每个智能体只输出固定数量的结果，无法表达完整预测分布。

Method: 1) 条件流匹配生成式框架，多智能体联合生成多样化未来运动；2) 同时输出全局轨迹与root-relative姿态；3) B样条重参数化将生成状态空间压缩到低维控制点，并显式施加时序平滑约束；4) 双流Transformer结构，将每个智能体的预测条件化于观测到的邻居运动以刻画交互。

Result: 在CMU-Mocap (UMPM) 与3DPW上取得SOTA；在联合位置误差、姿态误差与终点位移误差上均优于确定性基线，且输出的是完整预测分布而非单点估计。另在WorldPose（大规模职业足球数据集）上建立首个运动预测基线，并显示社会条件化会把预测不确定性集中到受交互约束的方向上。

Conclusion: 生成式（流匹配）建模范式可以在不牺牲精度的情况下替代确定性多人3D运动预测，配合样条低维化与交互条件化可同时获得多样性、平滑性与SOTA精度；社会条件化具有可观测的不确定性几何效应。

Abstract: Predicting the future motion of multiple people is inherently stochastic: the same observed history admits many plausible continuations depending on intent, coordination, and social context. Yet the dominant paradigm in multi-person 3D motion prediction remains deterministic, with existing methods producing a fixed number of outputs per agent. In contrast, we present a conditional flow matching framework that jointly generates diverse future motions for multiple agents, modeling both global trajectories and root-relative poses. A B-spline reparameterization compresses the generative state space while enforcing temporal smoothness, and a dual-stream transformer conditions each agent's predictions on observed neighbor motion for interaction-aware forecasting. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on CMU-Mocap (UMPM) and 3DPW, outperforming deterministic baselines on joint position error, pose error, and final displacement error despite producing a full predictive distribution. We additionally establish the first motion prediction baseline on WorldPose, a large-scale professional soccer dataset, where social conditioning demonstrably concentrates predictive uncertainty along interaction-constrained directions.

</details>


### [93] [Video-Coder: Reinforcing Agentic Coding for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09894)
*Chaoyang Wang,Sicheng Gao,Junan Hu,Kunyang Li,Dongyang Chen,Yiyang Zhou,Huaxiu Yao,Yong Jae Lee,Radu Timofte,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Video-Coder 训练一个"写代码驱动视频生成"的编码智能体：它自主编写并执行代码构建动态 3D 场景（Draft Video），为下游视频生成器提供结构与运动先验，并通过 SFT + 环境原生强化学习（执行奖励与视频奖励双反馈）联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型在视觉质量与时序一致性上进步显著，但仍难以精确控制空间结构、运动动力学与场景构图。作者希望用"代码驱动 3D 场景构建"作为可解释、可执行的中间表示，把结构化控制权交给智能体而非仅靠文本提示。

Method: 构建专用数据流水线，整理 Video-Coder-SFT-6k 与 Video-Coder-RL-2k 两个数据集；开发 Video-Coder Harness——集成代码执行、3D 场景渲染与执行反馈的统一交互环境；训练流程为 SFT 后接 harness 原生智能体强化学习，使用基于执行与基于视频的双重奖励，并设计退火策略逐步衰减执行奖励，使后期训练更依赖视频奖励。

Result: 将 Video-Coder 接入 Minimax-H3 后，在 PhyGenBench 上提升约 7 分，在 VBench-2.0 上提升约 24 分；数据、模型与代码全部开源。

Conclusion: 代码驱动的 3D 场景 Draft 可作为通用、可执行的视频生成控制接口，配合智能体强化学习与奖励退火显著增强物理合理性与可控性，为视频生成领域的编码智能体提供了开放基座。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed remarkable progress in video generation in terms of visual quality and temporal consistency. However, generating videos with precise spatial structure, coherent motion dynamics, and controllable scene composition remains challenging. In this paper, we introduce Video-Coder, the first attempt to train a code-driven video generation agent, which autonomously writes and executes code to construct a dynamic 3D scene (Draft Video) that provides structure and motion signals to downstream video generators. To support this paradigm, we design a dedicated data pipeline and curate two high-quality datasets, Video-Coder-SFT-6k and Video-Coder-RL-2k, which contain diverse prompts and ground-truth reference videos. We further develop the Video- Coder Harness, a unified interactive environment integrating code execution, 3D scene rendering, and execution feedback for agent training and inference. We then train Video-Coder with SFT fol- lowed by harness-native agentic reinforcement learning with dual reward feedback, which combines execution-based and video-based rewards. Additionally, we design an annealing training strategy that gradually reduces the execution reward over time, allowing the model to rely more on the video reward in the later training stages. Experiments show that Video-Coder achieves significant gains, improving Minimax-H3 by approximately 7 points on PhyGenBench and 24 points on VBench-2.0. We hope this work can serve as an open foundation for coding agents in video generation, and we fully open-source our data, models, and code

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [ROSE: Risk-Aware Orthogonal Subspace Navigation for Lifelong Knowledge Editing in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09770)
*Lingyun Song,Ziyao Chen,Kang Pan,Xiaolin Han,Yifei Zhang,Xiaoqi Wang,Yudai Pan,Xuequn Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ROSE：面向MLLM终身知识编辑的风险感知正交子空间导航框架，通过将参数更新限制在已编辑子空间的正交补、并用动态风险掩码保护稳定性关键子空间，配合推理期的多粒度知识整合（原型锚定语义门控+扰动驱动子空间融合），在1000次序贯编辑下保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM需持续更新事实知识，但终身编辑存在严重的局部性侵蚀与灾难性遗忘：序贯编辑相互干扰并破坏预训练能力。现有方法难以同时兼顾编辑有效性、无关输入的不干扰性以及长序列编辑下的稳定性。

Method: 1) 正交子空间约束：将新编辑的参数更新投影到此前已编辑子空间的正交补上，抑制对旧编辑的覆盖；2) 风险引导机制：动态计算风险掩码，保护对模型稳定性至关重要的特定事实参数子空间；3) 推理期多粒度知识整合：原型锚定的语义门控决定是否触发编辑知识，扰动驱动的子空间融合精确激活相关编辑，限制对无关查询的干扰。

Result: 在两个主流多模态基准上，五项关键指标均与SOTA方法具有竞争力，并在1000次以上序贯编辑中保持突出的稳定性；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 以正交子空间投影+风险掩码的组合同时缓解局部性侵蚀与灾难性遗忘，并以推理期门控融合保证编辑知识的精准激活，为MLLM终身知识编辑提供可行方案。属模型知识更新方向，与具身感知/3D重建等核心领域关联较弱。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) require continuous factual updates to stay current, yet lifelong knowledge editing remains a daunting challenge. The primary obstacle is the severe Locality Erosion and Catastrophic Forgetting, where sequential edits inevitably interfere with pre-trained capabilities and prior updates. In this paper, we propose \textbf{ROSE}, a \textbf{R}isk-aware \textbf{O}rthogonal \textbf{S}ubspace navigation framework for lifelong knowledge \textbf{E}diting. ROSE addresses forgetting by aiming to restrict parameter updates to the orthogonal complement of previously edited subspaces. Crucially, it introduces a risk-guided mechanism that utilizes a dynamically computed risk mask to safeguard specific factual parameter subspaces essential for model stability. During inference, ROSE employs a multi-granularity knowledge integration strategy, featuring prototype-anchored semantic gating and perturbation-driven subspace fusion to precisely activate edited knowledge for relevant queries while limiting interference on unrelated inputs. Comprehensive experiments on two prominent multimodal benchmarks show that ROSE is competitive with state-of-the-art approaches across five key metrics, while maintaining outstanding stability over $1,000$ sequential updates. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [UniMore: Fine-Grained Supervision for Unified Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08996)
*Haoyue Bai,Yinyi Luo,Yicheng He,Qingsong Wen,Sharon Li,Jindong Wang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: UniMore：从图文对的细粒度内容中派生互补任务做后训练——生成路径由短语预测对应裁剪区域，理解路径做正样本拟合+细粒度扰动负样本排序，分别优化后合并为单一统一模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型存在"特异性丢失"：生成图像含正确物体但属性错误，描述只识别场景而缺失区分性细节。需要同时监督"要实现什么视觉内容"与"区分性描述差异"。

Method: 后训练框架 UniMore。生成路由：给定短语预测对应的grounded crop，使条件与视觉目标范围匹配，并沿用原生视觉目标。理解路由：以完整图像为条件，正caption拟合 + 与省略/篡改细节的caption做排序对比。两路独立优化，学习到的权重更新合并进单一统一模型，推理流程不变。

Result: 在多个理解与生成基准上均取得提升（摘要未给出具体数值与基线）。

Conclusion: 细粒度、局部对齐的互补监督可缓解统一模型的细节丢失；参数/更新合并式融合无需改动推理即可同时改善双向能力。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models combine image understanding and generation within one system, creating an opportunity to improve both capabilities through paired image--text supervision. We examine the outputs of a pretrained unified model in both directions and observe a loss of specificity: generated images can contain the requested objects with incorrect attributes, while captions identify a scene without expressing its distinguishing details. These failures call for supervision that specifies both the visual content to realize and the descriptive differences to distinguish. We propose UniMore, a post-training method that derives these complementary learning tasks from the fine-grained content of image--text pairs. The generation route predicts grounded crops from their corresponding phrases, matching the scope of the condition and visual target while using the native visual objective. The understanding route combines positive-caption fitting with ranking against captions that omit or alter details, conditioned on the full image. The two routes are optimized independently, and their learned updates are combined into a single unified model without changing its inference procedure. Experiments show gains across multiple understanding and generation benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Flex-RSI: Benchmarking Recursive Self-Improvement of Frontier Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08946)
*Jingdong Zhang,Jionghao Wang,Zhengming Yu,Yancheng Cai,Wei Chow,Chris Choy,Zhiyang Dou,Hang Hua,Jiachen Li,Zhixuan Liu,Chen Wang,Songlin Wei,Siyuan Yang,Yu Yuan,Xiaohang Zhan,Yucheng Zhang,Shuhong Zheng,Xin Li,Lingjie Liu,Wojciech Matusik,Zhengzhong Tu,Wenping Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Flex-RSI 基准，在固定预算下评测智能体对自身 harness（提示、工具、记忆、控制循环及 improver 本身）的递归自我改进能力，任务覆盖感知、推理、机器人动作、3D 重建、视觉创作与基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准固定 agent harness 只评估产出解，或聚焦大规模模型训练；缺少"agent 能否利用任务反馈改进自身工作方式（而非仅答案）"的可比评测。

Method: 每轮中 executor 改进任务解，仅在留出验证集分数提升时保留；improver 随后修改 harness（含 executor 与自身）。所有运行从同一通用 harness 起步，最终解在隐藏测试集上打分；评测闭源与开源模型，并做配对运行消融。

Result: 自我改进在 agent 自行构建解时收益最大；仅记录成功/失败的二值反馈会拖累开源模型；开源模型在需通过物理稳定性或精确正确性严格检验的输出上差距最大；配对运行表明编辑 harness 本身对增益有实质贡献。

Conclusion: harness 层面的自我修改是可测量的性能来源，反馈粒度与校验严格性是限制开源模型自我改进的关键因素。

Abstract: Frontier language models are increasingly deployed as agents, and how well an agent performs depends not only on its model but also on its harness: the prompts, tools, memory, and control loop through which the model acts. When an agent is applied to a new problem, a natural question is whether it can use the feedback of the task to improve the way it works, and not merely its answer. Most existing benchmarks keep the agent's harness fixed and score the solution it produces, or target large-scale model training. We introduce Flex-RSI, a benchmark for the recursive self-improvement of agents on vision and robotics tasks under a fixed budget. In each round, an executor improves the task solution, which is kept only if it scores higher on held-out validation data, and an improver then revises the harness, including the executor and itself. Every run starts from the same general-purpose harness, and the final solution is scored on hidden test data. Evaluating closed-source and open-weight models on tasks spanning perception, reasoning, robot action, 3D reconstruction, visual creation, and infrastructure, we find that self-improvement helps most when the agent builds its own solution, that feedback recording only success or failure holds back open-weight models, and that open-weight models fall furthest behind where outputs must pass strict checks of physical stability or exact correctness. Paired runs further show that editing the harness itself contributes to the gains.

</details>


### [97] [Flow-Guided Target-Space Alignment via Path Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09863)
*Ruizhi Yuan,Zeqiu Yu,Wei Gao,Wei Chen,Xiwei Tang,Lu Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowAlign将条件流匹配用作训练期对齐信号，把源模态表征对齐到固定目标空间，同时兼顾配对对应性与目标分布全局结构，并在合成数据、单细胞多组学和图文对齐上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标空间直接用于下游分析的多模态对齐场景中，对比损失只保留相对相似度、分布匹配损失只对齐边缘分布、点对点回归忽略目标空间路径几何，现有目标函数各自只覆盖部分需求，缺乏同时保持全局结构与实例级配对一致性的方法。

Method: 提出FlowAlign：不将条件流匹配（CFM）作为推理期传输模型，而是重用途为训练期对齐信号，用学到的流场引导源侧表征向固定目标空间对齐，在保留配对对应性的同时维持与目标空间全局结构的兼容；并从端点精度与局部目标空间几何两方面给出理论分析。

Result: 在合成数据、配对单细胞多组学基准和图文对齐实验上，FlowAlign在不同模态设定下均提升了实例级对应性，同时保持目标空间结构。

Conclusion: 将生成式流匹配的几何先验作为对齐正则是一种有效且通用的多模态目标空间对齐范式，可推广至生物与视觉-语言等异构模态。

Abstract: Many multimodal alignment problems require mapping a source modality into a fixed space defined by a target modality, where the target space is not merely an intermediate embedding but is used directly for downstream analysis. In this setting, successful alignment should preserve both the global structure of the target distribution and the instance-level correspondence observed in paired source--target training data. Existing objectives typically address only part of this goal: contrastive losses encourage relative similarity, distribution-matching losses improve marginal agreement, and pointwise regression fits each source sample to its paired target without explicitly modeling target-space path geometry. We propose FlowAlign, a flow-guided target-space alignment method that repurposes conditional flow matching as a training-time alignment signal rather than an inference-time transport model. FlowAlign uses the learned flow field to guide the alignment of source-side representations with the fixed target space, encouraging them to preserve paired correspondence while remaining compatible with the global target-space structure. We further provide theoretical analysis showing how FlowAlign helps improve paired correspondence by controlling endpoint accuracy and capturing local target-space geometry. Experiments on synthetic data, paired single-cell multiome benchmarks, and image--text alignment demonstrate that FlowAlign improves instance level correspondence while preserving target-space structure across different modality settings.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [Measurement-Aware Multimodal LLM for No-Reference Low-Dose CT Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08754)
*Kerem Basim,Tolga Demiroglu,Mehmet Ozan Unal,Metin Ertas,Isa Yildirim*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出基于Qwen3.8-27B的"测量感知"MLLM用于低剂量CT无参考IQA：将6个图像域质量测量（噪声、锐度、高频、条纹）以文本百分位形式与图像一起输入LLM，输出可解释评分；在LDCT-IQA 2023上Overall 2.735，与重训冠军持平，显著优于零样本基础MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低剂量CT的无参考IQA需要模拟放射科医生的主观评分，用于比较剂量协议与去噪器；传统IQA网络分数与医生一致但不能解释，而MLLM可直接解释但仅靠提示在CT上表现很差。

Method: 构建测量感知MLLM（Qwen3.8-27B基座）：输入切片图像 + 6个图像域测量（噪声、锐度、高频内容、条纹伪影），测量以文本百分位形式注入语言模型，融合视觉与显式量化线索进行评分；并用打乱测量的消融实验检验模型对测量的依赖。

Result: 测试集Overall 2.735，持平重训挑战赛冠军（2.735），高于MedSigLIP-FiLM（2.713），大幅优于零样本基座MLLM；测试时打乱测量使分数下降0.048（主要通过噪声项），但同一组测量也导致模型对过度平滑的去噪图像打更高分。

Conclusion: 显式测量注入能有效弥补MLLM在医学影像IQA上的零样本短板，达到专家级评分精度并保留解释性；但模型存在被测量"误导"的偏差（偏好过度平滑图像），提示测量设计需与感知偏好对齐。

Abstract: No-reference image quality assessment (IQA) of low-dose CT predicts the score that radiologists would assign to a slice without a full-dose reference, as needed to compare dose protocols and denoisers. Trained CT IQA networks agree well with radiologists but output only a score, whereas multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can explain a score but perform poorly on CT when only prompted. We propose a measurement-aware MLLM, built on Qwen3.8-27B, that rates a slice from its image together with six image-domain measurements of noise, sharpness, high-frequency content and streaks, given to the language model as percentiles in text. On the LDCT-IQA 2023 test set, it reaches an Overall score of 2.735, on par with the retrained challenge winner (2.735) and MedSigLIP-FiLM (2.713), and substantially above its base MLLM used as a zero-shot agent. Shuffling the measurements at test time reduces the score by 0.048, mostly through noise, but the same measurements also lead the model to assign higher scores to over-smoothed denoised images.

</details>


### [99] [Semantic Extraction Framework for Goal-Oriented V2X Communication Supporting Safe Intersection Passage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09118)
*Haoran Yang,Shan Lu,Takaya Yamazato*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出面向交叉路口安全通行的车路协同（V2X）发送端语义提取框架，路侧单元从视频与信号灯序列中提取目标检测、跟踪、ROI语义与推荐动作，打包为紧凑语义包以低时延辅助驾驶决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始感知数据传输带宽开销大、时延高，难以满足面向任务的V2X低时延驾驶决策辅助需求，因此需要发送端语义提取。

Method: 将路口通行建模为日本交通法规下的任务集合；流水线包含目标检测、目标跟踪、基于ROI的语义提取与推荐动作生成，输出感知与意图语义包。在自建CARLA单车数据集（晴天）上实现并评估。

Result: 在测试任务集上推荐动作准确率较高；平均处理时延低于15fps输入视频的帧间隔。

Conclusion: 发送端语义提取可用于实时路口通行辅助，但验证仅限CARLA仿真、单车、晴天场景，缺乏真实路测与多车/恶劣天气泛化证据。

Abstract: This paper proposes a transmitter-side semantic extraction framework for goal-oriented Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) communication supporting safe intersection passage. Instead of transmitting raw sensory data, roadside infrastructure extracts task-relevant semantic information from video and traffic-signal sequences and organizes them into compact semantic packets for low-latency driving-decision assistance. The intersection-passing problem is formulated as a set of traffic tasks under Japanese traffic regulations, and the proposed pipeline generates perception and intent semantics through object detection, object tracking, region-of-interest (ROI)-based semantic extraction, and recommended-action generation. The framework is implemented and evaluated on a self-constructed, CARLA-based single-vehicle dataset collected under clear weather conditions. Experimental results show high recommended-action accuracy within the tested task set, while the mean processing latency remains below the frame interval of the 15~fps input video.

</details>


### [100] [Beyond Segmentation: Enriching ImageCAS with Coronary Ostia Landmarks, Rooted Centerline Graphs, and Open-Weight Localization Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09714)
*Matteo Leccardi,Andrea Moglia,Marco Morandini,Michele Brembilla,Alessandro Nicosia,Andrea Penserini,Christian Brandini,Ender Konukoglu,Marco Marcon,Luca Mainardi,Pietro Cerveri*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: Enriches the ImageCAS coronary segmentation dataset (1000 patients) with coronary ostium annotations and ostium-rooted centerline graphs, and benchmarks six 3D localization models plus a human-in-the-loop annotation pipeline.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing public ostium annotations are scarce and low-quality: the only explicit ostium set covers 77 non-gated aortic scans (not CCTA), while CAT08 (32) and ASOCA (40) only imply ostia as centerline start points. A large, CCTA-based, explicitly annotated ostium and centerline resource is missing for surgical planning and automated vessel tracking.

Method: Manual triple-annotator ostium marking on the first 500 ImageCAS volumes; model-assisted human-in-the-loop correction on the remaining 500. Centerlines extracted via a deterministic, quality-controlled minimal-path procedure. Six 3D localization models (including SwinUNETR-V2, nnResUNet) trained on ImageCAS, CAT08 and ASOCA, evaluated on 58 held-out volumes with failure defined as >5 mm error; cross-dataset ablation (CAT08+ASOCA vs. adding 250 ImageCAS volumes) and centerline fidelity against ASOCA references.

Result: SwinUNETR-V2 and nnResUNet localized all ostia without failure, median error 0.70-0.87 mm. Trained only on CAT08+ASOCA, SwinUNETR-V2 fails on 34% of right and 53% of left ostia; adding 250 ImageCAS volumes eliminates all failures. Extracted centerlines cover a median 99.5% of ASOCA reference length with median per-patient mean point distance of 0.22 mm.

Conclusion: The work delivers the largest public ostium-localization dataset and the only centerline set consistent with official ImageCAS masks and rooted at annotated ostia, with annotations, centerlines, code and weights released. It is a medical-imaging benchmark/resource contribution without a generic method insight transferable to Physical AI topics.

Abstract: Coronary ostia and centerlines are key reference points for automated analysis of coronary computed tomography angiography (CCTA), from surgical planning to intraoperative vessel tracking. However, the only public ostium annotations cover 77 non-gated aortic scans, not CCTA, while CAT08 and ASOCA (32 and 40 CCTA scans) mark the ostia only implicitly, as centerline start points. This work enriches ImageCAS, the largest public dataset for coronary artery segmentation, with ostium annotations for its 1000 patients and centerline graphs rooted at these new ostia, making it, to the authors' knowledge, the largest public dataset for ostium localization and the only one with centerlines following the official ImageCAS masks and starting at annotated ostia. Three annotators marked each ostium in the first 500 volumes; in the next 500, the candidates of a model trained on the first batch were corrected (human-in-the-loop). Six 3D localization models, trained on ImageCAS, CAT08 and ASOCA, were benchmarked on 58 held-out volumes (50 ImageCAS, 4 CAT08, 4 ASOCA). SwinUNETR-V2 and nnResUNet localized all ostia without failure (>5 mm error), with median errors of 0.70-0.87 mm. Trained on CAT08 and ASOCA alone, SwinUNETR-V2 fails on 34% of right and 53% of left ostia, whereas adding 250 ImageCAS volumes removes all failures. The centerlines were extracted by a deterministic, quality-controlled minimal-path procedure; on ASOCA, they cover a median of 99.5% of the reference length, with a median per-patient mean distance of 0.22 mm between matched reference points and centerlines. Annotations, centerlines, code, and weights are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [101] [AdaptivePnP: Fast Auto-Tuned Plug-and-Play for Deconvolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09845)
*Haley Duba-Sullivan,Emma J. Reid,Charles A. Bouman,Gregery T. Buzzard*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出 AdaptivePnP：基于数据拟合残差的零均值高斯性质，用可辨识最大似然（IML）估计器在每次迭代中联合估计 PnP 的去噪步长 γ 与噪声水平 σ，无需网格搜索调参或已知噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PnP 方法性能高度依赖步长 γ 与噪声水平 σ：γ 需在验证集上网格搜索且任务设定变化后须重调，σ 常被假定已知或单独估计，缺乏统一的在线自适应机制。

Method: 将 PnP 虚拟模型作用于数据拟合近端映射，证明残差服从零均值高斯分布且协方差由 γ 与 σ 参数化。针对两参数尺度悬殊时残差对较小参数信息量不足、ML 估计会破坏迭代的问题，提出 IML 估计器：将 ML 限制在可辨识窗口内，使每个参数的归一化 Cramér–Rao 下界不超过用户给定容差；该窗口对应比值 ρ=γ/σ 的区间，故 IML 估计退化为对 ρ 的一维搜索。将 AdaptivePnP 接入两种标准 PnP 算法。

Result: 在图像去模糊与超分辨任务上，AdaptivePnP 达到或超过使用真实 σ 调参的 PnP 基线，同时无需调优 γ 与已知 σ。

Conclusion: 残差统计特性可为 PnP 超参数提供可辨识的在线估计路径，IML 通过可辨识性约束替代启发式正则，实现免调参、免噪声先验的即插即用重建；方法通用于不同 PnP 主干，但不涉及三维或机器人场景。

Abstract: Plug-and-play (PnP) methods reconstruct images by alternating a data-fitting step with an image denoiser. Their performance depends on two parameters: the step size $\gamma$ and the measurement noise level $\sigma$. In practice, $\gamma$ is often tuned by grid search over validation images and must be retuned whenever the problem setting changes, and $\sigma$ is assumed known or estimated separately. We propose AdaptivePnP, a PnP framework that jointly estimates $\sigma$ and $\gamma$ at every iteration from the data-fitting residual. To do so, we apply the PnP virtual model to the data-fitting proximal map and show that the residual is zero-mean Gaussian with covariance parameterized by $\gamma$ and $\sigma$. However, when one parameter is much larger than the other, the residual carries little information about the smaller one and using the maximum likelihood (ML) estimator can degrade the PnP iterations. We therefore introduce the identifiable maximum likelihood (IML) estimator, which restricts the ML estimator to an identifiable window in which the normalized Cramér--Rao bound of every parameter is bounded by a user-specified tolerance. For the virtual model, this window is an interval of ratios $\rho = \gamma/\sigma$, so computing the IML estimate reduces to a one-dimensional search over $\rho$. We apply AdaptivePnP to two standard PnP methods, yielding algorithms that require neither a tuned $\gamma$ nor a known $\sigma$. On image deblurring and super-resolution, AdaptivePnP matches or outperforms PnP baselines tuned with the true $\sigma$. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [102] [Super-resolved digital terrain models for GEDI geolocation correction: agreement with airborne LiDAR DTM-based corrections at the beam level, not the footprint level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09854)
*Natsuki Yuda,Shinichiro Shirota*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 将10 m DTM 与全球30 m DTM 融合后超分到1 m，用于GEDI高程匹配定标；逐轨单次平移校正与机载1 m DTM结果接近，但逐观测校正不可行，误差主要源自输入DTM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GEDI星载LiDAR存在约10 m地理定位误差，与其它遥感数据错位；基于高分辨率DTM高程匹配校正的方法受限于机载LiDAR DTM覆盖稀少，需验证低分辨率超分DTM能否替代。

Method: 在日本西部陡峭林区，将10 m DTM超分为1 m，并与不同来源的全球30 m DTM融合以提升精度；用所得DTM对GEDI观测位置先做每条地面轨迹的单次平移校正，再做逐观测校正，与机载1 m DTM的校正结果对比。

Result: 多数轨迹的单次平移校正与机载LiDAR DTM几乎一致，原因是大批观测中DTM误差相互抵消；逐观测校正则不一致，单点误差过大难以支撑，误差主要来自输入DTM。

Conclusion: 无需1 m DTM：约10 m分辨率DTM经与全球DTM融合并超分至1 m即可获得接近1 m DTM的逐轨迹几何校正效果，但无法支持单观测级别校正。

Abstract: The Global Ecosystem Dynamics Investigation (GEDI) mission measures three-dimensional forest structure with a LiDAR aboard the International Space Station. GEDI observations have geolocation errors of about 10 m, which cause misalignment with other remote sensing data and reduce the accuracy of downstream tasks. Matching GEDI ground elevations against high-resolution digital terrain models (DTMs) from airborne LiDAR is one way to correct these errors, but such DTMs exist in few regions. To widen this method's applicability, we super-resolved a more widely available low-resolution DTM and used it for the matching. Our question is whether a super-resolved DTM yields the same correction as a 1 m-resolution airborne LiDAR DTM and, if not, why. In steep forests of western Japan, we super-resolved an existing 10 m-resolution DTM to 1 m. Improving its accuracy required fusion with a global 30 m-resolution DTM from a different source. Using the resulting DTM, we corrected GEDI observation positions first by a single shift per ground track, then per observation. For most tracks, the shifts nearly matched those from the airborne LiDAR DTM, likely because DTM errors canceled out across many observations. Per-observation corrections, however, did not match. At a single observation, the error does not cancel out and is too large for the correction. This error likely originates mainly from the input DTM. Therefore, even without a 1 m-resolution DTM, a roughly 10 m-resolution DTM, fused with a global DTM and super-resolved to 1 m, should yield track-wise corrections nearly matching those from a 1 m-resolution DTM.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.QM'></div>

# q-bio.QM [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Deep-Learning Algorithm for Indeterminate Thyroid Nodules: Comparison and Combination with Molecular Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05400)
*Jichen Yang,Jeffrey Ashton,Jikai Zhang,Maciej A. Mazurowski,Benjamin Wildman-Tobriner*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 将预训练CNN迁移到超声图像上，对Bethesda III（细胞学不确定）甲状腺结节进行恶性风险分层，并与Afirma/ThyroSeq分子检测比较及联合，发现DL特异性远高于分子检测，可进一步将分子阳性结节细分为高低风险组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bethesda III结节恶性风险中等，临床需在手术与随访间抉择；分子检测虽常用但昂贵且并非普遍可及，因此探索能否用常规超声图像+深度学习低成本地替代或补充分子检测。

Method: 回顾性纳入466条（418例）接受Afirma或ThyroSeq检测的结节记录；将已在明确诊断结节上预训练的CNN通过soft-label与迁移学习适配到不确定结节；去重后119例有手术病理的结节用于开发，47例留作测试；评估AUC、敏感度/特异度/PPV/NPV，并做DL输出+结节大小+分子结果的logistic回归。

Result: 测试集DL AUC 0.73（0.51–0.94），分子检测0.55（0.38–0.71），P=0.15；沿用原阈值时DL特异度显著更高（0.95 vs 0.32，P<0.001），PPV更高（0.67 vs 0.21），敏感度更低（0.44 vs 0.78，P=0.25），NPV相近（0.88 vs 0.86）；6例DL阳性均为分子阳性，DL输出将分子阳性结节分为高风险（4/6恶性）与较低风险（3/27恶性）两组（P=0.01）。手术验证高度受分子结果驱动（阳性99% vs 阴性19%），分子检测准确性依赖平台；联合回归剔除DL项且未优于单用DL。

Conclusion: 超声DL可补充分子检测用于Bethesda III结节，尤其是识别分子阳性但实际恶性风险较低的结节；但样本量小、存在验证偏倚（手术选择受分子结果驱动），需要前瞻性多中心验证。

Abstract: Thyroid nodules with indeterminate cytology (Bethesda III) carry an intermediate risk of malignancy, and molecular testing is widely used to decide between surgery and surveillance, but it is costly and not universally available. We investigated whether a convolutional neural network (CNN) applied to routine ultrasound images can stratify Bethesda III nodules, and how it compares and combines with molecular testing. We retrospectively identified 466 nodule records from 418 patients who underwent molecular testing (Afirma or ThyroSeq). A CNN pre-trained on determinate nodules was adapted to indeterminate nodules with soft labels and transfer learning. After removal of duplicate records, 119 nodules with surgical pathology were used for development and 47 were held out for testing. On the test set, the deep learning (DL) model achieved an AUC of 0.73 (95% CI 0.51-0.94), compared with 0.55 (0.38-0.71) for molecular testing (P = 0.15). At a cut-off inherited from the original algorithm, the DL model was more specific than molecular testing (0.95 vs 0.32; P < 0.001), with a higher positive predictive value (0.67 vs 0.21), a lower sensitivity (0.44 vs 0.78; P = 0.25) and a similar negative predictive value (0.88 vs 0.86). All six DL-positive test nodules were molecular-positive, and the DL output split the molecular-positive nodules into a high-risk (4/6 malignant) and a lower-risk group (3/27 malignant; P = 0.01). Surgical verification was strongly driven by the molecular result (99% of positive vs 19% of negative results), and molecular-test accuracy depended on the platform. A logistic regression of the DL output, nodule size and the molecular result dropped the DL term and did not improve on the DL output alone. Ultrasound DL complemented molecular testing for Bethesda III nodules, in particular by identifying molecular-positive nodules at lower risk of malignancy.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [AnimWeb-Bench: Benchmarking Animated Webpage Code Generation from Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09769)
*Zhongyi Zhang,Jingyu Xiao,Shuqing Li,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AnimWeb-Bench：首个从视频生成动画网页代码的基准，含124个真实网页、7类应用域、33种动画类型，并提出静态外观保真+动画保真二维评测协议，评测14个MLLM与3个编码智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web动画极为普遍（仅CSS animation属性就出现在超四分之三网页），但现有网页代码生成基准仅覆盖静态截图、用户触发交互或孤立动效，MLLM/编码智能体重建完整动画网页的能力尚未被系统评估。

Method: 构建AnimWeb-Bench：124个真实网页（7个应用域、33种动画类型，91.9%页面混合多种动画类型），每个页面配参考视频与原始静态资源；提出人类对齐的二维评测协议：静态外观保真度（在对齐关键帧上评估结构、文本、颜色与视觉相似度）与动画保真度（跨帧跟踪UI组件，比较位置、尺寸、速度与外观）。

Result: 评测14个MLLM与3个编码智能体发现：（1）复现网页动画显著难于复现静态外观；（2）显式帧检查与渲染反馈可提升编码智能体重建质量；（3）快速连续变化比离散状态切换更难复现。

Conclusion: 动画网页代码生成是MLLM的明显短板；显式视觉反馈（帧检查、渲染回路）是有效改进方向，而连续高速动画仍是开放难题。代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Animation is pervasive on the Web, the CSS animation property alone appears on over three-quarters of webpages. Yet existing webpage code generation benchmarks target static screenshots, user-triggered interactions, or isolated motion graphics, leaving the ability of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and coding agents to reconstruct complete animated webpages largely unexplored. To fill this gap, we introduce AnimWeb-Bench, the first benchmark for animated webpage code generation from videos. It comprises 124 real-world webpages spanning 7 application domains and 33 animation types, with 91.9% of pages combining multiple types, each paired with a reference video and the static assets of the original webpage. We further propose a human-aligned, two-dimensional evaluation protocol: Static Appearance Fidelity measures structure, text, color, and visual similarity on aligned keyframes, while Animation Fidelity tracks UI components across frames and compares their position, size, velocity, and appearance with those in the reference video. Evaluating 14 MLLMs and three coding agents reveals: (1) reproducing webpage animations is substantially more difficult than reproducing static appearance; (2) explicit frame inspection and render feedback improve the reconstruction quality of coding agents; and (3) rapid, continuous changes are harder to reproduce than discrete state transitions. Our code and dataset are available at this https URL .

</details>


<div id='math.MG'></div>

# math.MG [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Multiscale Closing Index (MCI) for measuring structural similarity between 2D point sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09745)
*Nicolò Zava,Angel Franco-Alvarez,Samy Blusseau,Santiago Velasco-Forero,Maria-Jose Jimenez*

Main category: math.MG

TL;DR: 提出多尺度闭运算指数（MCI），一种基于多尺度数学形态学的2D点集配准质量度量，取值0-1，值越小结构一致性越高


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Hausdorff类距离与Sliced Wasserstein距离难以刻画点集间结构层面的对齐程度，而生物图像细胞群体配准需要评价结构一致性

Method: 基于多尺度数学形态学闭运算构造归一化相异性度量；用Perlin噪声生成生物可信的合成点云，施加局部扰动、删除、平移、旋转、脉冲噪声等变换，并与Hausdorff类度量、Sliced Wasserstein距离对比

Result: MCI随扰动强度单调递增；同一空间结构与其变换版本之间与不同结构点云之间存在明显不连续（可比性差距）

Conclusion: MCI能捕捉点集结构一致性并量化偏差，可作为2D点集配准评价的补充度量

Abstract: We introduce Multi-scale Closing Index (MCI), a new dissimilarity measure for evaluating the alignment of two 2D point sets. MCI is bounded between 0 and 1 and is based on multiscale mathematical morphology operations, with smaller values indicating greater structural agreement. The measure was motivated by the need to assess the quality of registration between point sets representing cell populations in biological images. To evaluate its behaviour under controlled conditions, we generated synthetic point clouds from Perlin-noise-based spatial structures, following established approaches for simulating biologically plausible spatial organization. We assessed MCI between those point clouds and different transformed versions, obtained by local perturbation, point removal, translation, rotation and addition of impulsive noise. Across these experiments, the dissimilarity MCI increased gradually and consistently with perturbation severity, while showing a clear discontinuity between clouds originating from the same underlying spatial structure and point clouds generated from different structures. We further compared MCI with commonly used point-set distances, including Hausdorff-based measures and Sliced Wasserstein distance. These experiments show the ability of MCI to capture structural agreement and quantify deviations between point sets, highlighting its potential as a complementary metric for evaluating two-dimensional point-set registration.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [On the Few-Shot Adaptation of Vision-Language Models: A Few Gradient Signs Are All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08800)
*Nairouz Mrabah,Ismail Ben Ayed,Mahdi S. Hosseini,Eric Granger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对少样本适配视觉语言模型时监督稀缺导致梯度估计噪声大的问题，作者发现梯度幅值严重失准，但幅值最大的少量分量的符号仍有信息量，据此提出 SignSparseSGD 与 DynSignSparseSGD 两种无状态优化器：仅用幅值选择更新位置，用固定幅值的符号决定更新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
