Motivation: CLIP 类视觉语言模型少样本适配（如 prompt/adapter 微调）监督信号极少，梯度估计噪声大、幅值不可靠；作者观察到梯度幅值整体标定失准，但 top-magnitude 子集的方向符号仍具判别力，因此希望设计一种规避幅值噪声、且无需维护优化器状态、跨设定共享超参的优化器。

Method: 提出两种无状态（state-free）优化器：SignSparseSGD 用梯度幅值仅在首个 minibatch 上确定稀疏更新支撑集（support），之后固定该支撑并用固定幅值的符号更新；DynSignSparseSGD 在每一步刷新支撑集。两者不引入任务专用模块、不维护一阶/二阶动量状态，所有数据集/骨干/采样层级共用一套配置。

Result: 在 11 个数据集、3 个 CLIP 骨干、5 种 shot 设置上，平均而言与参数高效微调和基于优化的基线相当或更优，且对长训练日程的敏感性显著更低。

Conclusion: 少样本场景下优化器设计可通过"幅值选位置、符号定方向"的解耦来抑制梯度噪声；该方案轻量、通用、无状态，具备作为少样本适配默认优化器的潜力。

Abstract: The limited supervision available for the few-shot adaptation of vision-language models makes gradient estimation particularly noisy. We find that the gradient magnitudes are severely miscalibrated, while the signs of a small top-magnitude subset remain informative. This motivates SignSparseSGD and DynSignSparseSGD, two state-free optimizers that use gradient magnitudes only to select where to update and fixed-magnitude signs to decide how to update. The static variant fixes the support after the first minibatch. The dynamic variant refreshes it at every step. Across 11 datasets, three CLIP backbones, and five shot levels, the proposed methods are competitive with or outperform parameter-efficient and optimization-based baselines on average, while exhibiting markedly lower sensitivity to long training schedules. They introduce no task-specific modules, maintain no first- or second-moment optimizer states, and use one shared configuration across datasets, backbones, and shot levels. Our code is included in the supplementary materials and will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [107] [Global Solar Siting from Geospatial Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08831)
*Abdullah Rehman,Quentin Paletta,Rui Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 用地理空间嵌入(geospatial embeddings)学习太阳能选址的隐含适宜性信号,集成随机森林与MLP的软投票模型在13国验证中ROC-AUC中位数达0.90,并发布全球10米分辨率适宜性数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光伏装机快速扩张,但最优选址困难;现有MCDA方法依赖人工挑选的地理物理图层与太阳能资源产品,地理可迁移性差,且难以反映实际选址需求。

Method: 使用稠密地理空间嵌入向量表征位置的地球物理属性,基于38,591个已建光伏站点及其占用土地学习潜在适宜性信号;对比线性与非线性模型,选用随机森林+多层感知机的软投票集成(RF+MLP),兼顾精度与大规模可部署性;并在标准选址约束下做全球制图。

Result: 在13国未来新增装机上验证,集成模型区分真实站点与随机土地的ROC-AUC中位数为0.90;相较已发表MCDA方法在每国提升0.06–0.30;最适宜20%土地集中了84%估算发电量与67%装机容量;施加标准约束后全球5.6%土地(允许耕地时9.8%)高度适宜,并发布10米分辨率全球数据集与代码。

Conclusion: 地理空间嵌入+集成学习可跨区域泛化地刻画光伏选址适宜性,显著优于传统MCDA,并可作为公开的全球高分辨率选址工具;本质是地球观测/地理空间机器学习应用,与具身智能、3D重建等核心方向无关。

Abstract: Global solar photovoltaic (PV) capacity is rapidly growing, helping decrease our emissions and dependence on fossil fuels. However, optimally siting a solar plant remains challenging, requiring anticipation of geographical, urban, and infrastructure needs. Current methods rely on multi-criteria decision analysis (MCDA) using hand-picked geophysical layers and solar-resource products, however these strategies are not geographically transferable and do not capture the practical requirements of solar sites. We use geospatial embeddings, dense vectors representing geophysical properties of a location, to analyse 38,591 existing solar sites and the land they were built on to learn the latent solar suitability signal. We assess a set of linear and non-linear models and select a soft-voting ensemble of a random forest and a multilayer perceptron (RF+MLP ensemble), chosen for accuracy and large scale deployability. Validated against future installations in thirteen countries, the ensemble separates existing solar sites from random land with a median ROC-AUC of 0.90. Compared with published MCDA strategies, the ensemble increases ROC-AUC by 0.06-0.30 in every country. The ranking also concentrates real deployment: the most-suitable 20% of land holds 84% of estimated generation (annual energy yield) and 67% of installed capacity (potential power output). After applying standard siting constraints, 5.6% of global land (9.8% if cropland is permitted) remains highly suited to solar. The global 10 m resolution solar suitability dataset and code can be found here: this https URL .

</details>


### [108] [Ordered Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08936)
*Dongkyu Derek Cho,Pan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OPO（Ordered Policy Optimization）：在PPO中放弃固定clip区间，改为按minibatch对重要性比值排序，对极值比值分配样本级自适应裁剪半径，半径由预设的尾部分布"目标轮廓"推导；只需minibatch排序与修改后的策略损失，仍为一阶目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO对所有样本使用统一固定裁剪区间，无法区分统计上有害的极端比值与较大但可容忍的策略变化；作者通过案例研究表明可靠更新需控制比值分布尾部的极端值，而非仅检查每个比值是否落在固定区间内。

Method: 在每次minibatch中对重要性比值排序，选出对更新影响可能最大的极端比值，用指定tail profile（为所选集值设定目标值）反推每个样本的自适应裁剪半径，替换统一半径，构造修改后的policy loss，保持一阶优化与实现简洁性。

Result: 在MuJoCo与Atari基准上，这一简单改动相对既有基线（如标准PPO等）取得有竞争力的性能表现；论文主要以案例分析与基准实验支撑有效性。

Conclusion: 将裁剪半径与重要性比值分布的可靠性绑定、对尾部极值实施样本级自适应裁剪，是稳定策略更新的有效且低成本手段；OPO可视为PPO的即插即用替换。

Abstract: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) stabilizes policy updates by clipping the importance ratio between the new policy and the data-collecting policy. The standard rule uses one fixed clipping range for all samples. While this choice offers simplicity and effectiveness, it fails to distinguish statistically harmful extreme ratio values from large, yet tolerable changes. We study how to adapt the clipping radius to the reliability of the induced importance ratio distribution. Our case study demonstrates that reliable updates require controlling these extreme ratios, rather than merely verifying that individual ratios fall within a fixed interval. Motivated by this observation, we propose Ordered Policy Optimization (\textsc{OPO}). In each minibatch, \textsc{OPO} sorts the importance ratios, selects the extreme ratios that potentially have the largest effect on the update, and assigns them adaptive sample-wise radii instead of using the fixed clipping radius. These radii are derived from a prescribed tail profile, which specifies desired target values for the selected extreme ratios. The resulting objective remains first-order and requires only minibatch sorting and a modified policy loss. Experiments conducted on MuJoCo and Atari benchmarks demonstrate that this simple modification achieves strong performance relative to established baselines.

</details>


### [109] [Augmentron: Scalable Multi-View Visual Augmentation for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09145)
*Tanay Tandon,Aseem Doriwala,Jade Choghari,Catherine Weaver,Pragna Mannam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Augmentron：几何感知的机器人数据增强管线，用可复用场景表示+录制相机位姿渲染，生成时序与多视角一致的环境/工作空间变化，显著提升策略OOD泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉单一示范训练的机器人策略易受任务无关环境特征干扰；逐帧逐视角的生成式增强代价高且跨相机/跨时间不一致。

Method: 几何感知管线：构建可复用场景几何表示，按录制的相机位姿渲染，实现时序与多视角一致的环境/工作空间多样化，同时保留机器人、任务物体、动作与标注。

Result: 三个物理操作任务中，用Augmentron数据替换一半真实训练数据，在OOD干扰物与OOD工作空间变化两类评测上均提升成功率；与替代增强方法比较，三种条件下池化成功率最高。

Conclusion: 将几何一致的数据增强纳入训练配方可改善策略在动态真实环境中的泛化性。

Abstract: Robot policies trained on visually narrow demonstrations can become sensitive to environmental features that are unrelated to the task. Existing generative augmentation methods can introduce visual diversity, but processing every frame and camera view independently is computationally expensive and may produce inconsistent observations across cameras and timestamps. We introduce Augmentron, a geometry-aware pipeline that generates reusable scene representations and renders them through recorded camera poses. This produces temporally and multi-view consistent environ- ment and workspace variations while preserving the robot, task objects, actions, and annotations. Across three physical manipulation tasks, replacing half of the real world training dataset with Augmentron data improves model success across three tasks for both in out-of-distribution distractor objects, and out-of-distribution workspace variation evaluations. In a separate comparison against alternate augmentation techniques, Augmentron achieves the highest pooled success in all three conditions. The results show that introducing augmented data to a data training recipe can help policies generalize to dynamic real-world settings.

</details>


### [110] [SIFR: Stable Signed Flow Reinforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09175)
*Runlong Liao,Xingqian Xu,Lizhang Chen,Baiyu Su,Yufan Huang,Zhonghao Wang,Akash Gokul,Ming-Yu Liu,Qiang Liu,Yogesh Balaji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SIFR用有符号优势替代正奖励权重以补上"抑制低奖励样本"的信号，并对相对参考模型的velocity drift施加Lagrange约束（自适应正则强度），统一解释现有flow RL方法，在多尺度T2I骨干上稳定长程训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于奖励微调flow模型需同时提升高奖励、抑制低奖励样本并在多轮迭代中保持稳定。迭代奖励微调只有正奖励权重，缺少显式"降权/不拟合"信号；反复重加权会使分布收窄并导致崩溃。

Method: 提出SIFR：(1) 用有符号优势signed advantage替代奖励权重，提供缺失的unfitting信号；(2) 约束相对参考模型的velocity drift，用Lagrange乘子把正则强度自适应到预设drift预算；(3) 理论分析表明现有flow RL方法均隐式最小化该signed loss，仅正则选择不同。

Result: 在SD3.5 Medium(2.5B)、FLUX.2 Klein(9B)及Cosmos 3 Super Text2Image(64B)上验证，覆盖12组backbone-reward组合（偏好奖励与规则奖励），均持平或超过现有方法，更快达到其峰值奖励，并在长程训练中持续提升，而基线出现平台期或崩溃。

Conclusion: 有符号优势+drift预算约束为flow模型奖励微调提供了一个稳定、可统一解释的框架，具备良好扩展性，但属生成模型后训练而非具身/3D/世界模型方向。

Abstract: Reward-based fine-tuning of flow models requires effectively promoting high-reward and suppressing low-reward samples, while maintaining training stability over many rounds of iterative updates. Iterative reward fine-tuning provides a simple starting point, but the positive reward weights give no explicit unfitting signal, and repeated reweighting concentrates the distribution and causes collapse. We propose Stable Signed Flow Reinforcement (SIFR), which replaces the reward weights by signed advantages to supply the missing unfitting signal, and constrains the velocity drift from a reference model to stabilize the updates, with a Lagrange multiplier that adapts the regularization strength to a prescribed drift budget. We further show that existing flow RL methods fall within this framework: they implicitly minimize the signed loss, differing in their regularization choices. We validate SIFR on SD3.5 Medium (2.5B) and FLUX.2 Klein (9B) and scale it to Cosmos 3 Super Text2Image (64B): across all 12 backbone--reward pairs with preference and rule-based rewards, it matches or exceeds existing methods, reaches their peak rewards much faster, and keeps improving over long training where they plateau or collapse.

</details>


### [111] [A Stochastic Latent Variable Generalization of Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09302)
*Nikos Giakoumoglou,Paschalis Giakoumoglou,Tania Stathaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: S-JEPA将JEPA中将辅助变量z由固定确定性token改为显式随机隐变量，用学习后验（训练）与上下文条件先验（推理）建模多模态补全分布，无解码器、无重建；性能不降反升并产生不确定性图与多样性补全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原JEPA蓝图中z本应是随机潜变量，但此前所有实例化都把z塌缩为固定确定性token，把多模态补全分布退化为点估计，丧失了表征补全不确定性与多样性的能力。

Method: 面向掩码图像预测：训练时用学习后验q(z|上下文,目标)推断z，推理时用上下文条件先验p(z|上下文)近似可行补全分布；保持无解码器、无像素重建，仅预测潜空间嵌入。其目标在确定性极限下函数形式约化为I-JEPA损失，属于对I-JEPA目标的推广而非严格包含该模型。

Result: 在架构与训练调度匹配的条件下，S-JEPA优于确定性I-JEPA基线，并超过同架构的LeJEPA，表征质量无损；额外获得空间结构化预测不确定性图与语义多样的补全结果，这是确定性预测器无法产生的。

Conclusion: 将z实现为真正的随机潜变量可为JEPA类自监督表征学习"免费"增加误差相关的预测不确定性与多模态补全多样性的能力，拓展了JEPA框架的表达力。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) predict the latent embedding of a target signal from a context signal, with an auxiliary variable z conditioning the predictor. Despite its prominent role in the original JEPA blueprint, the content of z has been carried by a fixed deterministic token in every prior instantiation, collapsing the genuinely multi-modal distribution over plausible completions to a point estimate. We present S-JEPA, which realises z as a proper stochastic latent variable for masked image prediction: a learned posterior infers z from context and target during training, while a learned context-conditioned prior approximates the distribution of plausible completions at inference, with no decoders and no reconstruction. In its deterministic limit the objective reduces to the I-JEPA loss in functional form, generalising that objective rather than strictly containing the I-JEPA model. Our central finding is that stochasticity adds capabilities, error-correlated predictive uncertainty and diverse completions, at no cost to representation quality: under matched architectures and schedules S-JEPA improves over the deterministic I-JEPA baseline and exceeds LeJEPA at matched architecture, while additionally producing spatially structured uncertainty maps and semantically diverse completions, neither of which the deterministic predictor produces.

</details>


### [112] [Encoding Memory in World Models with Test-Time Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09625)
*Maxime Burchi,Fayaz Ali Dharejo,Radu Timofte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TT-MBRL：在测试时用梯度下降把当前episode的观测记忆编码进世界模型参数，为每个episode学习专属世界模型，超越仅依赖上下文记忆的范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型强化学习（MBRL）在需要长期记忆与规划的任务上仍难以超越人类，且主流做法局限于架构设计，上下文记忆容量有限。

Method: 测试时梯度下降：在推理阶段针对单个episode的观测序列更新世界模型参数，将情节记忆写入参数而非仅存于上下文；先在简化二维Memory Maze 2D上做概念验证，覆盖多种世界模型架构，再扩展到3D Memory Maze与DeepMind Control、Atari 100k。

Result: Memory Maze 2D上跨架构一致提升；3D Memory Maze刷新SOTA；在无记忆需求的DMC与Atari 100k上性能保持稳定。

Conclusion: 测试时参数级记忆是提升MBRL长期记忆能力的有效且架构无关的路径，且不牺牲通用控制性能；但其本质是单场景逐episode拟合，与机器人/具身核心议题关联较间接。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) approaches have demonstrated strong performance across diverse domains in recent years, learning accurate world models from agent trajectories. However, despite this progress, current approaches still struggle to surpass human performance on tasks that require long-term memory and planning. In this work, we address the problem of long-term memory in MBRL by moving beyond the commonly studied paradigm of architecture design. We introduce Test-Time Model-Based Reinforcement Learning (TT-MBRL), using gradient descent at test time to encode the memory of episode observations into world model parameters. This enables the agent to learn a distinct world model for each episode, improving memory capabilities compared to traditional approaches that solely rely on contextual memory. We first establish a proof of concept on a simplified two-dimensional variant of the Memory Maze environment, which we refer to as Memory Maze 2D, showing consistent improvements across different world model architectures. We then demonstrate the effectiveness of TT-MBRL in the 3D domain, achieving new state-of-the-art results on the Memory Maze benchmark. Finally, we show that TT-MBRL maintains strong performance on non-memory benchmarks such as DeepMind Control and Atari 100k.

</details>


### [113] [Computer-Use Agents Are Embodied Generalists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09847)
*Pranjal Aggarwal,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将具身任务统一表述为对"数字软件"的交互：用软件渲染智能体视角并提供控制，使 computer-use agent（CUA）仅凭截图+键鼠操作完成具身任务，同一设计在四个 ECCV workshop 竞赛中获第 1/2/3/6 名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用模型已在诸多 AI 领域取代手工方法与专用架构，作者主张具身 AI 也将发生同样转变，希望用统一的通用智能体（CUA）替代任务专用架构。

Method: 构建把物理环境渲染为屏幕视图并提供操作控制的软件层，使 CUA 通过截图观测与键盘/鼠标动作执行具身任务；保持单一 CUA 设计与提示，仅更换软件，不做竞赛专用微调。

Result: 在四个 ECCV workshop 竞赛中取得第一、第二、第三与第六名，且无需任何针对赛题的智能体调优，说明视觉定位、精确点击与探索等软件技能可迁移至具身任务。

Conclusion: CUA 可成为"具身通才"，按通用智能体的训练策略，具身 AI 有望收敛到单一通用模型；但证据限于竞赛榜单与界面抽象，缺少真机部署与定量消融。

Abstract: In many areas of AI, general-purpose models have increasingly replaced handcrafted methods and specialized architectures. We argue that embodied AI is poised for a similar shift. Our key claim is that embodied tasks can be formulated as interaction with digital software. Specifically, we build software that renders an agent's view of a physical environment on a screen and provides controls for acting in it. A computer-use agent (CUA) can then perform embodied tasks through the same screenshots and keyboard and mouse actions that it uses to operate ordinary software. Using a single CUA design and changing only the software, our entries win first-, second-, third-, and sixth-place prizes across four ECCV workshop competitions, without any competition-specific agent tuning. These results suggest that the skills CUAs learn on software, such as visual grounding, precise clicking, and exploration, already carry over to embodied tasks, giving CUAs a far broader scope in embodied AI than previously realized. We therefore advocate that computer-use agents can be embodied generalists. Further, we advocate that, by following the training strategy that made computer-use agents general, embodied AI can converge on a single general model as well, ushering in a new era of general embodied AI. Website: this https URL

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [EventHOI: Event-Driven Resource-Efficient Hand-Object Interaction Logging on Smartglasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09089)
*Le Zhang,Kailai Cui,Hao Chen,Vlad Roznyatovskiy,Jianzhong Zhang,Anhong Guo,Kang G. Shin,Ke Sun*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: EventHOI：用低功耗事件相机做门控与空间压缩，结合IMU自运动消除，实现智能眼镜上BLE带宽内的自我中心手-物交互视频日志。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能眼镜持续RGB采集与无线传输超出能量与带宽预算；需在保留下游HOI日志性能前提下实现低功耗、低带宽的自我中心记录。

Method: 事件驱动感知+HOI感知编码：低频事件流判断何时触发RGB采集、何区域保留细节；IMU自运动消除抑制自运动事件以凸显手-物运动；据此做触发与空间压缩；配智能眼镜原型，端到端评测。

Result: 相比纯RGB基线，常开后端功耗降低5.8-10.4倍，上行带宽降低2.9倍，HOI识别精度损失极小；上行<130kbps，可纳入商用BLE带宽。

Conclusion: 事件感知门控+HOI感知压缩使BLE视频流式自我中心HOI日志可行，为可穿戴长期交互记录提供实用系统方案。

Abstract: Hand-object interactions (HOIs) can be captured by smartglasses to learn how people manipulate objects in daily life. Automatic logging of these interactions from an egocentric perspective provides time-stamped evidence of daily actions for applications like memory assistance. However, continuous RGB capture and wireless transmission exceed the energy and bandwidth budgets of smartglasses. We present EventHOI, an event-driven sensing and compression system that couples selective RGB capture with HOI-aware encoding to enable egocentric logging over Bluetooth Low Energy (BLE) while preserving downstream HOI logging performance. Our key insight is to use low-power event sensing both to determine when RGB capture is needed and to identify where visual detail must be preserved. A lightweight IMU-based egomotion cancellation pipeline suppresses egomotion-induced events so that residual HOI motion can guide RGB triggering and spatial compression. We build a smartglasses prototype and conduct an end-to-end evaluation of EventHOI on a 50-hour egocentric benchmark with 97 subjects and a 20-hour real-world dataset we collect from 10 participants, together covering a broad range of daily-life scenarios and environments. Compared to RGB-only baselines, EventHOI reduces always-on backend power consumption by 5.8-10.4x and uplink bandwidth by 2.9x, with minimal HOI recognition accuracy loss. The resulting RGB uplink of less than 130 kbps fits within commodity BLE bandwidth budgets, making stable BLE video streaming feasible for egocentric HOI logging. Demo video and project website: this https URL

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Q-OIL: Optimistic Exploration in Learning from Human Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08576)
*Pranav Teegavarapu,Sriyash Poddar,Rico Qi,Cleah Winston,Krisha Khandelwal,Aykut Onyol,Natasha Jaques,Abhishek Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 分析真实人类干预行为，提出以"进度停滞"驱动的干预模型以改进仿真基准，并提出Q-OIL方法（在干预转移上加权乐观加成并软引导探索），配合解耦critic保证稳定，在仿真与真机上取得10-50%成功率提升并可增强预训练VLA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人部署中难免出错，人类干预是有价值的在线反馈，但真人在环学习实验昂贵缓慢，现有算法开发依赖与真人行为不符的仿真人类模型，导致基准失真。

Method: 1) 分析真人干预数据，发现干预多发于智能体进度停滞时且纠正动作常次优；2) 据此构建基于进度的干预模型，用于仿真中以提升算法排序相关性；3) Q-OIL：对干预转移加乐观加成、软引导探索朝人类纠正方向；采用解耦critic防止加成回传到干预前状态。

Result: 仿真人类模型与真实用户算法排名相关性更高；Q-OIL在仿真和真机实验中成功率比竞争基线高10-50%；在两个双臂操作任务上提升预训练VLA表现。

Conclusion: 将评估基准与学习算法同时锚定在真实人类干预行为上，为可扩展的从干预中学习提供了实用路径。

Abstract: As robots are deployed in the real world, they will inevitably make mistakes. Human interventions provide valuable feedback for improving their behavior, enabling them to adapt online to new scenarios. However, studying human-in-the-loop robot learning is difficult: real-world experiments are slow and expensive, so most algorithm development relies on simulated human models. We show that common human modeling assumptions do not match how real people intervene in practice. Through analyzing intervention data from human participants, we find that humans tend to intervene when agent progress stalls, and that their corrective actions are often suboptimal. Motivated by these findings, we introduce a progress-based model of human intervention that achieves higher rank correlation with real-user algorithm rankings, enabling realistic algorithm benchmarking and development in simulation. Building on these insights, we propose Q-OIL, a method that adds optimistic bonuses to intervention transitions in reinforcement learning and softly guides exploration toward human corrections. We find that decoupled critics are critical for stability because they prevent the bonuses from backing up to pre-intervention states. As a result, Q-OIL learns more efficiently than competitive baselines, achieving 10-50% higher success in simulation and on real robots, and improving pretrained VLAs on two bimanual manipulation tasks. As we ground both evaluation and algorithms in realistic human behavior, this work provides a practical path toward scalable robot learning from interventions.

</details>


### [116] [Meituan-Robotics-0: A Vision-Language-Action Foundation Model for Desktop Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08577)
*Meituan Robotics Team,Caicheng Wang,Chen Zhao,Chengpeng Jin,Duo Xu,Fengjiao Chen,Guanglu Wan,Heqing Yang,Jia Wang,Jiacong Zhou,Jie Hu,Jiyuan Ru,Lingfeng Zhang,Linjiang Huang,Manyuan Zhang,Ningchu Liu,Shijie Zhou,Si Liu,Shuo Li,Tianyi Zhang,Xi Zhang,Xin Chen,Xuezhi Cao,Xunliang Cai,Yanbin Zeng,Yang Yang,Yang Yi,Yanli Tan,Yifu Xu,Yimeng Jia,Yuchen Tang,Yuchen Xie,Yuxiang Zheng,Zerwei Chng,Zilong Huang,Zipeng Ye,Ziyuan Qin,Ziqin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MR0 是面向固定基座桌面操作的多模态 VLA 基座模型与具身智能体系统，融合 1.47 亿图文样本与 15,060 小时、12 种本体的机器人轨迹，采用离散动作 token 预训练+流匹配跨本体中训练+真机后训练三阶段配方，并提出 Rollback 为 CFG-DAgger 提供正负监督，真实任务成功率超过 π0.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建可跨异构本体、具备跨任务事件记忆与中断恢复能力的桌面操作 VLA 基座模型，并降低 DAgger 式在线纠错后训练中对价值学习与优势标注的依赖。

Method: 1) 大规模预训练：1.47 亿图文样本 + 15,060 小时、12 本体轨迹；2) 三阶段训练：离散动作 token 预训练对齐多模态表示→流匹配中训练迁移到连续跨本体控制→真机后训练适配策略；3) Rollback：将被替换的策略片段与人类纠正配对，为 CFG-DAgger 提供正负监督，免去价值函数与优势标注；4) 统一末端执行器接口支持异构本体；5) 异步 action chunk 合并以在推理延迟波动下保持执行平滑；6) 事件驱动的智能体维护持久任务事件、进度/可行性变化时重规划，支持跨任务检索与条件式任务恢复。

Result: RoboTwin 2.0 成功率 93.6%，RoboDojo VLA 赛道第五；在匹配 HG-DAgger 后训练条件下，三个真机任务的平均成功率与进度分均高于 π0.5；以太网插入任务中，使用 Rollback 数据的 CFG-DAgger 达 70% 成功率与 0.90 进度分；定性展示跨任务事件检索与暂时不满足任务条件下的恢复。

Conclusion: MR0 表明“离散 token 预训练→流匹配跨本体中训练→真机 CFG-DAgger 后训练”的三阶段路线可在异构桌面操作本体上取得强真机表现，Rollback 提供了一种无需价值学习的廉价纠错监督；事件驱动智能体层补充了长时任务记忆与恢复能力。

Abstract: We present Meituan-Robotics-0 (MR0), a vision-language-action foundation model and embodied-agent system for stationary-base desktop manipulation. MR0 combines 147 million vision-language samples with 15,060 hours of curated robot trajectories spanning 12 embodiments. A three-stage training recipe aligns multimodal representations with robot behavior through discrete action-token pre-training, transfers these priors to continuous cross-embodiment control through flow-matching mid-training, and adapts the policy through real-world post-training. We introduce Rollback, which pairs replaced policy segments with human corrections to provide positive and negative supervision for CFG-DAgger without value learning or advantage labeling. A unified end-effector interface supports heterogeneous embodiments, while asynchronous action-chunk merging maintains smooth execution under variable inference latency. An event-driven agent maintains persistent task events and reassesses plans when progress or feasibility changes, enabling cross-task retrieval and conditional task resumption. MR0 achieves 93.6% success on RoboTwin 2.0 and ranks fifth in the RoboDojo VLA track. Under matched HG-DAgger post-training, MR0 achieves higher average success rate and progress score than $\pi_{0.5}$ across three real-robot tasks. On Ethernet insertion, CFG-DAgger trained with Rollback data reaches 70% success and a progress score of 0.90. Qualitative agent deployments demonstrate cross-task event retrieval and recovery from temporarily unsatisfied task conditions.

</details>


### [117] [CS-Pi: Constraint-Satisfying Flow Policy Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08654)
*Soumojit Bhattacharya,Oliver Kroemer,Shubham Tulsiani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CS-Pi: a draw-project-renoise sampler that enforces inference-time constraints on flow-matching policies by projecting each sample to the nearest safe action, re-noising proportionally to the projection displacement, and re-running the unmodified policy until a safe draw is found, giving both plausibility and efficiency.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deployed flow-matching policies often face constraints absent from demonstrations (e.g., unseen obstacles). Retraining is impractical, so constraints must be enforced at inference while preserving task competence: actions must be both safe and plausible under the policy. Gradient guidance evaluates cost on the policy's mean estimate of the clean action, which can average bimodal modes (left/right detours) and pass straight through the obstacle; rejection is intractable when safe actions are rare; projection is efficient but yields implausible actions.

Method: Exploit the structure of flow-matching sampling to combine rejection and projection: CS-Pi draws a sample, projects it onto the nearest constraint-satisfying action, adds noise scaled to the projection distance to move back toward the policy's distribution, and re-runs the unmodified pretrained policy to denoise, repeating until a safe sample is produced. Substituting a different projection step extends the method to continuous costs.

Result: Evaluated on a 2D diagnostic, a 7-DoF shelf pick-and-place, an orientation-preference task, and a zero-shot Franka deployment: 81% safe success on the shelf and 55% on hardware, versus 55% and 40% for projection, while maintaining plausibility.

Conclusion: Projection-rejection hybrids within flow-matching sampling yield higher safe-success than pure projection and remain plausible, enabling inference-time constraint enforcement without retraining, and generalize to continuous cost constraints via a swapped projection step.

Abstract: A flow-matching policy deployed in the field must often satisfy a constraint that was absent from its demonstrations, such as avoiding an obstacle it has never seen. Retraining for every new constraint is impractical, so the constraint must be enforced at inference time. Enforcing it must not discard what the policy learned: an action the policy would rarely sample may avoid the obstacle yet fail the task. We therefore want actions that are both safe, satisfying the constraint, and plausible, remaining likely under the policy. Gradient guidance, the most common approach, steers noisy states, so it must evaluate its cost on the policy's running estimate of the clean action; when the policy is uncertain between passing left or right of an obstacle, this estimate is their average and can pass straight through it. We instead build on rejection and projection. Rejection redraws actions until one is safe: every output is plausible, but the wait is intractable when safe actions are rare. Projection moves an action to the nearest safe one: it is efficient, but the result need not be plausible. Our key insight is that flow-matching sampling lets us combine the two, keeping projection's efficiency and rejection's plausibility. CS-Pi, our draw-project-renoise sampler, projects each draw to the nearest safe action, adds noise back scaled to how far the projection moved it, and re-runs the unmodified policy until a draw is safe. Replacing only the projection step extends CS-Pi to continuous costs. Across a 2D diagnostic, a 7-DoF shelf pick-and-place, an orientation preference, and a zero-shot Franka deployment, CS-Pi reaches 81% safe success on the shelf and 55% on hardware, against 55% and 40% for projection, while staying plausible.

</details>


### [118] [ForPC: Force-Aware Whole-Body Control for Precise Humanoid Cart Pushing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08664)
*ZhenYu Zou,Xing Liu,ShiChuang Wang,YunLong Dong,LiNan Deng,Feng Hua,Jun Wan,Cheng Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForPC：力感知全身控制框架，用两阶段强化学习调节人形机器人推车速度；先训运动先验提供平衡支撑，再从本体观测推断力隐变量与状态隐变量驱动残差策略协调臂/腰/腿调整，无需外力传感器与人类示范，仿真训练可直接迁移至实体 Unitree G1，推 30 kg 车达 0.5 m/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在制造、物流、医疗中承担重物搬运时，直接操作现有轮式推车可避免改造工作场所；但双臂抓握会形成力耦合闭环——驱动推车的接触力同时也扰动平衡，且负载变化会改变耦合动力学，既有运动控制方法难以兼顾速度跟踪与稳定。

Method: 两阶段强化学习：阶段一在多种手部负载与上身姿态下训练运动先验，提供平衡支撑；阶段二从机载观测（无外部力传感器）推断力潜变量与状态潜变量，输入残差策略，联合输出有界的手臂、腰部、腿部调整量与基座速度指令。多目标奖励显式惩罚双臂内部对顶力、抓握滑移与执行器饱和，鼓励按需施加接触力，无需人类示范或负载特定调参。

Result: Sim-to-sim 中，相较仅运动控制基线（Loco-only），车体速度 RMSE 最高降低 68.2%，多弯道路线横向误差 RMSE 最高降低 74.2%；纯仿真训练的策略零样本、零微调迁移至实体 Unitree G1，在无负载特定调整情况下以 0.5 m/s 推动载重 30 kg 的推车。

Conclusion: 表明通过隐式力估计与残差全身控制可解耦"推力—平衡"耦合，人形机器人有望在真实场景替代人类完成高效物料搬运；后续可关注更长路线、更大负载范围与真实环境鲁棒性验证。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are expected to take over strenuous material handling in manufacturing, logistics, and healthcare, where operating existing wheeled carts would let them move heavy loads without modifying the workplace. However, humanoid cart pushing remains challenging because the bimanual grasp closes a force-coupled loop in which the contact wrenches that drive the cart also disturb the robot's balance, and payload changes alter the coupled dynamics. We propose ForPC, a force-aware whole-body control framework that regulates cart velocity through two-stage reinforcement learning. A locomotion prior is first trained under varied hand loads and upper-body postures to provide balance support. A force latent and a state latent are then inferred from onboard observations, without external force sensors, and fed to a residual policy that coordinates bounded arm, waist, and leg adjustments with base-velocity commands. Multi-objective rewards penalize bimanual internal opposition, grasp slip, and actuator saturation, encouraging the policy to apply contact force on demand without human demonstrations. Sim-to-sim experiments show that ForPC reduces cart-velocity RMSE by up to 68.2% relative to a locomotion-only baseline (Loco-only) and cross-track RMSE by up to 74.2% on the multi-bend route. Trained solely in simulation, the policy also transfers to a physical Unitree G1 without real-world training or fine-tuning and, with no payload-specific adjustment, pushes a cart carrying up to 30 kg at 0.5 m/s. These results suggest that humanoid robots could eventually replace humans for efficient material handling in daily life.

</details>


### [119] [National and State-Level Approaches to Automated Vehicle Certification: A Comparative Framework for State Operational Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08681)
*Sk Nahia Ahsan,Md Abdullah Al Hasan,Danjue Chen,George List*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文系统比较美国各州对SAE L4-5自动驾驶系统的认证监管模式，归纳出三模型类型学并与欧、中、日、澳国际框架对照，评估北卡罗来纳州监管策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国缺乏统一的联邦AV认证框架，各州授权与监管方式差异显著，需要一致的分析基准来比较监管介入程度、治理复杂度与机构责任。

Method: 审查公开的州法规、行政法规、机构指南与联邦政策文件；选取10个代表性州，用涵盖运营范围、治理与监督、联邦互动、实施效果的结构化框架分析；并对比欧盟、中国、日本、澳大利亚的认证实践。

Result: 识别出三种州级认证模型：基线法律合规型、带持续监督的行政自认证型、需部署前正式机构审查的许可授权型；北卡目前属基线合规型。国际框架趋同于场景化测试、ODD专项批准、安全案例文档、网络安全保障与全生命周期监督，但向美国的直接可移植性有限。

Conclusion: 提出三模型类型学并整合州实践与国际框架，为跨辖区评估AV监管差异提供一致基础；属政策法规研究，不涉及技术方法。

Abstract: Automated vehicle (AV) deployment in the United States is expanding without a unified federal certification framework, leading to substantial variation in how states authorize and oversee AV testing and deployment. This paper examines how U.S. states regulate Society of Automotive Engineers (SAE) Level 4-5 automated driving systems (ADS), identifies common certification approaches, compares them with international practices, and evaluates the implications for North Carolina's future regulatory strategy. Publicly available state statutes, administrative regulations, agency guidance, and federal policy documents were reviewed. Ten states, selected to capture the range of regulatory approaches, were analyzed using a structured framework covering operational scope, governance and oversight, federal interaction, and implementation outcomes. Certification practices in the European Union, China, Japan, and Australia were reviewed for comparative context. Three state certification models were identified, reflecting increasing regulatory involvement: baseline legal compliance, administrative self-certification with ongoing oversight, and permit-based authorization requiring formal agency review before deployment. North Carolina currently aligns with the baseline compliance model. International frameworks are converging toward scenario-based testing, operational-design-domain-specific approval, safety-case documentation, cybersecurity assurance, and lifecycle oversight, although direct transferability to the U.S. is limited. The paper develops a three-model typology for comparing state AV certification approaches and integrates state practices with international frameworks, providing a consistent basis for evaluating differences in regulatory involvement, governance complexity, and institutional responsibilities across jurisdictions.

</details>


### [120] [VICTR: Value-Informed In-Context Learning for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08698)
*Luca Macesanu,Tianluo Zhang,Aditya Patil,Archit Sharma,Kevin Angers,Christopher M. Clark,Chen Feng,Juexiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VICTR：用「进度感知的上下文检索」为 VLA 实现免微调的 in-context learning，核心是以上下文价值函数估计器（IC-VFE）预测任务相对进度，据此检索示范片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于检索后训练赋予 VLA 的 ICL 能力依赖「选出与当前执行阶段相关的示范片段」，而纯视觉相似度无法刻画执行阶段差异，导致检索到的上下文与当前进度错位。

Method: 提出 IC-VFE：以参考示范视频、语言指令与当前观测为条件回归任务相对进度；因显式条件于参考示范，可对训练中未见任务对齐状态与示范阶段。用估计进度从示范中检索对应阶段的 chunk，输入经 in-context 动作生成后训练的 π0.5 策略执行。

Result: 在未见过的域外真实机器人操作任务上，成功率从微调 π0.5 基线的 6.2% 提升至 23.8%，任务进度率由 28.9% 提升至 55.5%（翻倍以上）。

Conclusion: 进度信号是优于视觉相似度的检索判据，可显著增强 VLA 的跨任务 ICL 泛化；但绝对成功率仍偏低，说明示范检索式 ICL 在域外长程任务上仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Recent advances in robot foundation models have highlighted the potential of in-context learning (ICL) for adapting robot behavior from demonstrations without further fine-tuning. Previous work has used retrieval-based post-training to equip pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) models with the capability for ICL by teaching them to condition action predictions on retrieved visually-relevant demonstration chunks. However, the effectiveness of retrieval hinges on selecting context relevant to the current execution stage, which visual similarity alone may not capture. We introduce VICTR, a framework enabling in-context learning in VLAs via progress-informed context retrieval built around an In-Context Value Function Estimator (IC-VFE). IC-VFE conditions on a reference demonstration video, a language instruction and the current observation to estimate task-relative progress. By conditioning progress estimation on a reference demonstration, IC-VFE can align the current state with a demonstrated execution even for tasks unseen during training. VICTR uses the estimated progress to select demonstration chunks at corresponding stages of execution and supplies them to a $\pi_{0.5}$ policy post-trained for in-context action generation. On unseen out-of-domain real-world manipulation tasks, VICTR raises the success rate from 6.2% for a fine-tuned $\pi_{0.5}$ baseline to 23.8% and more than doubles the task progress rate, from 28.9% to 55.5%. Project page with code, data and videos: this https URL

</details>


### [121] [It Takes Two to Tangram: Multi-Agent Object Centric Reinforcement Learning for Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08814)
*Paul Knaußt,Kay Pompetzki,Nico Bohlinger,Niklas Funk,Cristiana de Farias,Rolf Findeisen,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以物体为中心的强化学习框架,将每个零件视为一个智能体,用图神经网络编码零件、目标位姿与空间关系,通过带中心化图critic的per-agent PPO学习共享策略,在平面七巧板装配中实现并行装配并迁移到真实机器人顺序执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何装配需在紧配合下精细的时空协调,以避免碰撞与死锁;传统采样规划难以生成实时闭环协调控制,而端到端策略又缺乏对多零件关系结构的显式建模。

Method: 以物体为中心的多智能体RL:每零件一个agent,共享策略经GNN处理零件/目标位姿/空间关系并输出各部件平移与角速度;采用中心化图值网络(centralized graph critic)的per-agent PPO单反馈策略内同时学习运动与时间协调;程序化生成目标构型与随机初始布局;消融关系结构与多智能体优化,对比采样规划;仿真并行装配后迁移至真机顺序执行。

Result: 在多样平面七巧板装配问题上训练与评估,消融证实关系结构建模与多智能体优化各自的贡献;仿真中实现并发(同时)装配,并将策略零改动物理层地迁移到真实机器人顺序执行,表现优于/可对比采样规划。

Conclusion: 对象中心化形式将高层装配协调与低层物理控制解耦,提供可迁移的共享多智能体装配策略;局限在于任务限于平面紧公差装配,真机为顺序执行,三维、重力/接触丰富场景与并行真机有效性尚未验证。

Abstract: Geometric assembly requires fine spatiotemporal coordination to place parts without causing collisions or deadlocks under tight clearances. To address this, we present an object-centric reinforcement learning framework that treats each part as an agent and learns a shared policy for continuous assembly control. A graph neural network represents the pieces, their target poses, and their spatial relationships, and predicts translational and angular velocities for each component. Per-agent proximal policy optimization with a centralized graph critic directly learns motion and temporal coordination within a single feedback policy. We study this formulation through planar Tangram assembly, where pieces must slide and rotate into a given configuration while remaining on the assembly surface. Procedural generation of target configurations and randomized initial arrangements support training and evaluation across diverse assembly problems. Our experiments isolate the roles of relational structure and multi-agent optimization, and compare learned control with sampling-based planning. We demonstrate concurrent assembly in simulation and transfer the policy to sequential execution on a real robot, as our object-centric formulation decouples learned assembly coordination from low-level physical control. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [122] [ActPrune: Action-Grounded Visual Token Pruning for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08827)
*Sungwon Moon,Minji Kim,Hyemin Boo,Daeun Song,Jiyoung Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出训练无关的视觉token剪枝方法，利用上一步动作块作为几何先验，在视觉编码器之前决定保留/剪除patch，实现VLA推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在闭环控制中推理开销大，现有视觉token剪枝只能在视觉编码完成后基于embedding或attention估计冗余，无法提前节省编码器计算。

Method: 将剪枝决策前移至视觉编码器之前：利用上一控制步预测的动作块（已可用）给出相邻观测间的末端执行器与相机运动；对固定第三人称视角保留投影末端轨迹附近的patch及语义锚点，对腕部视角在补偿动作引发的相机运动后剪除未变化patch；全程无需训练。

Result: 在LIBERO与VLABench两个仿真环境及四个真机任务上，最高1.61×加速、59.5% FLOPs削减，成功率下降可忽略。

Conclusion: 以机器人自身动作作为几何先验的编码前剪枝是高效且低损的VLA推理加速方案，兼具仿真与真机验证。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models perform versatile manipulation tasks from language instructions, but their inference cost limits how fast a robot can respond in closed-loop control. Visual token pruning reduces this cost; however, prior methods estimate token redundancy from encoded visual embeddings or attention, only after the visual encoder has processed the entire observation. We propose \ours, a training-free token pruning method that moves the pruning decision ahead of the vision encoder by grounding it in a geometric prior derived from the robot's own action. The action chunk predicted at the previous control step is already available before the current observation is encoded, and it specifies the end-effector and camera motion between consecutive observations. The same action affects the two standard camera views asymmetrically: in the fixed third-person view, \ours retains patches along the projected end-effector trajectory together with semantic anchors, while in the wrist view, it prunes patches that remain unchanged after compensating for the action-induced camera motion. Extensive experiments on two simulation environments (LIBERO and VLABench) and four real-robot tasks demonstrate that \ours achieves up to 1.61 $\times$ speedup and 59.5\% FLOPs reduction with negligible degradation in success rates.

</details>


### [123] [STRIDE: Learning Humanoid Locomotion in Minutes with Acceleration-Based Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08857)
*Nico Bohlinger,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: STRIDE 用仅依赖机载 IMU 的"指令加速度跟踪"奖励替代基座速度跟踪，结合 off-policy XQC、高增益关节位置控制与左右对称增广，在 Unitree G1 与 Booster T1 真机上从数分钟样本学会行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接在真机上训练人形机器人可规避 sim-to-real 差距，但主流 RL 运动奖励依赖基座速度，而该量在无外部动捕或高精度状态估计器时无法机载获得。作者希望设计一种只需机载 IMU 即可计算的奖励，同时保持样本效率。

Method: 将奖励从"跟踪基座速度"改为"跟踪指令加速度剖面"（IMU 可测）；采用 off-policy 算法 XQC 提升样本效率；结合高增益关节位置控制设定与左右对称数据增广；在仿真中对 Unitree G1、Booster T1 训练并部署真机。

Result: 仿真中 14 分钟内学会行走；相比去掉高增益控制与对称增广的版本，训练期摔倒减少 81%，且后者需超过 1 小时才学会。真机部署展示了直接学习运动能力。

Conclusion: 去除对基座速度估计的依赖是人形机器人真机直接学习的可行路径；高增益位置控制与对称增广是该奖励下高效学习的关键组件。

Abstract: Learning locomotion directly on a humanoid robot is a promising direction to avoid the sim-to-real gap. However, locomotion rewards in Reinforcement Learning (RL) typically track the base velocity, which cannot be measured onboard without an external motion capture system or well-calibrated state estimator. This is why we introduce \method{}, which learns humanoid locomotion from minutes of samples with a reward that only requires the onboard Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU). Instead of tracking the base velocity, STRIDE rewards the robot for following a commanded acceleration profile. To learn efficiently from this reward, we use the off-policy RL algorithm XQC and combine a high gain setting for the joint position controllers with left-right symmetry augmentation. In simulation, STRIDE learns to walk on the Unitree G1 and Booster T1 humanoids within 14 minutes of samples. Compared to learning without these two components, it reduces the falls during training by 81%, while without them the robot learns only in over an hour.

</details>


### [124] [What Limits mmWave Human-State Sensing for Robot Perception?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08863)
*Shuntian Zheng,Guangming Wang,Hongyang He,Shuai He,Yu Guan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将人体与环境统一建模为时变散射场，给出联合定位的Cramér-Rao型误差下界（随带宽、孔径、SNR、融合帧数、相位相干性、可见回波），并把帧融合/多视角/回波选择/位姿估计作为检验该下界的匹配探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助与搜救场景中相机/LiDAR在烟雾、黑暗、隐私敏感环境下不可靠；毫米波雷达可替代，但紧凑阵列孔径小，几米处联合定位误差达分米级，危及安全裕度。现有管线逐项使用帧融合、多视角、回波选择或更强估计器，但未与统一测量极限关联，也未区分"信息增益"与"估计器补全"带来的收益。

Method: 以时变散射场统一建模人体与环境，推导依赖带宽、孔径、SNR、融合帧数、相位相干性及保留回波可见性的Cramér-Rao型定位误差下界；将现有帧融合、多视角、回波选择、位姿估计方法作为该下界的受控探针进行实验验证（三个数据集+实机部署）。

Result: 各策略使联合定位误差朝理论预测方向变化：加第二视角将平均逐关节位置误差从约97 mm降至65 mm；更强估计器主要通过对测量无法分辨的关节进行补全来降低整体误差，而非提升可测自由度精度。

Conclusion: 为机器人社区提供了在部署可靠毫米波人体感知时，判断应优先优化观测设计还是估计器容量的原理性依据。

Abstract: Assistive robots and search-and-rescue platforms require reliable human state sensing in smoke, darkness, and privacy-sensitive environments, where cameras and LiDAR may be unreliable Millimeter-wave radar offers an alternative that works without ambient light, penetrates smoke, and does not capture visual appearance. However, the compact arrays that fit robot size, power, and cost budgets have limited apertures, producing joint-localization errors on the order of decimeters at a few meters, large enough to compromise the safety margins and tracking accuracy a robot depends on. Existing pipelines reduce pose error through frame fusion, multiple viewpoints, return selection, or stronger estimators, but examine one technique at a time, without relating its effect to a shared measurement limit or separating gains from added information from gains from estimator completion. We address this gap with a framework that models the human and environment as one time-varying scattering field and states a Cramer-Rao type error floor for joint localization as a function of bandwidth, aperture, signal-to-noise ratio, fused frame count, phase coherence, and visibility of retained returns. We then use existing frame fusion, multi viewpoint, return selection, and pose estimation methods as matched probes of this floor rather than as competing pipelines. Under the evaluated conditions, experiments on three datasets and a live deployment show these strategies move joint localization error in the predicted directions, for example lowering mean per-joint position error from about 97 to 65 mm with a second viewpoint, while a stronger estimator mainly lowers overall error by completing joints the measurement cannot resolve. These results give the robotics community a principled basis for deciding whether to prioritize observation design or estimator capacity when deploying reliable mmWave human sensing.

</details>


### [125] [Lodos-v2: An Efficient Primal Formulation of the Exact Frictional Contact Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08868)
*Onur Beker,Andreas René Geist,Georg Martius*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Lodos-v2 是一个基于 GPU 的大规模并行刚体接触动力学仿真框架，通过外层近端最小化循环中顺序求解一串凸松弛来精确求解摩擦接触问题，并将接触组重参数化扩展到原始锥互补形式，从而支持关节限制、焊接等完整仿真器约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前代 Lodos-v1 存在两点局限：一是求解锥互补接触动力学的标准凸松弛，导致抬离（lift-off）、法向人工柔顺和切向蠕变等物理伪影；二是其接触组重参数化用每碰撞对单一因子缩放弹簧-阻尼力，无法与其他约束（关节限制、焊接）整合。目标是在大量精细网格碰撞场景下实现高吞吐且物理准确的仿真。

Method: 提出在原始（primal）形式下顺序求解级联凸松弛的算法，嵌入外层近端最小化（proximal minimization）循环以逼近精确摩擦接触解；并将 Lodos-v1 的接触组重参数化推广至锥互补接触动力学的原始表述，使其可与完整仿真器的其他约束联合求解。

Result: 实验表明相比已有刚体仿真框架实现了吞吐量提升，同时消除了凸松弛引入的物理伪影；源代码已公开。

Conclusion: Lodos-v2 以精确接触求解加可组合的重参数化方案，为大规模精细网格刚体仿真提供了高吞吐、物理正确且可扩展至完整约束体系的 GPU 仿真器，对物理仿真与机器人仿真数据生成具有实用价值。

Abstract: Lodos-v2 is a GPU-based simulation framework for massively parallel rigid-body dynamics with contact. It is specifically designed to achieve high throughput in scenes involving collisions among large numbers of detailed meshes. Its predecessor, Lodos-v1, had two main limitations. First, it solved the standard convex relaxation of cone-complementarity-based contact dynamics, thereby inheriting well-known physical artifacts, including lift-off and artificial compliance in the normal direction, as well as creep in the tangential direction. Second, its contact-group reparameterization scheme, which rescales spring-damper forces using a single factor per collision pair, could not be integrated with other constraints required by a full-fledged simulator, such as joint limits and welds. Lodos-v2 addresses both limitations through two main contributions. First, it introduces an efficient algorithm for solving the exact frictional contact problem by sequentially solving a cascade of convex relaxations in primal form within an outer proximal minimization loop. Second, it extends the contact-group reparameterization scheme of Lodos-v1 to the primal formulation of cone-complementarity-based contact dynamics, enabling its integration with the other constraints of a full-fledged simulator. Experiments demonstrate that these contributions result in throughput improvements over established rigid-body simulation frameworks. The source code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [126] [Lodos: A Lightweight Simulator Designed for Contact-Rich Interactions with Detailed Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08872)
*Onur Beker,Andreas René Geist,Georg Martius*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出从零设计的刚体仿真框架，针对 mesh-mesh 碰撞瓶颈重写 broadphase、narrowphase 与接触动力学例程，在保持物理可信度的同时将细致网格碰撞场景吞吐量提升约一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模并行刚体仿真（强化学习、随机最优控制、随机平滑轨迹优化）依赖球/胶囊/圆柱/盒等基元近似碰撞体；当表面复杂或精度要求高而必须使用 mesh-mesh 碰撞时，吞吐量骤降，成为现有仿真器的主要瓶颈。

Method: 从零构建仿真框架：重新设计 broadphase 与 narrowphase 碰撞检测算法，以及随后的接触动力学计算流程，围绕 mesh-mesh 碰撞的高吞吐目标进行整体优化（具体算法细节摘要未给出）。

Result: 实验表明与现有成熟仿真器在物理保真度上相当，并在其专长的细致 mesh-mesh 碰撞设置下取得约 10 倍吞吐提升；开源代码已发布。

Conclusion: 通过系统级重写碰撞检测与接触求解，可显著缓解 mesh-mesh 碰撞的吞吐瓶颈，为接触丰富场景下的并行机器人仿真提供可行替代方案。

Abstract: In recent years, massively parallelized rigid-body simulation has played a central role in robotics research, enabling rapid progress in areas such as reinforcement learning, stochastic optimal control, and randomized smoothing-based trajectory optimization. Particularly in contact-rich settings, the scalability of such simulations in large part depends on well-optimized representations of collision geometry, often requiring a coarse approximation of the underlying surfaces using primitives such as spheres, capsules, cylinders, and boxes. Whenever the complexity of the surfaces or the precision demands of the application render such approximations insufficient, it is instead necessary to employ mesh-mesh collisions, which impose a significant reduction in throughput. This paper proposes a simulation framework designed from scratch to address this bottleneck, with novel routines for broadphase and narrowphase collision detection as well as the subsequent contact dynamics computation. As experimentally validated, the framework matches existing well-established simulators in terms of physical faithfulness, and can achieve approximately an order of magnitude higher throughput in the detailed mesh-mesh collision setting that it specializes in. The source code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [127] [Localizing and Editing the Goal Representation of a Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08895)
*Sudhanshu Srivastava,Faisal Aqlan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文用闭环rollout、激活修补、线性探针、激活引导与运行时监控，研究OpenVLA是否遵循"不要做X"类禁令，以及它在何处表征所执行的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作员通常以禁令形式（如"不要把碗放在炉子上"）向机器人陈述安全约束，但VLA模型是否真正遵循否定指令、以及目标在模型内部如何表征尚不清楚，这直接关系到安全部署与可信监控。

Method: 利用LIBERO-Goal十个任务共享同一场景的特性，为每次rollout标注其实际执行的目标；结合闭环rollout、激活修补（activation patching）、线性探针、激活引导（fixed steering vector）以及运行时监控，逐层定位指令与目标信息的表征位置。

Result: 否定措辞对执行任务无可检测影响：被禁止任务在79%的否定rollout中被执行，普通任务完成率78%；目标探针在"not X"与"X"下读出相似。首个动作主要依赖第12层前的指令token，第16层起依赖最后一个prompt token；第8层指令token上的固定引导向量重定向了18次中的12次，而第12–20层的固定向量与prefill修补在158次中均未成功；自第16层起需修补每个解码动作token（第24层18次中15次成功）。rollout中指令变更时，第32层目标读出比失败检测器更能识别不合规。

Conclusion: 禁令应在指令之外强制执行，并监控目标读出与规范的一致性；对OpenVLA的目标编辑应在第12层之前生效或修补每个动作token。

Abstract: Operators can state safety constraints for robots as prohibitions such as "do not put the bowl on the stove". We study whether OpenVLA follows such prohibitions and where it represents the goal it executes. The 10 tasks of LIBEROGoal share one scene, so we can label the goal that each rollout executes. We combine closed-loop rollouts with activation patching, linear probes, activation steering and runtime monitors. On LIBERO-Goal, negation had no detectable effect on which task OpenVLA executed. The model executed the forbidden task in 79% of negated rollouts and completed 78% of plain tasks. A goal probe gives similar read-outs under "not X" and "X". Activation patching shows that the first action depends mostly on the instruction tokens before layer 12 and on the last prompt token from layer 16 onward. A fixed steering vector at the instruction tokens at layer 8 redirected 12 of 18 rollouts. Fixed vectors and prefill patches at layers 12 to 20 redirected none of 158. From layer 16, only patches at every decoded action token redirected the robot (15 of 18 rollouts at layer 24). When the instruction changes during a rollout, the layer-32 goal read-out detects non-compliance better than failure detectors, and an external probe on image and instruction features performs comparably. These results support enforcing prohibitions outside the instruction and monitoring goal read-outs against the specification. Goal edits in OpenVLA should act before layer 12 or patch every action token.

</details>


### [128] [FATHOM: World-Model-Guided Diffusion Action-Trajectory Selection for Sparse-Marker AUV Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08898)
*Xiaorui Wang,Zeyu Sha,Mingyang Yang,David Chen Wang,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FATHOM: a world-model-guided diffusion action-trajectory selection framework for relative tracking of AUVs with sparse active light markers under partial observation; generates multi-thruster trajectory candidates with a diffusion prior, predicts their consequences via learned latent dynamics, and selects via receding-horizon objective; validated on real AUVs in real time.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cooperative AUV relative tracking is hard due to nonlinear dynamics and degraded underwater vision. Active light markers are robust to appearance variation but yield deliberately sparse observations that are often incomplete or corrupted by occlusion, reflections and detection noise during dynamic motion, creating a partial-observation control problem.

Method: Multimodal task-supervised recurrent state-space model (RSSM-style) fusing structured marker observations, IMU, action history and temporal dynamics into a predictive tracking belief; a diffusion action prior generates multiple multi-thruster action trajectories; their future tracking consequences are rolled out in the learned latent world model; candidates are scored under an explicit receding-horizon objective and the best is executed, with the decision re-updated from onboard observations each step (MPC-style, no direct execution of the generated trajectory).

Result: Offline analyses verify the predictive belief, multi-step world-model prediction, diffusion trajectory generation and predictive selection. Real-AUV experiments (strong pose-based baselines and component ablations) show robust autonomous tracking under nominal and challenging motions with naturally occurring partial marker visibility, running real time onboard without motion-capture feedback.

Conclusion: Combining a learned world model with diffusion-based trajectory proposal and predictive selection is an effective recipe for control under sparse/partial observations; the framework transfers to real underwater vehicles without external tracking infrastructure.

Abstract: Reliable relative tracking is fundamental to cooperative autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs), yet remains challenging under nonlinear vehicle dynamics and degraded underwater visual conditions. Active light markers improve robustness to appearance variation, but provide deliberately sparse observations that can become incomplete or corrupted by occlusion, reflections, and detection noise during dynamic motion. To address the resulting partial-observation control problem, this paper proposes FATHOM, a world-model-guided diffusion action-trajectory selection framework for sparse-marker AUV tracking. FATHOM first integrates structured marker observations, inertial measurements, action history, and temporal dynamics into a predictive tracking belief using a multimodal task-supervised recurrent state-space model. A diffusion action prior then generates multiple expert-like multi-thruster trajectories, whose future tracking consequences are predicted through the learned latent dynamics. Rather than directly executing a generated trajectory, FATHOM selects the most favorable candidate under an explicit receding-horizon objective and continually updates the decision from onboard observations. Extensive offline analyses verify the learned predictive belief, multi-step world-model prediction, diffusion trajectory generation, and predictive selection. More importantly, comprehensive real-AUV experiments, including strong pose-based comparisons and component ablations, demonstrate robust autonomous tracking across nominal and challenging motions with naturally occurring partial marker visibility, while maintaining real-time onboard operation without motion-capture feedback.

</details>


### [129] [Polarization calibration of fisheye cameras using skylight in the wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08911)
*Shuo Wang,Shiqi Che,Dianze Dong,Wenxin Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出鱼眼镜头透射-偏振分析器联合标定方法，用8参数统一建模，仅需自然天空下短手持室外序列即可标定，AoLP误差<0.5°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天空偏振可为机器人导航提供方向线索，但鱼眼镜头效应与偏振分析器缺陷会破坏全视场偏振测量精度，现有标定需专门偏振设备或预定义天空偏振模型。

Method: 在已知几何标定前提下，利用视觉特征估计帧间旋转，并以跨帧方向偏振光一致性作为约束，将入射角相关的镜头透射率与分析器增益、角度偏移统一为8参数联合模型；仅用自然天空下的短手持室外序列离线优化（无需专用偏振标定装置与天空偏振先验模型）。

Result: 真实室外序列上加权跨帧AoLP平均绝对误差全视场<0.5°，80°–90°入射角区域误差降低73.4%；不重新拟合即迁移到独立序列总体MAE为0.466°；四足机器人平台上的初步太阳方位实验显示与可见太阳中心对齐改善。

Conclusion: 低成本、免专用设备的偏振相机标定方案可显著提升大入射角AoLP精度并具备跨序列泛化性，为偏振-惯性相对航向估计与机器人导航奠定基础；标定软件将开源。

Abstract: Skylight polarization provides directional cues for robotic navigation, but fisheye lens effects and analyzer imperfections can degrade polarization measurements across the field of view. We present a joint lens-analyzer calibration method that explicitly models incidence-dependent lens transmission together with analyzer gains and angular offsets in a unified eight-parameter formulation. Given known geometric calibration, rotations between camera frames can be estimated using visual features and cross-frame directional polarized light consistency can be used for polarization calibration. Therefore, our calibration approach requires only a short handheld outdoor sequence under natural skylight, without dedicated polarization calibration equipment or a predefined sky polarization model. On real-world outdoor sequences, the proposed method achieves a weighted cross-frame angle-of-linear-polarization (AoLP) mean absolute error below 0.5° across all incidence angles, with a 73.4% reduction in the 80°-90° incidence angle region, and generalizes to an independent sequence with an overall mean absolute error of 0.466° without refitting. Preliminary solar-azimuth experiments using a camera mounted on a quadruped robot further show improved alignment with the visible solar center, supporting the potential of calibrated AoLP measurements for relative-heading estimation and future polarization--inertial navigation. The calibration software tool will be released upon acceptance of this paper.

</details>


### [130] [HomeLoop: A Simulation Framework and Benchmark for Robot-IoT Coordination in Everyday Domestic Assistance and Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08914)
*Ramy ElMallah,Marc Bishara,Hyegang Kim,Yuxi Zhang,Chongyu Zhu,Chi-Guhn Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HomeLoop 是一个基于 Isaac Sim 的仿真框架与基准，融合机器人本体、联网 IoT 设备、可穿戴设备、模拟住户/访客、故障传感器与随时间演化的危险事件，用于评估 VLM 驱动家居助手在模糊告警场景下的推理与行动。含 20 个任务（5 个任务族），用带时序逻辑的运行时监控器对结果与过程动作打分，动作有预设代价。6 个 VLM 评测最佳仅 61.9%，普遍存在"过度反应"（违规干预:漏干预 > 2:1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 驱动的具身机器人在家居部署中需与既有摄像头/IoT/可穿戴设施协同，并处理同一告警对应多种成因（锅将起火、已着火、住户在烹饪、传感器故障）的歧义，现有基准缺乏此类多源、易故障、动态危险的家庭级评测环境。

Method: 基于 Isaac Sim 构建仿真框架，整合机器人本体、20 类联网设备、可穿戴设备、模拟住户与访客、故障传感器及随时间演化的危险；设计 5 家族共 20 个任务，同一告警可对应不同成因；采用运行时监控器以时序逻辑校验最终状态与过程动作，并为每个动作设定成本；评测 6 个开源与闭源 VLM。

Result: 最佳模型家族均衡得分 61.9%；跨模型呈现一致的过度反应模式，违规干预失败回合数超过漏做必需干预的两倍以上，模型常未先查明告警原因即关断炉灶；随附 2100 套可自动布置的家居布局、5 种机器人本体与 20 类联网设备。

Conclusion: HomeLoop 提供可扩展基础平台，面向机器人-IoT 协同、长期记忆、主动辅助与隐私-安全权衡研究，公开资源以支持后续工作。

Abstract: Recent progress in embodied robotics is bringing robots closer to deployment in homes, as vision-language models (VLMs) are demonstrating significant advances in reasoning, making VLM-driven robots promising home assistant agents. These agents will often be introduced into homes that already have cameras, Internet of Things (IoT) devices, and residents with wearables. To fulfill their potential, they need to integrate with existing smart infrastructure and reason through ambiguous scenarios: a smoke alarm may come from a pan about to ignite, a pan already on fire, a resident cooking or a faulty sensor, and each calls for a different response. To that end, we introduce HomeLoop, a simulation framework and benchmark built on Isaac Sim that combines robot bodies, networked devices and wearables, simulated residents and visitors, faulty sensors, and hazards that evolve over time. Our benchmark is composed of 20 tasks across five families grounded in real-world household hazards and needs, and designed so that the same alert can have different causes. A runtime monitor scores each episode with temporal-logic checks on the final outcome and the actions along the way, and every action carries a predefined cost. We evaluate six open-weight and proprietary VLMs. The best achieves a family-balanced score of 61.9%, and across models we see a pattern of over-reaction: failed episodes involving prohibited interventions outnumber those involving missed required interventions by more than two to one, with models often shutting off the stove before investigating the alarm's cause. HomeLoop is an extensible foundation intended for use in research on robot-IoT coordination, long-term memory, proactive assistance, and privacy-safety trade-offs. HomeLoop will be released with 2,100 home layouts that can be automatically furnished in diverse configurations, five robot bodies and 20 networked device types.

</details>


### [131] [PRISM: Primitive Routing via In-context Skill Mixing for Retentive VLA Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08917)
*Jiachen Tao,Gengyu Zhang,Haoxuan Wang,Bing Liu,Yan Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRISM 将可复用技能表示为参数级低秩原语（primitive bank），在组合任务推理时用 per-chunk、以 VLM 前缀表示为条件的路由器按阶段调用不同技能适配器；在 RoboCasa365 上原子技能 56.43%、长程组合 20.00%，优于 π0.5-LoRA 两阶段微调（17.86%/17.50%），并有 AgileX PIPER 真机验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 微调把每个新任务吸收进共享参数更新，既缺少参数层面承载可复用技能的"位置"，也缺少沿轨迹按时序调用各技能的机制，导致技能遗忘与长程组合能力不足。作者进一步发现该结构缺失是"潜在而非不存在"：单技能低秩更新天然占据可区分的低维子空间，组合任务 LoRA 约一半 Frobenius 能量落在该子空间，而随机旋转对照近乎为零。

Method: 基于上述子空间几何，提出 PRISM：(1) 技能库，每个技能族存为专用低秩 action-expert adapter；(2) 原语附加技能特定低秩残差，推理时以 per-chunk 的 VLM 条件路由器读取前缀表示来跟踪任务进度并组合调用；(3) 路由器由确定性、任务条件化、动作派生的 oracle 生成 per-chunk 标签进行监督；(4) 组合训练中更新视觉编码器、语言 LoRA、技能残差、路由器及共享动作投影与 time MLP，而各原语冻结，从构造上保留基础技能适配器。

Result: RoboCasa365 上原子技能成功率 56.43%、长程组合任务 20.00%；标准 π0.5-LoRA 两阶段微调为 17.86% 原子、17.50% 组合。另在 AgileX PIPER 真机平台完成验证。

Conclusion: 参数级技能原语 + 时序路由为 VLA 的技能保持与长程组合提供了结构化方案，且由低秩子空间分析提供了可解释依据；在仿真基准上组合成功率相对基线有明显提升，并具备真机迁移证据。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models promise a single language-conditioned policy adaptable to many manipulation behaviors, yet deployment additionally requires skill retention and long-horizon composition: a policy must retain previously acquired skills and reuse them inside composite tasks whose behavior changes across phases. The standard recipe absorbs every new task into one shared update, which provides neither a parameter-level locus for reusable skills nor a mechanism for invoking each skill along the trajectory. We observe that this missing structure is latent rather than absent: per-skill low-rank updates of a pretrained VLA occupy a robustly distinguishable, low-dimensional subspace, and a single composite-task LoRA concentrates roughly half of its Frobenius energy in this subspace, against a near-zero matched random-rotation control. Building on this geometry, we introduce PRISM, which represents reusable skills as parameter-level primitives: each skill family is stored as a dedicated low-rank action-expert adapter in a primitive bank, primitives are augmented with skill-specific low-rank residuals and composed at inference by a per-chunk VLM-conditioned router that reads the prefix representation to track task progress, and the router is supervised by per-chunk labels from a deterministic, task-conditioned, action-derived oracle. During composite training, the vision encoder, the language LoRA, the skill residuals, the router, and the shared action projections and time MLP are updated; every primitive is held frozen, so the base per-skill action-expert adapters are preserved by construction. On RoboCasa365, PRISM achieves 56.43% atomic-skill success and reaches 20.00% on long-horizon composite tasks, while a standard $\pi_{0.5}$-LoRA two-stage fine-tune reaches 17.86% atomic and 17.50% composite success. We additionally validate PRISM on an AgileX PIPER real-robot setup.

</details>


### [132] [HOC2R: Hand-Object Contact Trajectories for Human-to-Robot Motion Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08920)
*Hongkun Yu,Francisco M. López,Francisco Cruz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HOC2R 用冻结的 DINOv3 + SAM 2.1 特征加学习头提取人手-物体接触轨迹，支持"计数感知自动化"与"固定双接触"两种模式，作为伪标注器从人类视频中抽取机器人可用运动线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频含大量物体操作演示，但缺少机器人可跟随的运动线索；需要从单目人手视频中可靠抽取手-物接触轨迹并转成机器人可用伪标签。

Method: 冻结 DINOv3 与 SAM 2.1 特征 + 学习头做手-物关联与接触定位；两种模式：(1) count-aware 自动化，在未裁剪视频中检测/计数/定位可见接触；(2) fixed-two，已知接触帧时用于双指夹爪。30 任务点标注基准 + 7 个未见 RGB-D rollout + 6 条真机评测序列。

Result: 基准上接触计数准确率 58.1%→71.4%，平均定位距离 19.81→10.46 px（对比适配 VRB2）；未见 RGB-D 上 67.67→27.02 px；真机 6 序列中 30 任务 fixed-two 检查点得到 4 条可行轨迹、3 个成功任务（基线各为 2 条 / 1 个）。单只手场景有竞争力、对手部颜色漂移鲁棒、多手场景最强。

Conclusion: HOC2R 可作为实用的伪标注方法，从人类演示中提取机器人可用的接触轨迹；但真机成功率（3/6）与单只手优势有限，规模仍偏小。

Abstract: Human videos contain abundant object-manipulation demonstrations, but they do not directly provide motion cues that a robot can follow. We present HOC2R, a Hand-Object Contact trajectory extractor for Robot motion that combines frozen DINOv3 and SAM 2.1 features with learned heads for hand-object association and contact localization. HOC2R supports two modes: count-aware automation that detects, counts, and localizes visible contacts in untrimmed videos, and fixed-two localization for two-finger grippers when contact frames are known. On a 30-task point-annotated benchmark, count-aware automation improves contact-count accuracy from 58.1% to 71.4% and reduces mean localization distance from 19.81 to 10.46 pixels relative to an adapted VRB2 baseline. On seven unseen RGB-D rollouts, a five-task fixed-two checkpoint reduces mean localization distance from 67.67 to 27.02 pixels. Across six robot evaluation sequences, the 30-task fixed-two checkpoint produces four feasible trajectories and succeeds on three tasks, compared with two feasible trajectories and one successful task for each adapted baseline. HOC2R is competitive in the evaluated single-hand scenes, robust to the hand-color shift, and strongest in the evaluated multi-hand scenes. These results support HOC2R as a practical pseudo-labeling method for extracting robot-usable contact trajectories from human demonstrations.

</details>


### [133] [Wellness Robots and the Path to Full Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08930)
*Mohammad H. Mahoor,Matthew Hessler,Lombe Chileshe,Ola Alsaadi,Eshrat Emamian,Kerstin S. Haring,Richard M. Voyles*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"自主健康机器人"作为社交辅助机器人的独立类别，基于SAE J3016提出六级（L0–L5）Care Robot Autonomy Scale（CRAS）分类法；以Ryan机器人为案例，给出NIH资助纵向研究（N=18, 887小时）中认知改善显著（SLUMS +2.61, p=0.015）、抑郁症状方向性下降（PHQ-9 −2.50, p=0.073）且无新异性效应的证据，并将Ryan定位在L1–2，规划2030年代初达L5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交辅助机器人领域缺乏统一的自主性分级标准，难以界定"自主健康机器人"这一类别，也缺少可比较的能力评价框架与长期实证支撑。

Method: 类比汽车自动化标准SAE J3016，构建CRAS六级（L0–L5）分类体系，沿四个与健康维度对齐的维度评估；围绕ICAA七维健康模型定义自主健康机器人；以DreamFace Technologies商业化的Ryan机器人为案例，结合NIH/NIA资助的纵向研究（N=18，887小时）进行干预效果评估。

Result: SLUMS +2.61（p=0.015）达统计显著；PHQ-9 −2.50（p=0.073）仅为方向性趋势，未达显著；未见新异性效应。Ryan当前处于CRAS L1–2，部分维度具备初现的L3能力；提出三阶段路线图，目标2030年代初实现L5完全自主。

Conclusion: CRAS提供了可操作的自主性分级框架，长期纵向证据初步支持自主健康机器人在认知维度的收益；但样本量小、抑郁效果未显著，L5仍属愿景，需更大规模与更长周期验证。

Abstract: This paper introduces the autonomous wellness robot as a distinct category of socially assistive system, defined by its capacity to continuously assess and respond across the seven ICAA dimensions of wellness (see Figure 2). We propose the Care Robot Autonomy Scale (CRAS): a six-level taxonomy (L0-L5) modeled on the automotive automation standard SAE J3016 and evaluated across four wellness-aligned dimensions. We present Ryan, developed initially at the University of Denver and commercialized by DreamFace Technologies, as a case study. NIH/NIA-funded longitudinal evidence (N = 18; 887 hours) shows statistically significant cognitive improvement (SLUMS +2.61, p = 0.015) and a clinically meaningful directional reduction in depressive symptoms (PHQ-9 -2.50, p = 0.073), with no novelty effect. We position Ryan today at CRAS Level 1-2, with emerging Level 3 capabilities in some dimensions, and outline a three-phase roadmap to L5 (full autonomy) by the early 2030s.

</details>


### [134] [RevoSim: Scalable Multimodal Tactile Simulation for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08939)
*Ke Zhang,Lixin Xu,Ziyi Wang,Xiyue Dong,Jie Tan,Lianghu Shao,Xianpeng Wang,Chuanyu Li,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RevoSim：面向 BrainCo Revo3 灵巧手的可扩展高保真多模态触觉仿真器，联合建模 285 个压阻触觉单元与 5 个视触觉指尖，同步生成压力、深度、RGB、标记位移数据，单卡 RTX 5090 支持至多 4096 并行环境，并借此纯仿真训练抓取与灵巧操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作的触觉策略学习受限于真实数据采集成本；现有触觉仿真器多只覆盖单一模态（压力或视觉），缺乏对真实灵巧手传感布局（压阻阵列 + 视触觉指尖）的高保真、可大规模并行的联合建模，难以支撑大规模强化学习。

Method: 构建多模态触觉仿真器：285 点压阻 taxel 力学响应建模 + 5 个视触觉指尖（深度条件化的 marker 位移、RGB、接触深度）渲染；四类观测（压力、深度、RGB、marker 位移）时间同步；GPU 批量化渲染支持数千并行环境；在该平台内以强化学习/仿真数据训练抓取与手内操作策略。

Result: 在 RTX 5090 上，深度条件化 marker 位移配置可支持 4096 个并行环境，具备良好扩展性；成功在纯仿真中训练出手内操作与抓取策略，验证仿真管线可支撑大规模触觉策略学习。

Conclusion: RevoSim 为灵巧手多模态触觉提供可扩展仿真基座，并为后续 sim-to-real 迁移奠定基础；当前阶段以仿真为主，尚缺真机验证与开源信息。

Abstract: We present RevoSim, a scalable and high-fidelity multimodal tactile simulator for the BrainCo Revo3 hand that jointly models 285 piezoresistive taxels and five visuotactile fingertips. RevoSim efficiently generates synchronized pressure, depth, RGB, and marker-displacement observations; on a single RTX 5090, its depth-conditioned marker-displacement configuration supports up to 4,096 parallel environments. We train in-hand manipulation and grasping policies entirely in simulation, establishing a foundation for future sim-to-real transfer. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [135] [VLA Handover: Failure-Prediction-Based Switching from On-Robot to Server VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08987)
*Takayuki Nishio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"VLA handover"框架：机器人本地运行小VLA（S-VLA），仅当预测其可能失败时切换到大VLA（L-VLA）远端推理，将切换问题形式化为在给定成功率约束下最小化请求代价，并用不需重训练两类VLA的启发式方法求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大VLA泛化好但难以在机器人上实时运行；小VLA可本地实时但失败率高。远程（B5G/6G）推理可让机器人用大VLA，但每个动作块都请求服务器会严重限制单服务器可服务的机器人数量，因此需要按需调用。

Method: 把S-VLA与L-VLA的切换建模为在满足要求的成功概率前提下最小化远端请求代价的优化问题；由于该问题无法直接求解，提出启发式框架（含S-VLA失败可能性估计与切换决策），且不重训练任何一个VLA。仿真中使用SmolVLA与π0.5，在LIBERO四个套件中的三个上评测，并与纯S-VLA、仅服务器控制两种基线对比，考察不同网络时延下的表现。

Result: 相较纯S-VLA，成功率提升11–22个百分点；相较服务器端全量推理，请求数减少49–77%，成功率仅低2–4个百分点；完成时间随网络时延增长更平缓。

Conclusion: 通过按需将小模型交由大模型"接管"，可在不重训练的前提下同时获得接近大VLA的成功率与远低于全远端推理的通信开销，为多机器人共享服务器算力的部署提供了可行思路；但证据仅限LIBERO仿真，缺少真机验证与时延之外的系统级评估。

Abstract: Large vision-language-action (VLA) models generalize well but are difficult to run in real time on a robot, and small VLAs that run there fail more often. Remote inference over beyond-5G and 6G networks lets a robot use a large VLA on a server, but querying the server for every action chunk limits the number of robots per server. We propose VLA handover: the robot runs a small VLA (S-VLA) and switches to a large VLA (L-VLA) on a server only when the S-VLA is likely to fail. We formulate the switching as minimizing the request cost under a required success probability and, since this problem cannot be solved directly, propose a heuristic framework that retrains neither VLA. In simulation with SmolVLA and $\pi_{0.5}$ on three of the four LIBERO suites, VLA handover raised the success rate by 11-22 percentage points over the S-VLA alone and sent 49-77% fewer requests than server-only control at a 2-4 percentage-point lower success rate, and its completion time grew less with the network latency.

</details>


### [136] [FASTFUNNELS: Agile Multi Robot Navigation in Narrow Spaces via Adaptive Dynamic Funnels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09005)
*Vignesh Rajagopal,Rohan Chandra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FastFunnels: 用强化学习生成动态椭圆"液滴"包络 + 分布式MPC保域跟踪，实现多辆Ackermann车在狭窄杂乱空间高速编队通行，7.73 m/s下89%完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速多机器人穿越狭窄瓶颈时，安全与敏捷难以兼得：减速防碰撞或高速来不及转向；传统编队控制依赖刚性预设形状与解析切换（方阵→线阵→方阵），高度依赖初始条件、环境与车辆动力学，高速下难以维持。

Method: 仿水滴自适应形状的层次化框架。高层：RL策略实时输出可形变（挤压、拉伸、转向）的斑块状椭圆patched region；低层：分布式DMPC将各车约束在该patched软约束内并互相避碰。

Result: 在14个场景、N=1~6的F1TENTH仿真赛车上各20次试验：7.73 m/s平均速度下成功率89%；所有基线要么无法完成赛道，要么仅能在0.07–1.79 m/s低速下以约40%成功率完成。

Conclusion: 将形状自适应交由学习策略、跟踪交由分布式MPC，可在不减速前提下兼顾安全与敏捷，优于刚性编队法；验证限于仿真，未含实车实验。

Abstract: We focus on the problem of high-speed multi-robot navigation in cluttered spaces with tight spatial constraints. A key challenge is balancing safety with agility, as existing methods often trade one for the other (slowing down near a bottleneck to avoid collisions versus failing to swerve in time at high speeds), especially for car-like systems. Our approach belongs to the family of formation control methods, in which Ackermann-steering agents coordinate while navigating tight spaces. However, current formation control techniques rely on rigid, pre-defined shapes and transitions that depend on initial conditions, the environment, and vehicle dynamics, and are impractical to maintain at high speeds. For example, agents passing through a small gap must first form a square, transform into a line using analytical equations, and then return to a square. In contrast, our approach is inspired by a droplet of water that continuously adapts its shape to the space it flows through without slowing down. We propose a hierarchical algorithm in which, at the high level, a reinforcement learning policy produces a dynamic, blob-like elliptical patch that morphs its shape in real time, squeezing, stretching, and orienting itself around obstacles and through tight spaces. At the low level, the robots are controlled via distributed model predictive control (DMPC) to stay inside the patch, which acts as a soft forcing function, while avoiding collisions with each other. We evaluate our approach, FastFunnels, on simulated F1TENTH racing cars across 14 scenarios with N=1 to N=6 robots, averaged over 20 trials each. FastFunnels achieves an 89% completion rate at 7.73 m/s, while all baselines either fail to complete the track or succeed only at very low speeds (0.07-1.79 m/s, about 40% completion rate).

</details>


### [137] [A Controlled Study of Models, Skills, Feedback, and Curriculum in Agentic Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09030)
*Kaiyuan Chen,Xinyue Zhu,Shuangyu Xie,Uksang Yoo,Thi Thu Phuong Luong,Marcus Gualtieri,Christian Juette,Liu Ren,Spencer Huang,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过九项操作任务、631种配置、2.8万次编码智能体 rollout 的受控仿真研究，系统分析 agentic robotics 自学习闭环的瓶颈：关键不在信息获取不足，而在于把信息转化为正确的策略更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探明编码智能体自主实现并迭代改进机器人策略时的失效来源，厘清模型能力、技能先验、反馈结构与课程设计等因素的作用。

Method: 单一大规模受控仿真消融实验：9个操作任务×631种配置×28K次编码智能体 rollout，变量包括模型能力、技能先验、反馈结构与课程安排。

Result: 自学习闭环的成功主要取决于智能体能否有效归因失败、推理任务相关的空间与几何结构、并有选择地重构代码以改进策略；信息量本身并非主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: Agentic robotics 的改进方向应聚焦失败归因与代码重构机制，而非单纯提升信息/反馈供给。

Abstract: Agentic robotics asks coding agents to implement and iteratively improve robot policies from simulated and real-world physical interactions. We study the self-learning loop of agentic robotics through a controlled simulation study spanning nine manipulation tasks, 631 different experiment configurations, and 28K coding agent rollouts, systematically varying model capability, skill priors, feedback structure, and curriculum. The experiment data suggests that the bottleneck is often not the access to sufficient information, but interpreting it into the right policy update. Across these factors, a successful self-learning loop depends on how effectively agents attribute failures, reason task-relevant spatial and geometric structure, and selectively restructure code to improve policies.

</details>


### [138] [ToolDex: An Ego-Exo Human Demonstration Dataset for Creative Physical Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09036)
*Mohamed Elmoghany,Kehan Li,Ruoqu Chen,Tarun Chiruvolu,Shutong Zhang,Ryan Wang,Stephen Tian,Yaru Niu,Michelle Guo,Ding Zhao,Weiyu Liu,Mengdi Xu,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ToolDex：大规模自我中心/外中心多视角人类创造性工具使用演示数据集，含手、工具、目标物体及可变形交互标注，并建立工具使用推理、稀疏视角3D/4D重建、机器人策略学习三大基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创造性工具使用（选择、改造、复用日常物体作为工具）是具身智能的关键能力，但现有数据缺乏真实世界、大规模、高保真、多样的工具使用行为，基准多为仿真或简化交互，缺少细致观测与标注。

Method: 构建ToolDex数据集：同步1个自我中心相机与8个外中心相机采集高保真多视角人类演示，覆盖工具选择/改造/复用场景；提供手部、工具、目标物体及可变形交互的丰富标注；并设计包含物理工具使用推理、稀疏视角3D与4D重建、机器人策略学习的三类基准任务。

Result: 摘要仅给出数据规模、多视角配置与标注类型，以及三大基准任务设定，未给出具体量化结果（可视化与基准结果通过项目页面提供）。

Conclusion: ToolDex为研究人类工具使用行为及构建可自适应使用工具的具身智能体提供了真实世界数据基础，填补了高保真ego-exo工具使用数据的空白。

Abstract: Creative tool use is a fundamental human capability and an important step toward general-purpose embodied intelligence. It requires agents to reason about object affordances, physical constraints, and manipulation strategies, especially when standard tools are unavailable. However, progress in this area is limited by the lack of real-world datasets that capture human tool-use behavior with sufficient scale, fidelity, and diversity. Existing benchmarks are often simulation-based, focus on simplified interactions, or lack detailed observations and annotations. We introduce ToolDex, a large-scale egocentric-exocentric (ego-exo) human demonstration dataset for creative physical tool use. ToolDex captures scenarios in which humans select, adapt, and repurpose available objects as tools. Using synchronized egocentric cameras and eight exocentric cameras, the dataset provides high-fidelity multi-view observations and rich annotations for hands, tools, target objects, and their deformable interactions. Beyond data collection, we establish a benchmark suite characterizing three core capabilities: physical tool-use reasoning, sparse-view 3D and 4D reconstruction, and robot policy learning. ToolDex provides a foundation for studying human tool-use behavior and developing embodied agents that can adaptively use tools in complex real-world environments. Dataset visualizations and benchmarking results are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [139] [DobotTrack: Target-Grounded Vision-Language-Action Learning for Embodied Person Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09041)
*Shengjun Deng,Yinghan Zhang,Chengrui Wei,Liu Liang,Songyuan Liu,Pengkun Zheng,Yajun Zheng,Ying Zhang,Yuan Tian,Xi Li,Teng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DobotTrack：0.8B 参数的目标锚定 VLA 框架用于语言引导的具身人员跟踪，通过系统比较五种辅助 grounding 任务（人员分割、框 grounding、视觉-空间分类、DINO 特征预测、未来帧预测），并引入 Q-identity 目标条件历史视觉压缩模块，在 EVT-Bench 上取得 77.9% 平均成功率，超 OmTrackVLA 16.9 个百分点；同时用四足机器人采集 126 条轨迹、59.6 万帧掩码标注的真实数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导的具身人员跟踪需要在自中心视角下从指代描述中识别目标并持续跟随，难点在于外观歧义、遮挡和长期历史表征效率；现有 VLA 缺乏显式目标 grounding 监督，且历史帧存储冗余。

Method: 在 VLA 动作策略上并联辅助 grounding 监督：掩码分割 + 边界框 grounding 双路监督，并系统消融五类辅助任务；提出 Q-identity 模块，利用 grounding 预测作为线索在历史 token 中压缩保留身份可区分的外观与空间信息；数据侧以掩码为源自动导出框与视觉-空间标签（水平位置、表观尺度），免去额外人工标注。

Result: EVT-Bench 三任务平均成功率 77.9%（+16.9pp vs OmTrackVLA，仅 0.8B 参数）；构建含 126 episodes、596,323 掩码标注帧的四足机器人真实跟踪数据集，验证掩码驱动的自动标注流水线。

Conclusion: 辅助 grounding 监督与目标条件历史压缩可显著提升具身跟踪策略的性能与参数效率，掩码中心标注流水线可在仿真与真实数据间复用，支持目标锚定跟踪策略的可扩展学习。

Abstract: Language-guided embodied person tracking requires a mobile agent to identify a target from a referring description and consistently follow the target under egocentric observations. We present DobotTrack, a target-grounded vision-language-action (VLA) framework that integrates auxiliary grounding supervision with efficient temporal modeling. We systematically investigate five auxiliary tasks---person segmentation, bounding-box grounding, visual-spatial classification (VSC), future DINO feature prediction, and future-frame prediction---and adopt complementary mask and bounding-box supervision to enhance target delineation and localization. We introduce Q-identity, a target-conditioned historical visual compression module that leverages target-grounding predictions to selectively preserve identity-discriminative appearance features and spatial cues in compact historical tokens. On EVT-Bench, our 0.8B-parameter model achieves an average success rate of 77.9%, exceeding OmTrackVLA by 16.9 percentage points across the three tasks. We further construct a real-world person-tracking dataset collected using a quadruped robot, comprising 126 episodes and 596,323 mask-annotated frames.
