The target masks enable automatic derivation of bounding boxes and visual-spatial labels encoding horizontal position and apparent image scale, providing additional grounding supervision without separate manual annotation of these labels. Together, our framework and annotation pipeline support learning target-grounded tracking policies from simulated and real-world data. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [140] [When to Trust the BIM: Identifiability-Aware LiDAR-Inertial--BIM Fusion under As-Built Deviations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09045)
*Yujie Zhang,Yuxuan Guo,Xinbao Sun,Chunlei Zhao,Langqi Miao,Xiaohui Jia,Jinyue Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将BIM辅助LiDAR-惯性里程计中“扫描与BIM不一致”的归因问题形式化为三类故障识别问题，推导出归因可行性的几何判据，并提出可识别性感知的方法：仅在可归因时给出归因，否则保留候选、暂缓BIM更新与模糊方向位姿修正，待新观测恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BIM可作为LiDAR-惯性里程计（LIO）定位的全局几何参考，但建成模型与设计模型存在偏差，导致扫描与BIM的不一致可能来自位姿误差、墙面偏移（建成偏差或未建模遮挡物）或房间整体位移，且在某些几何下这些解释产生完全相同的观测，直接据此更新BIM或修正位姿会引入错误。

Method: 将归因问题形式化为三类故障的故障识别（fault identification）问题，推导出归因在几何上何时可能的判据；在此基础上提出可识别性感知（identifiability-aware）方法，仅在故障可归因时输出归因，否则保留候选假设，在歧义方向上延迟BIM更新与基于BIM的位姿修正，并在获得新参考信息后恢复。采用预注册的二维研究，并在实验室房间（含IFC模型）与SLABIM数据集三层进行成对故障注入实验。

Result: 预注册二维研究中，在故障可检测但不可归因的案例上，条件错误率0.83%，而采用不确定性弃权的基线在相同回答率为31.67%；在5,600个可归因案例中，5,574次给出唯一归因且无误归因。实验室IFC房间成对故障注入未产生错位归因（168个条件中0次），并成功归因全部8个预测可归因故障；SLABIM 1,302个条件中0次错位归因；两个复现基线在所有设置中均出现误报的结构性标记。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模归因的可识别性并以“延迟而非误判”为原则处理歧义，可在BIM-激光定位中显著降低错误归因而几乎不损失可用性，为建成偏差下的BIM更新与位姿校正提供了有原则的决策框架。

Abstract: Building information models (BIM) can provide a global geometric reference for robot localization with LiDAR-inertial odometry (LIO). However, as-built deviations from the as-designed model mean that a disagreement between a scan and the BIM may arise from a pose error, an offset wall surface (an as-built deviation or an unmodeled object in front of the wall), or a shifted room, and in some geometries these explanations produce identical observations. In this paper, we formalize this attribution problem as fault identification over three fault classes and derive a geometric criterion for when attribution is possible. Building on it, we propose an identifiability-aware method that reports an attribution only when the fault is attributable; otherwise, it retains the candidates, defers BIM updates and BIM-based pose corrections in the ambiguous directions, and recovers once new references arrive. In the pre-registered two-dimensional study, on cases whose fault is detectable but not attributable, the method's conditional error rate was 0.83%, against 31.67% for a baseline with uncertainty-based abstention at the same answer rate. On the 5,600 attributable cases, the method reported a unique attribution 5,574 times, with no incorrect attributions. In paired fault injections in a laboratory room with its IFC model and on three floors of the SLABIM dataset, it raised no off-target attribution (0 of 168 conditions in the laboratory; 0 of 1,302 on SLABIM) and attributed all eight laboratory faults predicted to be attributable, whereas both reproduced baselines raised off-target structural flags in every setting.

</details>


### [141] [Robust Readout of Cross-Point Resistive Arrays via Time-Domain Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09059)
*Taro Tako,Hiroshi Kaminaga,Taisuke Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出时域优化读出方法，用整段波形拟合时域观测模型来估计交叉点阻性阵列的电阻值，在噪声与长互连条件下比传统频域幅值法更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交叉点阻性阵列可降低高密度触觉传感的布线复杂度，但传统基于频域的读出方法在机器人环境中因长互连、测量噪声与环境噪声而失准，需要更鲁棒的读出方案。

Method: 不局限于频谱幅值，而是对整个观测区间内测得的波形拟合时域观测模型，通过时域优化反解电阻值，利用完整波形信息提升抗噪性。

Result: 16×16 阵列仿真中平均读出误差较基线降低 20%；1000 次试验中平均误差与标准差分别降低 24.6% 和 77.6%；在模拟触觉响应的电阻分布下亦验证了改进。

Conclusion: 时域全波形拟合可显著提升阻性阵列读出的精度与稳定性，适用于高密度触觉传感的低成本读出。

Abstract: Cross-point resistive arrays are attractive for high-density tactile sensing because they reduce wiring complexity. However, conventional frequency-based readout methods can become inaccurate in robotic sensing environments, where long interconnects and measurement and environmental noise degrade the observed signals. We propose a time-domain optimization method for robust readout of cross-point resistive arrays. The method determines resistance values by fitting a time-domain observation model to the measured waveform over the interval, rather than relying solely on spectral amplitudes. By exploiting the full waveform, the proposed method improves robustness under noisy observation conditions. In simulations of a 16 x 16 array, the proposed method reduced the mean readout error by 20% compared with the baseline method. Furthermore, validation over 1,000 trials showed that the proposed method achieves more robust estimation than the conventional method, reducing the mean error and standard deviation by 24.6% and 77.6%, respectively. The improvement was also confirmed in cross-point array simulations with resistance distributions emulating tactile sensor responses.

</details>


### [142] [Safe Circumnavigation on Heterogeneous Orbits: A Range-Only HOCBF-QP Framework for Nonholonomic Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09112)
*Shubham Singh,Anand Singh,Anjali Tiwari,Lara Briñón-Arranz,Katsuki Fujisawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种安全关键的多机器人环绕静止目标控制框架，各机器人仅测目标距离并与左右两个环形邻居通信，通过加速度精确到达各自轨道半径、用转向率均衡队形，并用高阶控制障碍函数QP同时约束目标、围栏与机器人间安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人以不同轨道半径环绕静止目标时，需同时满足各自保持距离、与邻居均匀分布、且时刻避免与目标和彼此碰撞；现有最接近的多轨道设计即便拥有全状态信息也会违反目标保持距离与机器人间安全裕度。

Method: 每个机器人同时控制加速度与转向率，仅测量到目标的距离，仅与两个环形邻居交换信息。利用模型的结构性质，使同一控制律适用于任意指定半径；加速度把半径驱至目标值、转向率平衡队形；采用高阶控制障碍函数（HOCBF）二次规划，在两类输入上同时施加目标、地理围栏与机器人间安全约束，在远离约束处保持名义控制器不变，并在未测量的距离变化率仍处于估计过程中时依旧有效。

Result: 仿真验证了安全性等保证；对比中，现有最接近的多轨道方法即使使用全状态信息也违反目标保持距离与机器人间安全裕度，而所提控制器不违反。

Conclusion: 该工作以轻量感知与通信（单距离测量+双邻居）实现了多半径环绕的安全关键控制，HOCBF-QP框架兼顾安全性与名义性能，可作为分布式环绕编队的可扩展安全控制方案。

Abstract: Circumnavigating a stationary target with a team of robots requires each robot to hold its own standoff distance, to stay evenly spaced from its neighbours, and to avoid collisions with the target and with each other at every instant. The task becomes arduous when the robots are assigned different orbital radii, as their sensors or roles may demand. This paper presents a safety-critical control framework for this problem. Each robot commands both its acceleration and its turn-rate, measures only its distance to the target, and exchanges information only with its two ring neighbours. The design has three features. First, a structural property of the model lets one control law serve every robot regardless of its assigned radius. Second, the acceleration drives each radius exactly to its target, while the turn-rate balances the formation with the neighbours. Third, a higher-order control-barrier-function quadratic program enforces target, geofence, and inter-robot safety on both inputs at once; it leaves the nominal controller untouched away from the constraints and stays valid while the unmeasured range rate is still being estimated. Simulations confirm these guarantees. In a comparison, the closest existing multi-orbit design, even with full state information, violates both the target standoff and the inter-robot margin, whereas the proposed controller does not.

</details>


### [143] [Toward the Era of Agentic Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09124)
*Chao Liang,Ke Chen,Fengxiaoxiao Li,Shibo Huang,Yufeng Sun,Zhaokai Yin,Jianhuan Lai,Haozhe Feng,Chenghuan Jia,Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: A2-MEAS：以"代码"为扩展接口的具身多智能体框架，用可学习的修正码（语言修正+空间证据+执行历史+动作提议）在训练时蒸馏进策略权重、部署时以冻结权重在线修正；配套 A2-WLA（运动感知历史+潜世界模型的 VLA 策略）。BEHAVIOR 2026 全公开集 Q-score 0.6411、任务成功率 45.4%；RoboTwin 2.0 95.05%；12 项真机家务任务 57.92% vs pi0.5 26.67%；长程手冲咖啡失败恢复从 14%/8% 提升至 94%/82%；训练吞吐 427.1 samples/s（3.05x 加速）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型驱动的机器人策略需要持续自主性，但面临两个瓶颈：高效控制（推理/训练成本）与失败恢复（长程任务中的误差累积）。作者希望在不大幅改动策略结构的前提下，以低成本、可在线更新的方式利用外部多智能体的纠错知识与执行历史来提升鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 A2-MEAS 具身多智能体框架，核心是"code as a scaling interface"：把修正信息编码为可学习的 correction code（融合纠正语言、空间证据、执行历史、动作提议），由 MetaBrain 在训练期提供监督与运行期指导；训练期用 correction encoder + adapter 将知识吸收进策略权重，部署期用新生成的 code 在冻结权重上做行为适配。动作模型 A2-WLA 在 VLA 策略上引入运动感知历史与潜世界模型，配合动作预训练与任务专门化。附训练基础设施支持高吞吐训练。

Result: BEHAVIOR 2026 公开 50 任务子集在线修正前即达 0.5755 Q-score（为 2025 冠军所报 0.2605 的 2.21 倍，注意基准版本与训练配置不同）；全部 100 公开任务 0.6411 Q-score、45.40% 成功率。RoboTwin 2.0 上 A2-WLA 平均成功率 93.98%，完整系统 95.05%。5 类家庭场景 12 项真机任务中 A2-WLA 57.92% vs pi0.5 26.67%。长程手冲咖啡的两个滤纸操作步骤，失败后完成率由 14%→94%、8%→82%。训练吞吐 427.1 samples/s，为 pi0.5 参考实现同硬件下的 4.03 倍。

Conclusion: 以修正码为中心、训练蒸馏+部署冻结的轻量设计，在仿真基准、真机多场景与长程恢复任务上均取得显著提升，且训练效率高；作者将开源系统、模型、训练脚本与基础设施。

Abstract: Foundation models increasingly guide robot actions, yet sustained autonomy requires efficient control and failure recovery. We introduce A2-MEAS, an embodied multi-agent framework built around code as a scaling interface. Learned correction codes combine corrective language, spatial evidence, execution history, and action proposals. MetaBrain provides training supervision and runtime guidance. A correction encoder and adapter absorb this knowledge into policy weights during training, while fresh codes adapt behavior with frozen weights at deployment. The action model, A2-WLA, adds motion-aware history and a latent world model to a vision-language-action policy. Surprisingly, this simple design with action pretraining and task specialization reaches 0.5755 Q-score on the 50-task BEHAVIOR 2026 public subset before online correction, 2.21x the 2025 winner's reported 0.2605 across different benchmark editions and training configurations. On all 100 BEHAVIOR 2026 public tasks, A2-MEAS achieves 0.6411 Q-score and 45.40% task success. On RoboTwin 2.0, A2-WLA achieves 93.98% average success, rising to 95.05% with the complete system. Across 12 real-robot tasks in five household settings, A2-WLA achieves 57.92% average success versus 26.67% for pi0.5. In long-horizon pour-over coffee preparation, a history-conditioned adapter raises post-failure completion at two filter-handling steps from 14% to 94% and from 8% to 82%, compared with A2-WLA retries. Our training infrastructure delivers 427.1 samples per second, 4.03x the throughput of a pi0.5 reference implementation on matched hardware. We will release the system, models, training scripts, and infrastructure to support efficient embodied learning.

</details>


### [144] [Auditing Grasp Feasibility Predictors with Planner-Verified Counterfactuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09130)
*Sajid Ansari,Antony Thomas,Girish Varma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"规划器验证的反事实（CF）测试"：固定抓取位姿，在接近走廊中插入实体障碍并把其点云加入输入，由规划器重新给出标签（可行→不可行），据此检验抓取可行性预测器是否真正使用了规划器所检查的碰撞几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Task and Motion Planning基准上，抓取可行性预测器报告近乎完美的准确率（自然AUROC 0.920-0.996），但高基准准确率并不能证明预测器使用了运动规划器实际检查的碰撞几何，可能存在捷径学习或几何盲区。

Method: 反事实测试协议：以抓取位姿为不变量，向接近走廊注入实体障碍物点，标签由规划器重新推导从而发生翻转；在8个预测器（点云、体素、深度图、物体token四类表示）上评估CF配对AUROC，并跨4个规划器、2种机械臂、真实物体障碍验证；另提出走廊点云的clearance-aware读出模块，仅在未修改的基准上训练。

Result: CF配对AUROC从自然AUROC的0.920-0.996骤降至随机水平（0.470-0.504）；翻转结论在四个规划器、两种机械臂及真实物体障碍下均成立；失败分辨率各异，从包围盒到12.5 cm体素。在点云模型中，障碍要么使表征沿与"是否阻挡抓取"无关的方向移动，要么完全未被编码。clearance-aware读出达到0.983 CF配对AUROC，但仍绑定于其训练时的障碍放置位置。

Conclusion: 现有抓取可行性预测器在基准准确率上存在严重的高估，实际缺乏对碰撞几何的敏感性；构造对障碍放置不变的clearance表征仍是开放问题。作者公开反事实标签、指标、datasheet与重标注流水线。

Abstract: Grasp-feasibility predictors report near-perfect accuracy on Task and Motion Planning benchmarks, but high accuracy on these benchmarks does not verify that a predictor uses the collision geometry that the motion planner checks. We propose a planner-verified counterfactual (CF) test: holding the grasp pose fixed, we insert a solid obstacle into the approach corridor, add its points to the model input, and let a planner re-derive the label, which flips from feasible to infeasible. Across eight predictors spanning point clouds, voxels, depth, and object tokens, CF-pair AUROC falls from high natural AUROC (0.920-0.996) to chance (0.470-0.504), and the flip holds across four planners, two arms, and a real-object obstacle. Predictors fail at different resolutions, from a bounding box to a 12.5 cm voxel. In the point-cloud models we dissect, the obstacle either moves the representation in a direction unrelated to whether it blocks the grasp or is not registered at all. A clearance-aware read of the corridor points, trained on the unmodified benchmark with no counterfactual scenes, reaches 0.983 CF-pair AUROC, but it remains bound to its trained obstacle placements; a placement-invariant clearance representation is still open. We release the counterfactual labels, metrics, datasheet, and relabeling pipeline at this https URL .

</details>


### [145] [TPS-A*: Path Planning for Any-Shape Vehicles with Certified Collision Checking in Trajectory Parameter Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09210)
*Jose Luis Blanco-Claraco,Shravan S. Rai,Fernando Cañadas-Aránega,José Luis Torres-Moreno,José Carlos Moreno-Úbeda*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TPS-A*：以参数化轨迹生成器产生运动学可行边，构建“认证”碰撞检测框架，用按足迹预计算的网格一次性给出每条候选轨迹的认证自由距离，实现与足迹复杂度、候选轨迹数无关的逐节点碰撞代价，并在真实校园场景中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局地面车辆规划器通常用圆盘近似车体，以保守性换安全；或用精确足迹对每个候选运动、位姿与足迹边逐一检测，代价高。希望兼顾精确足迹的安全性与计算效率。

Method: 基于格状（lattice）规划器 TPS-A*：边为参数化轨迹生成器的运动学可行轨迹；在轨迹参数空间中进行认证式碰撞检测。对每个足迹预计算一张网格，对局部障碍做单次遍历即可返回任意候选轨迹上的认证自由距离；证明该网格不会高估连续扫掠、可能非凸足迹的自由距离，并给出无碰撞路径与相对格状抽象的有界次优性保证。带/不带朝向的目标由 Reeds-Shepp 射击精确到达，并认证为同一生成器的轨迹。

Result: 在300个杂乱场景、真实足迹与同一目标位姿下，中位三毫秒求解全部查询且目标位姿无碰撞（AMD Ryzen 7 2700X）；与 hybrid A* 速度相当，而后者45%路径与障碍重叠；比唯一另一无碰撞的状态格状规划器快近60倍；位置目标下比 hybrid A* 快89倍。自动泊车与窄门通过上与 hybrid A* 持平，可对无凸模型可容纳的非凸叉车足迹完成对接，并在真实机器人上穿越有行人的大学校园。精确独立测试未在路径中发现碰撞。

Conclusion: 认证式轨迹参数空间碰撞检测使精确足迹规划的每节点代价与足迹/轨迹数量解耦，兼顾安全性、最优性界与毫秒级实时性，具备实机部署可行性。

Abstract: Global planners for ground vehicles usually approximate the vehicle by a disk, trading safety against conservativeness, while exact-footprint checking pays for every candidate motion, pose, and footprint edge. We present TPS-A*, a lattice planner whose edges are kinematically feasible trajectories from parameterized trajectory generators, built on certified collision checking in trajectory parameter space. A grid precomputed per footprint returns, in one pass over the local obstacles, a certified free distance along every candidate trajectory, so the per-node collision cost is independent of footprint complexity and trajectory count. We prove that this grid never overestimates the free distance of the continuously swept, possibly non-convex footprint, and state the planner's guarantees: collision-free paths and bounded suboptimality relative to its lattice abstraction. Goals with or without heading are reached exactly by Reeds-Shepp shots certified as trajectories of the same generators. An exact independent test finds no collision in its paths. On 300 cluttered worlds, with the true footprint and the same goal pose for all planners, it solves every query with a collision-free goal pose in a median of three milliseconds on an AMD Ryzen 7 2700X, on par with hybrid A*, 45\% of whose paths overlap obstacles, and nearly sixty times faster than a state-lattice planner, the only other collision-free one; with position goals, eighty-nine times faster than hybrid A*. It matches hybrid A* in automated parking and on narrow doorways, docks a non-convex fork footprint that no convex model admits, and drove a real robot across a university campus among people.

</details>


### [146] [SUGARv2: Learning Diverse Visual Humanoid Loco-Manipulation Skills from Synthesized Interaction References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09266)
*Tianshu Wu,Tianxiang Gui,Yue Chen,Tingxu Liu,Ziye Peng,Guangjie Yang,Xiangqi Kong,Ye Shi,Jingya Wang,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SUGARv2：通过可复用任务模板合成机器人-物体协调参考轨迹，用统一的参考模仿目标+特权教师蒸馏+RL，实现多样化视觉人形全身移动操作技能的规模化学习，仿真平均成功率91.0%并完成真机迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉人形loco-manipulation技能规模化获取时，任务与空间配置扩展导致演示采集与任务专属训练设计的人力成本急剧上升，需降低这两类人工开销。

Method: 1) 将可复用任务模板适配到不同空间配置，低成本合成协调的机器人-物体运动参考；2) 以参考模仿作为跨任务的统一学习目标，避免任务专属奖励设计；3) 先训练特权教师执行参考，再通过教师蒸馏+结合同一参考模仿奖励的强化学习训练视觉策略；4) 使用多样物体实例与视觉域随机化提升视觉泛化与sim-to-real。

Result: 在5个任务上仿真平均成功率91.0%；真机人形机器人上实现全身操作的sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: 统一模板化参考合成与共享训练管线可有效降低人力并泛化到多任务，展现规模化学习视觉人形移动操作技能的潜力。

Abstract: Scaling up visual humanoid loco-manipulation skill acquisition requires reducing the human effort needed as tasks and spatial configurations expand, particularly in demonstration collection and task-specific training design. We present SUGARv2, a unified framework for learning diverse visual humanoid loco-manipulation skills through interaction reference synthesis and a shared training pipeline. To obtain diverse references at low cost, we adapt reusable task templates to different spatial configurations, synthesizing coordinated robot and object motions. To reduce task-specific reward design and training effort, we use reference-based imitation as a common learning objective across tasks. We first train privileged teachers to execute these references, then train visual policies through teacher distillation and reinforcement learning with the same reference-based mimic rewards, using diverse object instances and visual domain randomization to support visual generalization and sim-to-real transfer. Across five humanoid loco-manipulation tasks, SUGARv2 achieves a mean success rate of 91.0% in simulation. Deployment on a real humanoid robot further demonstrates successful sim-to-real transfer for whole-body manipulation. These results demonstrate the framework's potential for learning diverse visual humanoid loco-manipulation skills.

</details>


### [147] [EGT: Execution-Grounded Traversability through Learned Closed-Loop Trajectory Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09270)
*Chinmay Mundane,Amith Manoharan,Arun Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Execution-Grounded Traversability (EGT)，将地形可通行性定义为"特定车辆-控制器对能执行候选路径并满足轨迹级跟踪与进度要求"的概率，用潜空间flow matching预测闭环轨迹分布，并结合MPC采样规划器与真实世界零样本迁移验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沿规划路径逐点的roll/pitch/force-angle裕度是静态几何/力学指标，忽略了地形扰动与底层控制器修正对车辆实际运动的耦合影响，导致估计不准且过度保守（安全路径被大量误弃、危险路径反被接受）。

Method: 定义EGT为车辆-控制器对成功执行候选路径的概率；以多分辨率地形特征、3D车辆姿态与轮-地接触点先验为条件，训练潜空间flow matching模型预测闭环轨迹分布，再由轨迹分布估计跟踪误差与进度达标概率；将该指标嵌入滚动时域采样规划器。

Result: 在未见地形的650次仿真试验中，EGT将安全路径误弃率降至2.2%（baseline为11.4%–39.5%），危险路径误接受率7.7%；集成到MPC采样规划器后，相对[1][2]导航失败率分别下降24–42与30–44个百分点，到达目标时间缩短最多20.5%与45%；仿真训练模型零样本迁移到真实户外实验。

Conclusion: 把可通行性从静态几何裕度转为"闭环执行概率"是有效范式，能显著提升保守性与可靠性平衡，并具备仿真到真实的迁移能力。

Abstract: Rough terrain traversability is often estimated through point-wise roll, pitch, or force-angle margins along a planned path. However, these metrics are often inaccurate and overly conservative because they ignore how terrain disturbances and low-level controller corrections can affect the vehicle's motions. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Execution-Grounded Traversability (EGT), defined as the probability that a vehicle-controller pair can execute a candidate path while meeting trajectory-level tracking and progress requirements. We estimate EGT by predicting the distribution of closed-loop trajectories using a latent-space flow matching (FM) model conditioned on multi-resolution terrain features and 3D vehicle attitude and wheel-ground contact points prior. Across 650 simulated trials on unseen terrains, EGT reduces safe-path rejection to 2.2% (versus 11.4%-39.5% for pose-margin and force-angle baselines) and unsafe acceptance to 7.7%. Integrated into a receding-horizon sampling-based planner, EGT reduces navigation failure rates by 24-42 and 30-44 percentage points over [1] and [2], respectively, while cutting goal-reaching time by up to 20.5% and 45%. Finally, we demonstrate zero-shot transfer of our simulation-trained model to real-world outdoor experiments.

</details>


### [148] [Task-Driven Closed-Loop Design Optimization of Passively Morphing Quadrotors for Contact-Mediated Narrow Gap Traversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09272)
*Etor Arza,Luca Girardi,Gabriel Maquignaz,Javier Paez,Stefan Leutenegger,Stefano Mintchev,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出闭环设计优化方法，联合优化四旋翼几何形状与两个被动折叠臂的力矩-挠度响应，以接触式穿缝任务的仿真成功率为反馈信号采样更优设计；原型可在31.3→14.1 cm被动变宽、以2 m/s穿越15 cm窄口，容忍30°接近角误差，无接触飞行仅增加2 mm位置RMSE与<1%电耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有被动变形式无人机多依赖人工设计，难以在折叠比、飞行效率与控制跟踪性能之间取得平衡；且缺乏面向具体任务（狭窄缝隙接触穿越）的任务驱动设计方法。

Method: 闭环设计优化：优化器采样候选构型，在基于接触的穿缝任务仿真中评估，将任务成功率作为反馈信号迭代采样更优设计；优化变量同时包含四旋翼几何参数与两个被动折叠臂的力矩-挠度（扭转刚度）响应，并以此驱动制造原型。

Result: 原型宽度由31.3 cm被动缩减至14.1 cm，以2 m/s参考速度穿越15 cm宽开口；可容忍最高30°接近角误差，并能折叠通过40 cm长受限通道。与刚性对照机相比，软构型在无接触飞行中仅增加2 mm位置RMSE和不足1%的电耗。

Conclusion: 任务驱动的闭环设计优化可生成“接触时紧凑、接近误差容忍、标称飞行近似刚性”的被动变形机体，为形态-材料-控制协同设计提供可复用的范式。

Abstract: Aerial robots operating in cluttered environments may encounter openings narrower than their nominal width. Existing passively morphing drones reduce their width upon traversal, but they typically involve human-made designs, which can struggle to find the right balance among folding ratio, flight efficiency, and control tracking performance. We introduce a closed-loop design method that jointly optimizes quadrotor geometry and the torque-deflection response of two passively folding arms driven by the success rate in simulation for contact-mediated gap traversal. An optimization algorithm samples candidate designs that are evaluated in a contact-based simulation of the traversal task, where the task success rate is used as a feedback signal to sample progressively better designs over time. The resulting prototype passively reduces its width from 31.3 to 14.1 cm and traverses a 15 cm wide opening at a 2 m/s reference speed. It also tolerates gap approach-angle errors of up to 30 degrees and folds through a 40 cm long confined passage. Compared to a rigid twin, the soft configuration adds only 2 mm of position RMSE and less than 1% electrical power during contact-free flight. These results show that task-driven closed-loop design optimization can produce passively morphing airframes that are compact under contact, tolerant to approach error, and effectively rigid during nominal flight.

</details>


### [149] [MDF++: Visuo-Tactile World Action Model with End-to-end Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09293)
*Zixuan Huang,Jinhao Yu,Zachary Charlick,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MDF++提出视觉-触觉世界-动作模型，以扩散骨干端到端学习多模态观测的潜在表示，并用多任务学习利用跨模态相关性避免特征坍缩，在接触密集操作任务上优于冻结预训练编码器的WAM达50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型(WAMs)依赖以重建目标训练的冻结预训练编码器，其表示未必适合机器人控制，缺乏物理基础与控制相关性。

Method: 提出MDF++：视觉-触觉世界-动作模型，与扩散骨干端到端联合学习多感官观测的潜在表示；针对朴素联合训练导致的特征坍缩，引入利用跨模态相关性的多任务学习策略，学习任务相关表示。

Result: 在接触密集操作任务（含真实世界螺栓拧入与螺母装配）上，相较基于冻结预训练表示的WAM提升最高50%。

Conclusion: 端到端学习控制相关的多模态表示优于冻结预训练编码器，为WAM的可控表示学习提供有效路径。

Abstract: Recently, world-action models (WAMs) have emerged as a powerful framework for robot manipulation. By jointly modeling action generation and future observation prediction, WAMs can act and anticipate the outcomes of the actions. However, existing WAMs typically rely on frozen pretrained encoders trained with reconstruction, which may not produce representations well suited for robot control. In this paper, we tackle the problem of learning physicallygrounded, control-relevant representations for WAMs. We propose MDF++, a visuo-tactile world-action model that learns latent representations of multisensory observations end-to-end with a diffusion backbone. While naive joint training can lead to feature collapse, MDF++ introduces a multi-task learning strategy that leverages cross-modal correlations to learn task-relevant representations. We evaluate MDF++ on a suite of contact-rich manipulation tasks, including realworld bolt screwing and nut mating, and show that it outperforms WAMs built on frozen pretrained representations by up to 50%. Additional videos are available on our website: this http URL .

</details>


### [150] [Horizon-Coupled Obstacle Guidance for Relative-Action Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09340)
*Guangyuan Wang,Haishan Xia,Zitao Zhang,Kejia Chen,Yuanchen Sun,Zhenguo Sun,Donghao Zhang,Yu Zhang,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出推理时障碍物引导框架：将预训练相对动作扩散策略的累积相对动作滚动展开为工作空间轨迹，用SDF障碍模型评估交互，并把每个障碍响应传播到去噪过程中所有贡献的动作增量（horizon-coupled），实现无需新演示或重训练的任务导向绕障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实部署中预训练策略需适应未见障碍物，但现有策略级引导在各时间步独立施加障碍修正，只能解决局部违规，无法将动作序列协调为完整、任务导向的绕行轨迹；障碍响应如何沿动作时域传播尚缺乏研究。

Method: 面向相对动作扩散策略的推理时引导：累积相对动作展开为轨迹 → SDF障碍模型沿轨迹评估交互 → 将障碍响应反传到所有相关动作增量，实现时域耦合的引导信号；并比较绝对动作逐点引导与相对动作时域耦合公式。

Result: 四个操作任务的七个障碍约束场景真机实验：105/140（75.0%）无碰撞任务成功，绝对动作逐点引导仅8/140（5.7%）；消融显示相对动作时域耦合表述在两种障碍模型下均带来更大提升。

Conclusion: 时域耦合的障碍引导可显著提升预训练扩散策略的即插即用避障成功率，且相对动作表述是关键增益来源；为推理时策略适配提供了简洁实用的范式。

Abstract: Real-world robot deployment requires adapting learned policies to unseen obstacles without collecting new demonstrations or retraining. Existing policy-level guidance often applies obstacle corrections independently at individual horizon steps, which may address local obstacle violations but fail to coordinate the action sequence into a complete, task-directed detour. How obstacle responses should be propagated across the action horizon to coordinate complete, task-directed avoidance motion remains underexplored. We introduce an inferencetime obstacle-guidance framework for pretrained relative-action diffusion policies. It rolls out cumulative relative actions into a workspace trajectory, evaluates obstacle interactions along the trajectory with an SDF-based obstacle model, and propagates each obstacle response to all contributing action increments during denoising. Real-robot experiments across seven obstacleconstrained scenarios spanning four manipulation tasks show that our method achieves 105/140 (75.0%) collision-free task successes, compared with 8/140 (5.7%) for absolute-action pointwise guidance. Ablations show that the relative-action horizoncoupled formulation provides the larger observed improvement across both obstacle models.

</details>


### [151] [Can LLMs Debug Robots by Editing Their Weights?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09376)
*Jusheng Zhang,Keze Wang,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GPT-6 Astra 以权重空间编程方式编辑 π_{0.5} 机器人策略仅 65 行 token embedding，在 765 条留出改写指令上把成功率从 86.3% 提升到 92.0%，但证据指向"精确词法修复"而非通用能力获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础策略对语言指令的词汇级接地（lexical grounding）脆弱：面对同义改写常失败，而微调或无梯度数据增广成本高、且可能破坏已有能力。作者希望用 LLM 直接读权重、在无梯度更新、无新示范的条件下完成定向修复。

Method: 让 GPT-6 Astra 读取策略权重，在 CPU 工作区中提出编辑，并借助带预算的模拟器反馈筛选候选；仅修改少量 token-embedding 行（最优编辑 65 行）。设置对照：随机方向编辑、用编辑器自身词表做整体替换、以及在 13 个额外机器人会话上的候选拒绝机制。

Result: 三次独立会话均提升 765 条留出改写的成功率，最佳 86.3%→92.0%（48 修复/4 回归，配对精确 p=1.31e-10）；含被编辑 token 的 335 条指令 74.3%→87.5%，其余 430 条结果零变化。随机方向编辑无效；整体词表替换能解释部分增益但回归更多，与编辑器差异在 0.05 水平不显著。13 个进一步会话中编辑器拒绝 12 个候选，唯一保留的编辑无显著留出增益。另发现基准元数据使 FastWAM 成功率 70.0%→48.6%，而所评估 VLA 的总体率不变。

Conclusion: 支持"精确词法修补"结论，而非通用机器人能力获取；同时提示基准元数据会造成显著评测偏差，需警惕评估口径问题。

Abstract: A language model can repair a robot policy's lexical instruction grounding by editing just 65 token-embedding rows. We bring weight-space programming to robot foundation policies: GPT-6 Astra reads weights and uses a CPU workspace and budgeted simulator feedback, without gradient updates or new demonstrations. Three independent sessions on $\pi_{0.5}$ all improve success on 765 held-out paraphrases; the best raises success from 86.3\% to 92.0\% (48 repairs, four regressions; exact paired $p=1.31\times10^{-10}$). Among the 335 instructions containing edited tokens, success rises from 74.3\% to 87.5\%; the other 430 episodes have zero outcome changes. Matched random-direction edits do not help. Full replacement using the editor's own lexicon explains much of the gain but produces more regressions; its direct difference from the editor is not significant at 0.05. Across 13 further robot sessions, the editor declines 12 candidates; the sole retained edit does not show a significant held-out gain. Separately, benchmark metadata lowers FastWAM success from 70.0\% to 48.6\%, while the evaluated VLA's aggregate rate is unchanged. The evidence supports precise lexical repair, not general acquisition of missing robot capabilities.

</details>


### [152] [Mechanical Interaction Sensing for Robotic Kiwifruit Handling with an Optoelectronic Soft Suction Cup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09468)
*Romina Adhami,Ebrahim Shahabi,Alessio Mondini,Samuel M. Youssef,Cosimo Della Santina,Gualtiero Volpe,Barbara Mazzolai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 用光电软吸盘（猕猴桃专用双组分设计）通过光学响应估计末端执行器与果实的接触力，最佳浅层网络在未参与训练的果实上R²=0.968（MAE=0.138 N），仅用平均光响应的单调映射即可达R²=0.925，跨采集日验证R²=0.955；附带Soft/Firm二分类平衡准确率约0.705。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精细水果机器人抓取需要顺应性接触与末端执行器力学交互的定量感知，而现有柔性吸盘缺乏低成本、集成化的力估计手段。

Method: 设计针对猕猴桃几何的双组分光电软吸盘，采集接触全过程中光信号与参考力；在已知接触区间用浅层网络及单调映射从光学响应回归交互力，并采用按果实分组、留一采集日等严格泛化验证。

Result: 分组折外R²=0.968±0.005（MAE=0.138 N），单果留出中位R²=0.973；均值光响应单调映射R²=0.925±0.005；留一采集日R²=0.955±0.031；Soft/Firm二分类平衡准确率约0.705，三档分级较弱。

Conclusion: 廉价光电柔性吸盘可提供高精度、跨会话稳健的接触力估计，具备力感知软体末端执行器的实用潜力；但材质分级能力有限，且为特定作物定制，尚缺乏实际抓取闭环验证。

Abstract: Robotic handling of delicate fruit benefits from compliant contact and quantitative sensing of the mechanical interaction at the end-effector. This study investigates an optoelectronic soft suction cup with a kiwifruit-specific bicomponent design for estimating interaction forces during contact with real fruit. During known-contact intervals, the optical signals accurately estimate interaction force on fruits excluded from model training: the best shallow network achieves grouped out-of-fold pooled R-squared = 0.968 +/- 0.005 (MAE = 0.138 N), with a median per-fruit held-out R-squared = 0.973 (MAE = 0.134 N). A simple monotonic mapping from the mean optical response alone achieves R-squared = 0.925 +/- 0.005, showing that substantial force-related information is already present in the optical response. Leave-one-collection-day-out validation remains strong (R-squared = 0.955 +/- 0.031), supporting robustness across acquisition sessions. As an exploratory secondary analysis, binary Soft/Firm classification from compression-test reference labels achieves moderate balanced accuracy (approximately 0.705) under grouped held-out-fruit validation, while finer three-class grading shows limited performance.

</details>


### [153] [CucumberVision at the Edge: Accuracy, Throughput, and Energy of RGB-D Cucumber Sizing on Jetson Orin Nano and Raspberry Pi 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09490)
*Manveen Kaur,Rajmeet Singh,Saeed Mozaffari,Majid Ahmadi,Shahpour Alirezaee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将在边缘设备(Jetson Orin Nano / Raspberry Pi 4)上部署曲率感知黄瓜长度测量流水线,以RTX 3050 Ti工作站为基线,系统刻画精度保持性、延迟、能耗与量化失效机理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB-D非接触水果尺寸测量在工作站已达亚厘米精度,但田间分选要求在低功耗单板计算机上运行,而这一"算力迁移成本"鲜有报道。

Method: YOLO26n检测 + 提示式分割 + 中轴弧样条估计的曲率感知管道;在Jetson Orin Nano、Raspberry Pi 4与RTX 3050 Ti上回放50段相同温室采集(7个样本),采用样本级cluster-bootstrap统计;TensorRT FP16编译;四项消融(分段后端、全图INT8量化、NMS-free检测头、功耗模式);测量能量/果。

Result: MAE 0.58 cm(95% CI 0.46–0.69),Jetson与工作站配对差为零;TensorRT FP16使Jetson检测延迟降低2.57×,给出72 ms@0.84 cm与2.74 s@0.64 cm两个工作点;分割后端决定精度(跨平台复现≤0.01 cm)而平台决定成本(差214×);NMS-free头整图INT8量化因单张量混合坐标与置信度而结构性失效(无检测输出),排除检测头后恢复并提速1.37×;Jetson 42.2 J/果,7 W模式比15 W模式多耗73%;Pi+MobileSAM每果成本为Jetson+SAM的2.5×,尽管功耗低47%,且持续负载无热降频。

Conclusion: 给出精度-吞吐-能耗包络及三个非支配配置与选型规则;精度与成本决策可解耦,但边缘量化需谨慎处理混合语义输出张量。

Abstract: Non-contact fruit sizing from RGB-D imagery reaches sub-centimetre accuracy on workstation hardware, but in-field grading requires the same pipeline to run on a low-power single-board computer, and the cost of that transition is rarely reported. We deploy a curvature-aware cucumber length-measurement pipeline (YOLO26n detection, prompt-based segmentation and a medial arc spline estimator) on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano and a Raspberry Pi 4, and characterize both against an RTX 3050 Ti workstation by replaying 50 identical greenhouse captures of 7 specimens, with specimen-level cluster-bootstrap statistics. Accuracy is preserved: mean absolute error (MAE) is 0.58 cm (95% CI 0.46-0.69) on both the workstation and the Jetson at matched precision, with a paired difference of zero. TensorRT FP16 compilation cuts Jetson detection latency 2.57x, giving two operating points on one board: 72 ms per fruit at 0.84 cm, or 2.74 s at 0.64 cm. Four ablations map the design space. For prompted segmentation backends, measurement accuracy is fixed by the backend and reproduces across platforms to within 0.01 cm, while its cost is fixed by the platform and varies 214x, so the two choices are separable. Whole-graph INT8 quantization of the NMS-free detection head fails structurally, returning no detections, because one output tensor carries both pixel coordinates and confidences; we show that no calibration strategy can avoid this. Excluding the head restores function at 1.37x. Energy per fruit is 42.2 J on the Jetson, and its 7 W mode consumes 73% more than its 15 W mode. The Raspberry Pi with MobileSAM costs 2.5x more per fruit than the Jetson with SAM despite drawing 47% less power, and it runs without thermal throttling under sustained load. The results define an accuracy-throughput-energy envelope with three non-dominated configurations and give rules for selecting among them.

</details>


### [154] [Lightweight Path-Aware Frontier Re-ranking for LiDAR-Based UAV Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09561)
*Duc Manh Do,Thanh Nguyen Canh,Ngoc Minh Do,Mai Thanh Thai,Xiem HoangVan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出轻量级路径感知的frontier重排序方法：先用轻量效用函数保留top-N候选，再用时间受限的RRT*估计可行路径代价进行重排序，并复用所选frontier的路径用于导航以减少重复全局规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于欧氏距离的frontier选择在杂乱环境中无法反映真实无碰撞通行代价，导致探索路径低效；同时机载算力与飞行时间受限，需要轻量化方案。

Method: 基于LiDAR的UAV自主探索管线；两阶段frontier选择：(1)轻量效用函数筛出top-N候选；(2)时间受限RRT*对候选做运动规划以估计路径代价并重排序；选中frontier的规划路径直接复用于导航，避免冗余全局规划。对比传统OctoMap frontier基线。

Result: 在三个仓库类环境中，探索轨迹一致短于传统frontier基线，最大减少14.1%飞行距离；两个环境中探索时间缩短最多9.5%，第三个环境保持竞争力；地图细节与OctoMap基线相当。

Conclusion: 路径感知重排序在有限计算预算下有效提升frontier探索效率，并在探索效率与建图质量间取得良好折衷；但仅仿真环境验证，缺少真实飞行实验与开源代码。

Abstract: Autonomous 3D exploration with UAVs requires efficient target selection under limited flight time and onboard computation. In conventional Euclidean-distance-based frontier pipelines, geometric distance may poorly represent the actual collision-free travel cost in cluttered environments, leading to inefficient frontier choices. This paper presents a lightweight path-aware frontier re-ranking method for LiDAR-based UAV exploration. The method first retains a small top-N set of promising frontier candidates using a lightweight utility function and then applies time-bounded RRT* planning to estimate their feasible path costs for re-ranking. The path generated for the selected frontier is further reused for navigation to reduce redundant global planning. Experiments in three warehouse-like environments show that the proposed method consistently produces shorter exploration trajectories than conventional frontier baselines, with traveled-distance reductions of up to 14.1%, while reducing exploration time by up to 9.5% in two environments and remaining competitive in the third. The method maintains map detail comparable to the conventional OctoMap-based baseline, demonstrating an effective trade-off between exploration efficiency and mapping quality.

</details>


### [155] [Extended Model-Based Learned Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09584)
*Sasanka Kuruppu Arachchige,Joni-Kristian Kämäräinen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出在机体坐标系下将机体力矩动力学（由旋翼信号推导）引入学习式惯性里程计，预测短时域相对位移并与IMU在EKF中融合，提升对未见轨迹的泛化性与速度估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯惯性积分存在传感器误差与时变偏置导致的位姿漂移；现有面向无人机竞速的学习式惯性里程计对训练分布外的轨迹泛化能力有限。

Method: 采用机体坐标系表示以降低对位姿的依赖，并将机体力矩动力学（最优由旋翼转速/信号推得）作为输入特征纳入网络训练；网络输出短时域相对位移，再与IMU测量在扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）中融合进行状态估计。

Result: 在未见轨迹上优于先前SOTA学习式惯性里程计；在四个公开四旋翼数据集上以整条轨迹形状作留出，所有留出集合的速度误差均更低；消融显示力矩输入在由旋翼信号推导时信息量最大。

Conclusion: 机体坐标系表示+力矩动力学先验能有效改善学习式惯性里程计的跨轨迹泛化，且代码、数据流水线与模型公开，可作为敏捷四旋翼状态估计的实用组件。

Abstract: Inertial odometry is a compelling approach to state estimation for agile quadrotor flight due to its affordability, low weight, and robustness to perceptual degradation. However, relying only on integrated inertial measurements is impractical, as sensor errors and time-varying biases lead to significant pose drift. Although recent advances have enabled inertial odometry for drone racing, existing approaches show limited generalization to trajectories not seen during training. This work improves generalization by adopting a body-frame representation and incorporating body-frame torque dynamics into the learning pipeline. The learned model predicts short-horizon relative displacements, which are fused with IMU measurements in an Extended Kalman Filter. Experimental results show that the proposed method outperforms the previous state-of-the-art learned inertial odometry approach for quadrotor pose estimation on unseen trajectories. An additional evaluation on four public quadrotor datasets, with whole trajectory shapes held out of training, confirms a lower velocity error on every held-out set and shows that the torque input is most informative when it is derived from the rotor signals. Code, data pipeline and trained models are publicly available.

</details>


### [156] [DKWBC: Decoupling Motion Tracking and Balancing for Humanoid Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09605)
*Dingkun Wei,Zehong Shen,Huaijin Pi,Xueyang Wang,Xing Zhu,Hujun Bao,Yujun Shen,Xiaowei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DKWBC提出动力学引导的人形全身控制框架，用逆动力学前馈+关节PD提供跟踪力矩，学习式残差策略仅输出平衡力矩修正；单一策略跨动作数据集与多种扰动场景泛化，并通过真机验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL跟踪器将运动跟踪与平衡耦合在一个统一输出中学习，但相似参考姿态的动态需求差异极大，导致学习困难且控制输出出现高频抖动。

Method: 基于浮动基动力学的模型项与环境外力项分解：以逆动力学前馈加关节PD反馈生成跟踪力矩，残差策略只学习额外的关节力矩平衡修正，从而显式处理运动相关力矩需求、简化策略学习任务。

Result: 仿真与真机实验显示，单个平衡策略可跨多个动作数据集泛化，在地面支撑、附加负载与物理交互条件下保持精确跟踪与平衡，并抑制高频抖动。

Conclusion: 将模型基跟踪与学习式平衡残差解耦是人形全身控制的有效范式，具有较好的跨任务泛化与实机部署潜力。

Abstract: Humanoid whole-body control requires a robot to track diverse reference motions while maintaining balance under gravity, torque limits, contact switching, and external disturbances. Existing reinforcement-learning-based trackers typically learn control outputs that jointly address motion tracking and balance. However, similar reference poses can correspond to substantially different dynamic demands, requiring a unified policy to learn motion-dependent control requirements alongside balance corrections. This can complicate learning and lead to high-frequency fluctuations in control outputs. We propose DKWBC, a dynamics-informed whole-body control framework that combines explicit model-based tracking with a learned balancing residual. Inspired by the model-based and environmental-wrench contributions to joint torques in floating-base dynamics, DKWBC uses inverse-dynamics feedforward and joint PD feedback to supply tracking torques, while a residual policy learns additional joint-torque corrections for balanced execution. By explicitly accounting for motion-dependent torque demands, this structure simplifies the policy's learning task. Experiments in simulation and on real hardware demonstrate that a single learned balancing policy generalizes across motion datasets while maintaining accurate motion tracking and balance across ground support, attached payloads, and physical interaction. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [157] [LACART: Lookahead-Augmented Collision Avoidance and Reference Tracking Strategies for Safety-Critical Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09640)
*Pedro Lopes,Amit Shivam,Luis Tiago Paiva*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LACART：将lookahead-based CBF、松弛CLF与QP结合，在统一优化层中处理圆形障碍与赛道边界，实时修正车辆名义指令，实现自主赛车避障与参考跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主赛车对避障的几何与计算约束极严，需要兼具安全性保证与毫秒级实时性的控制框架。

Method: 以lookahead点的CBF约束（对应前瞻点间隙）加松弛型CLF约束构成QP，叠加于名义控制器之上，同时处理圆形障碍物与赛道边界；给出前瞻点间隙与车身间隙之间的关联条件；并搭建1:43缩比车、相机估计与无线执行平台验证。

Result: 与Huang等人的方法相比，全赛道路径相似，中位计算时间更低：单步0.31 vs 0.33 ms，单次QP求解0.023 vs 0.025 ms；缩比实车在约25 Hz控制率下运行，并展示了安全干预与恢复。

Conclusion: 该方法面向地面机器人避障与实时安全监控，兼具形式化安全约束与低计算开销，但硬件验证规模有限，仍需更充分的实际部署与定量安全评估。

Abstract: Autonomous racing provides a demanding testbed for collision avoidance under tight geometric and computational constraints. Building on a recent conference contribution and a master's dissertation, LACART combines lookahead-based control barrier functions (CBFs), a relaxed control Lyapunov function (CLF), and quadratic programming (QP) to modify nominal vehicle commands for obstacle avoidance and reference tracking. The method handles circular obstacles and track boundaries in a common optimisation layer. Comparative simulation results against Huang \emph{et al.} \cite{r9} show similar full-track paths and lower median computation times: 0.31 versus 0.33 ms per step and 0.023 versus 0.025 ms per QP solve. The dissertation further demonstrates a 1:43-scale vehicle with camera-based estimation and wireless actuation at a reported 25 Hz control rate. We identify the conditions linking lookahead-point clearance to body clearance, illustrate hardware limitations, and propose a recorded demonstration of safety intervention and recovery. The contribution targets ground-robot collision avoidance and real-time safety monitoring.

</details>


### [158] [Goodhart in the Planner: Sampling-Based Control Exploits Rewards Learned from Human Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09670)
*Soovadeep Bakshi,Apurba Bose*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文揭示"用偏好奖励模型做搜索规划反被利用"(reward hacking):MPPI搜索越强、真实回报下降最多76%,并给出基于评分器误差分布的温度设定与χ²正则化MPPI的最坏情形最优性与校准修正方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人越来越多地用人类偏好学得的奖励模型配合MPPI或best-of-N搜索来选动作,但在"分布外"提议上奖励模型会被系统性利用;作者希望量化这种搜索对真实目标的实际损害,并给出可证明的安全规划规则。

Method: 在HalfCheetah、Hopper(众包标签)与Meta-World drawer-open、plate-slide(合成标签+两位作者盲标)上测量搜索强度对真实回报的影响;以规划权重相对提议分布的√KL为解释变量;理论分析χ²正则化MPPI,给出温度由评分器误差散布决定的最坏情形最优性、不可认证增益的阈值条件,以及单回合回报逐步分解;并测试基于真实回报排序的100对提议对进行校准的修正规则及"人不可区分对/第二人"修正。

Result: 更强MPPI搜索使真实回报最多下降76%(drawer-open人工标签:规划20次0次成功,起点克隆策略10/10成功),而留出标签准确率仍保持0.75–0.97;plate-slide上人工标签被利用程度低于合成标签,window-open上搜索有益;best-of-N配合合成标签验证器甚至劣于单个含噪动作;按误差散布设温度的校准规则在五组独立集合的每个arm运行中均成功,并保留克隆策略86–94%的回报。仅用单人比较校准无法检出该人引入的偏差。

Conclusion: 在奖励模型下单纯加大搜索会系统性地reward hacking,留出准确率不能作为安全指标;作者提供的误差散布温度规则与χ²正则化MPPI给出最坏情形保证,但校准依赖能反映真实目标的oracle排序,单人或人工标签校准存在不可检测的偏差。

Abstract: Robots increasingly choose actions by searching against a reward model learned from human preferences, in model-predictive control or best-of-$N$ selection; we measure what this does to the true objective. On HalfCheetah and Hopper with crowd-sourced labels, and on Meta-World drawer-open and plate-slide with synthetic labels and blind labels from the two authors, harder MPPI search cuts true return by up to 76%: with human labels the drawer opens in 0 of 20 runs against 10 of 10 for the cloned policy the planner starts from, while held-out label accuracy stays at 0.75-0.97. On plate-slide human labels are exploited less than synthetic ones, and on window-open search helps. True return roughly follows how far the planner's weights move from its proposals ($\sqrt{\mathrm{KL}}$) for both weighting rules we test, and best-of-$N$ with a verifier from synthetic labels falls below a single noisy action. We prove that $\chi^2$-regularized MPPI at a temperature set by the scorer's error spread on the proposals is worst-case optimal, that no positive gain can be certified once that spread exceeds the scorer's own, and that an episode's return splits exactly into per-step terms, of which the guarantee covers the selection terms. Calibrated from 100 proposal pairs ranked by true return (an oracle) on start states the evaluation never uses, this rule is the safest fix we test: with each of five independent sets it succeeds in every arm run and keeps on average 86-94% of the cloned policy's return on plate-slide and drawer-open, though it is cautious where search helps. Calibration from a person's comparisons cannot detect a bias the scorer learned from that person; correcting for the pairs people cannot tell apart, or a second person, limits the damage.

</details>


### [159] [ODEAction: From Predicting Chunks to Evolving Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09672)
*Qishen Wang,Pengxiang Ding,Shifeng Bao,Mingyang Sun,Hengtao Li,Yi Gao,Baohua Yin,Yang Liu,Jiajun Fu,Jiacheng Liu,Changjing He,Peiyang Li,Runze Suo,Yan Wang,Xiaobo Wang,Yonghao Dang,Cheng Chi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ODEAction 提出无 chunk 的动作生成范式，将动作块预测替换为"连续动作演化"：学习动作空间上的转移场，以当前动作为初值用 ODE 积分递归生成后续动作，消除 chunk 边界不连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型通常以 chunk 形式分段预测动作，段与段之间独立预测导致动作块切换处出现潜在不连续性，影响轨迹平滑性与精细操作表现。

Method: 以视觉观测、语言指令与本体感知为条件，学习定义在动作空间上的动作转移场（transition vector 表示动作相对演化时间的变化率）；执行时以当前动作为 ODE 初始条件，对学到的场进行数值积分，递归生成后续动作并保持显式依赖关系。实现为通用 action head，不绑定特定 VLM 主干。

Result: 在 LIBERO、EBench、RoboTwin 及真实机器人上，相较匹配的 action-chunking 基线，任务成功率与轨迹质量均提升，在精细操作任务上增益尤为明显。

Conclusion: chunk-free 的连续动作演化可有效缓解 chunk 边界不连续问题，且以轻量 action head 形式即插即用，为 VLA 动作解码头设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models typically generate actions in chunks, organizing continuous robot execution into separately predicted segments and thus introducing potential discontinuities at action-chunk transitions. We propose ODEAction, a chunk-free action generation paradigm that replaces action-chunk prediction with Continuous Action Evolution. Given visual observations, language instructions, and robot proprioceptive information, ODEAction learns an action transition field defined over action space, where a transition vector characterizes the evolution rate of the current action with respect to evolution time. During execution, the model takes the current action as its initial condition and integrates the learned field using an ordinary differential equation (ODE), recursively generating subsequent actions with explicit dependencies. ODEAction is implemented as an action head and does not depend on a specific vision-language backbone architecture. Experiments on LIBERO, EBench, RoboTwin, and real robots show that ODEAction improves both task performance and trajectory quality over matched action-chunking baselines, with particularly pronounced gains on fine manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [160] [Autonomous Particle Image Velocimetry Using Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09679)
*Chang Rajani,Luke Sebastien Marsden,Stephan Kunz,Claudio Mucignat,Ivan Lunati*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将相机放置建模为序贯决策问题，用强化学习训练6-DoF机械臂控制相机，在半实物水洞中定位、取景并高分辨率解析水翼尾流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PIV重建质量受限于图像中流场结构的解析度，而拍摄位置通常由操作者在测量前固定，难以自适应地聚焦于感兴趣的流动结构。

Method: 将相机位姿控制表述为序贯决策，训练RL智能体操作搭载相机的6自由度机械臂；智能体仅通过时间分离图像对估计的流速场与不确定性观测场景；全部在基于CFD生成不同翼型/位置/攻角水翼尾流的光真实感水洞仿真中训练，零样本迁移到真实水洞。

Result: 零样本迁移后策略能可靠定位并取景随机放置水翼的尾流；相比固定视角具有更高解析度、流场统计量误差更低，且耗时约为全扫描一半、所需图像对数量大幅减少。

Conclusion: 把主动感知（相机放置）与PIV重建闭环耦合的RL方法，可在真实实验设施中实现高效自适应的流场测量，展示了仿真训练+零样本实机迁移的可行性。

Abstract: Particle image velocimetry (PIV) reconstructs a flow field from the motion of tracer particles but the reconstruction quality is bounded by how well the flow structure of interest is resolved in the images. The acquisition position is typically fixed by the operator before the measurement. We cast camera placement as a sequential decision-making problem and train a reinforcement learning agent to control a 6-DoF manipulator equipped with a camera, enabling it to locate, frame and resolve a hydrofoil wake in a water tunnel. The agent commands the camera pose and observes the scene only through the velocity field and uncertainty estimated from time-separated image pairs. It is trained entirely in a photorealistic simulation of our water tunnel, with wakes generated by Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) simulations of hydrofoils with varying profiles, positions and angles of attack. After zero-shot transfer to the real water tunnel, the policy reliably locates and frames the wakes of randomly placed hydrofoils. It resolves the wake at a higher resolution than a fixed camera view and with lower error in the measured flow statistics, in roughly half the time of a full scan and with far fewer image pairs.

</details>


### [161] [SURE:Spatiotemporal Uncertainty Representation for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09684)
*Jingye Zhang,Choo Sin Wai,Dake Zhong,Zhifang Liu,Yuhong Zhang,Siyao Xiao,Guorui Song,Guocun Wang,Dongbin Zhang,Peng Jiao,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SURE 是一个数据高效的 VLA 训练框架，通过概率动作解码器建模空间不确定性、用 Soft-DTW 对齐监督建模时间不确定性，在仅 10% 演示数据或 20% 训练步数下达到或超过基线成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略依赖大量演示数据；操作任务中动作空间存在结构化时空不确定性——多个邻近动作/轨迹都能成功，且语义等价演示的速度不同。标准逐点监督把每个演示动作当作精确目标，带来不必要学习负担。

Method: 在 VLA 主干上加入轻量转置卷积概率动作解码器，利用相邻动作 bin 的局部相关性建模空间不确定性；并引入 Soft-DTW 对齐监督，容忍预测与演示动作块之间的局部时序变化。

Result: 在 LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0 及真实世界操作任务上评估，仅用 10% 演示数据或 20% 训练步数即可匹配或超越基线 VLA 的操作成功率。

Conclusion: 显式建模时空不确定性的软监督可显著提升 VLA 的数据效率，降低对演示者姿态与执行节奏的敏感性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies have become a promising paradigm for end-to-end robot control, but their effectiveness still depends heavily on large volumes of demonstration data. In manipulation tasks, this dependence is compounded by structured spatiotemporal uncertainty in the action space: multiple nearby actions or trajectories may successfully solve the same task, while semantically equivalent demonstrations often unfold at different speeds. Standard pointwise supervision treats each demonstrated action as an exact target, imposing an unnecessary learning burden under such uncertainty. We present SURE, a data-efficient VLA training framework that explicitly models both spatial and temporal uncertainty. SURE augments a VLA backbone with a lightweight transposed-convolutional probabilistic action decoder that captures spatial uncertainty through local correlations among neighboring action bins. It further introduces Soft-DTW alignment supervision to accommodate local timing variations between predicted and demonstrated action chunks. Together, these two components reduce sensitivity to demonstrator-specific poses and execution timing, enabling more data-efficient action learning. We evaluate SURE on LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0, and real-world manipulation tasks. Even with only 10% of the demonstration data or 20% of the training steps, SURE achieves manipulation success rates that match or surpass baseline VLAs.

</details>


### [162] [A wearable input/output interface learns personalized models of haptic perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09708)
*Wen Li,E. Magnus Adams,Zijie Xu,Jiawen Yao,Aybala Esmer,Xiaoyue Ni,Bozhi Tian,Pedro Lopes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发36通道电触觉阵列+电容触摸传感的可穿戴触觉I/O设备，在同一身体坐标系下建立电触觉与牵连热感的个性化感知地图，并提出人在回路标定框架，通过正演预测模型实现逆渲染的个性化触觉反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴触觉界面的实际感知位置因人而异，且常偏离执行器物理位置，尤其对新兴触觉反馈技术而言。需要测量、建模并按个体感知差异渲染的硬件—算法一体化方案。

Method: 硬件上将36通道电触觉阵列与电容触摸传感集成，使刺激位点与用户报告的感知位点在同一身体参考坐标系内对齐；对24名被试采集电触觉与牵连热感感知图；分析跨被试的电极对特异性空间结构及触觉—热感空间对应关系；据此构建人在回路标定框架，采用前向预测策略拟合个体化感知模型，进而通过逆渲染选择能产生目标感知的刺激模式。

Result: 电触觉与牵连热感感知图存在显著个体间差异，但两种模态在跨被试层面均保留电极对特异的空间结构，且二者存在个体差异强度的空间对应；个体化模型支持逆渲染，并在触觉引导机器人遥操作、视觉—触觉替代、人际触觉通信和VR四类场景中演示了个性化触觉渲染。

Conclusion: 该平台提供了"测量—建模—渲染"闭环的个体自适应触觉界面范式，是可穿戴触觉个性化的重要进展；但实验规模有限（24人）、缺乏下游任务定量性能与用户收益评估，且未涉及机器人本体端接触状态推断。

Abstract: Wearable haptic interfaces deliver physical stimuli to the skin, yet perceived locations vary widely across users and diverge from actuator locations, particularly for emergent tactile-feedback techniques. We developed a wearable haptic input/output (I/O) device integrating a 36-channel electrotactile array with capacitive touch sensing, enabling stimulation and user-reported percept locations to be registered within the same body-referenced coordinate frame. In 24 participants, the device revealed electrotactile and referred-thermal perceptual maps with notable interparticipant variability. Despite this variability, both modalities retained electrode-pair-specific spatial structure across participants. Tactile and thermal percepts also showed spatial correspondence, whose strength varied across participants. These findings motivated our human-in-the-loop calibration framework, which adapts a forward-prediction strategy to individual users and generates personalized perceptual models. These models enabled inverse rendering by selecting stimulation patterns predicted to generate a target percept for a specific user. Using these models, we demonstrate personalized haptic rendering in haptic-guided robot teleoperation, visual-to-haptic sensory substitution, interpersonal haptic communication, and virtual reality (VR). Together, this platform provides a route toward adaptive haptic interfaces that measure, model, and render according to each user's perception.

</details>


### [163] [Autonomous Whole-body Loco-manipulation Using a Snake Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09777)
*Adarsh Salagame,Henry Noyes,Haroon Hublikar,Alireza Ramezani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 蛇形机器人COBRA在不区分推进与操作部件的前提下，实现任意身体部位接触的闭环全身移动-操作；提出统一地面-机器人-物体接触的接触隐式动力学模型（Moreau-Jean时间步进），结合几何规划器与名义P控制，在侧向蜿蜒运动中自主将物体推至目标位置，并在硬件上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超冗余蛇形机器人理论上可同时用于移动与操作，但全身移动-操作困难：身体既作推进机构又作操作工具。已有轮式、腿式与蛇形平台通常通过专用附肢、身体分段或夹爪将两种功能解耦，牺牲了全身接触的通用性。

Method: 构建接触隐式动力学模型，在Moreau-Jean时间步进框架下统一建模机器人-地面、机器人-物体、物体-地面三类相互作用；任务层用几何规划器生成参考，配合名义比例控制器，并在全阶动力学上求解轨迹优化；硬件上跨多组构型验证规划器与控制器；仿真中给出简化轨迹优化以求解机器人与物体、物体与地面接触。

Result: 在真实硬件上于多种初始/目标构型下验证了几何规划器与比例控制器，成功实现边侧向蜿蜒边将物体推向目标；仿真展示了能解析机器人-物体及物体-地面接触的简化轨迹优化结果。

Conclusion: 全身接触的移动-操作可在无专用夹爪/分段解耦下通过接触隐式建模与规划-控制组合实现；当前轨迹优化仍为简化版本，全阶动力学优化与更丰富的接触模式有待进一步硬件验证。

Abstract: Snake robots can leverage their hyper-redundant morphology for both locomotion and manipulation through distributed body contact but whole-body loco-manipulation is particularly challenging since the entire body serves simultaneously as propulsive mechanism and manipulation tool Prior work on wheeled legged and snake robot platforms has decoupled these functions through dedicated appendages body segmentation or grippers We present closed-loop loco-manipulation with our snake robot COBRA without this distinction such that any part of the body may make contact We develop a contact-implicit dynamic model unifying robot-ground robot-object and object-ground interactions within a Moreau-Jean time-stepping framework and formulate a trajectory optimization over the full-order dynamics guided by a geometric planner and nominal proportional controller to autonomously push an object to a goal location during sidewinding We validate the planner and controller on hardware across varied configurations and present simulation results for a simplified trajectory optimization that resolves robot-object and object-ground contacts

</details>


### [164] [Seeing What Matters: Benchmarking Task-Conditioned Actionable Spatial Belief for Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09794)
*Yina Jian,Mingkun Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Seeing What Matters 基准：在仿真桌面操作中评估「任务条件化的可执行空间信念」，即视图选择策略能否主动获取当前指令真正需要的观测证据。含 860 个冻结评测案例、三个域（咖啡台、抽屉/柜、微波炉），闭环可执行性与同物体功能性 OOD 两协议；诊断性参考管线（空间-功能信念状态 + 缺失证据驱动视图排序）达 98.8%/98.9%，七个 VLM 仅 26–43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间推理基准只考察模型能否描述场景（场景级完整性），但操作任务真正需要的是为当前指令采集「使命令可执行」的证据；同一工位下对某任务有信息量的视角对另一任务可能无用，因此需按任务条件评估主动观测能力。

Method: 构建仿真桌面操作基准：每个案例 = 候选 RGB 视角集合 + 操作指令 + 共享脚本化技能 + 隐藏后置条件；两套协议（闭环可执行性、同物体功能性 OOD）；参考管线维护空间-功能信念状态并按「缺失证据」对视角排序；另有消融（去功能组件/指令绑定）、覆盖元数据替换为冻结 RGB 图像分数、视觉分数打乱等对照。

Result: 主预算设置下参考管线闭环 98.8%、功能 OOD 98.9%；消融功能或指令绑定组件后性能近乎归零；7 个 VLM 仅 26–43%；用冻结 RGB 派生分数替换覆盖元数据仍保持 98.5%/98.9%，打乱视觉分数则跌至 15%/11%，表明结果依赖图像内容。

Conclusion: 操作中的空间信念不应以场景级完整性评价，而应看其是否获取了当前指令所需的证据；现有 VLM 在此维度存在显著差距。

Abstract: When should a robot look at the cup, and when at the portafilter? Spatial-reasoning benchmarks evaluate whether a model can describe a scene, but manipulation needs something more specific: the ability to acquire the particular evidence that makes the current command executable. A view that is informative for one task can be useless for another, even at the same workstation. We introduce Seeing What Matters, a benchmark for task-conditioned actionable spatial belief in simulated tabletop manipulation. Across three domains--coffee workstation, drawer/cabinet, and microwave/appliance--the benchmark provides 860 frozen evaluation cases; expanding across methods, budgets, and inference repeats yields more than 115,000 evaluator episodes under two protocols: closed-loop actionability and same-object functional out-of-distribution generalization. Each case pairs a set of candidate RGB views with a manipulation command, shared scripted skills, and hidden postconditions, so the benchmark directly measures whether a view-selection policy acquires the observations its command actually needs. We also provide a diagnostic reference pipeline that maintains a spatial-functional belief state and ranks views by missing evidence. At primary budget settings, this pipeline reaches 98.8% closed-loop and 98.9% functional-OOD success; ablating the functional or command-binding components collapses performance to near zero. Seven vision-language models, evaluated on the same cases, reach only 26-43%. Replacing the reference pipeline's coverage metadata with frozen RGB-derived scores preserves 98.5%/98.9% success; shuffling the visual scores collapses it to 15%/11%, confirming that the result depends on image content. These gaps suggest that spatial belief for manipulation should be evaluated not by scene-level completeness, but by whether it acquires the evidence the current command requires.

</details>


### [165] [Grasp-TAGS: Task-Aware Grasp Selection via Critic Distillation for Prehensile Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09864)
*Xun Tu,Minghao Zou,Karthik Desingh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Grasp-TAGS：面向大物体重排的任务感知抓取选择框架，先用统一 PPO 策略在多种初始抓取下训练，再把 critic 蒸馏成轻量"抓取效用网络"，仅凭候选抓取配置与目标信息即可在接触前排序并选出最优抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序移动操作重排任务中，机器人-物体、物体-环境的交互动力学复杂；模型法需精确动力学模型，学习法通常假定固定或预定义初始抓取，而不同几何稳定抓取会导致下游操作性能差异显著，抓取选择因此成为关键但被忽视的环节。

Method: 跨多个初始抓取配置训练统一的 PPO 强化学习策略；将学到的 critic 蒸馏为轻量 grasp utility network，输入仅为候选抓取构型与目标信息；推理时在发生接触前为每个候选抓取预测下游效用并选最高者，交由下游策略执行任务。

Result: 重排成功率平均 89.84%，优于最佳单抓取策略 79.87%（提升 9.97 个百分点），并优于随机抓取选择；在所有评估物体上均降低最终朝向误差。

Conclusion: 将"抓取选择"显式建模为面向下游任务效用的前馈预测问题，可在接触前完成零成本决策，且蒸馏得到的轻量网络便于在线部署；为长时序移动操作提供了抓取与任务的解耦式协同方案。

Abstract: Loco-manipulation, which requires mobile manipulators to coordinate locomotion and manipulation over long horizons, is a key capability for large-object rearrangement in applications such as service robotics and warehouse automation. Despite recent progress, reliably executing these tasks remains challenging because performance depends on complex robot-object and object-environment interaction dynamics. Existing approaches address this challenge either through model-based methods, which often require accurate dynamics models, or through learning-based methods, which reduce this requirement but typically assume a fixed or predefined initial grasp. However, different geometrically stable grasps can lead to substantially different downstream manipulation performance. Therefore, we propose Grasp-TAGS, a task-aware grasp selection framework for goal-conditioned large-object rearrangement. We train a unified PPO-based reinforcement learning policy across multiple initial grasp configurations and distill the learned critic into a lightweight grasp utility network that ranks candidate grasps using only the candidate grasp configuration and goal information. At inference time, we select the grasp with the highest predicted downstream utility for the downstream policy to complete the task, before even making a contact. Experiments demonstrate that Grasp-TAGS improves rearrangement success over both fixed single-grasp policies and random grasp selection, achieving an average success rate of 89.84% compared with 79.87% for the best-performing single-grasp policies, an improvement of 9.97 percentage points. Grasp-TAGS also reduces final orientation errors across all evaluated objects. For more qualitative results, refer to the supplementary webpage: this https URL

</details>


### [166] [Locating Contact on Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots From Tendon Measurements Alone via Constrained Moving Horizon Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09877)
*Haitham El-Hussieny*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 肌腱驱动连续体机器人本体接触不可观，论文证明这是结构性的(零空间维度≥7)，利用已知障碍几何下的非穿透与互补条件使接触力局部可辨识，提出实时约束移动视野估计器(MHE)精确定位接触与估计力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌腱驱动连续体机器人可穿越受限空间，但全身接触对电机端传感器不可见；作者希望区分"调参问题"与"结构性不可观"，并在可获得障碍几何时恢复接触可辨识性。

Method: 证明接触力到肌腱测量的映射零空间维数至少为7，说明无约束估计器无法定位接触；在障碍几何已知时引入非穿透与互补约束，证明含未知接触输入的系统的局部指数强可检测性；构造约束MHE（每采样求解一个QP，实时运行），并给出分模式的局部鲁棒稳定性结果；用两段连续体机器人压/滑墙的有限元模型做基准。

Result: 接触定位误差0.9 mm、接触力误差0.22 N；带状态投影的调优EKF为65 mm与0.72 N。MHE可容忍毫米级障碍位置误差与未建模壁面摩擦（EKF失效）；40次随机模型失配（含肌腱摩擦）试验中力与位置每次都更优；无接触时MHE相较EKF无优势。

Conclusion: 接触约束（非穿透+互补）是性能提升的唯一来源；MHE为连续体机器人本体接触感知提供了可实时实现的带保证估计框架，但验证限于有限元仿真，缺少真实机器人实验。

Abstract: Tendon-driven continuum robots can reach through constrained spaces, but contact along their bodies is invisible to the sensors at their motors. We show that this is a structural limitation rather than a tuning problem: the map from contact forces to tendon measurements has a null space of dimension at least seven, so no estimator that leaves the contact support free can locate contact. When the obstacle geometry is known, however, non-penetration and complementarity make the contact force locally identifiable, and we prove that the resulting system with unknown contact inputs is locally exponentially strongly detectable. We present a constrained moving horizon estimator (MHE) that encodes these conditions and runs in real time as one quadratic program per sample, and we give a local, mode-wise robust stability result for it. In a finite- element benchmark of a two-section robot pressing and sliding on a wall, the estimator located contact to 0.9 mm and estimated contact force to 0.22 N, compared with 65 mm and 0.72 N for a tuned extended Kalman filter (EKF) with state projection. The estimator tolerated millimeter- level errors in the obstacle position and unmodeled wall friction, under which the projected EKF failed. Under randomized model mismatch over 40 trials, including unmodeled tendon friction, it remained better in force and location in every trial. Without contact, the MHE offered no advantage over the EKF, which confirms that its benefit comes from the contact constraints. This report extends a letter submitted to IEEE Control Systems Letters with complete proofs and additional experiments.

</details>


### [167] [Scenario Orchestration for Closed-loop Policy Evaluation of Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09888)
*Frieda Rong,Zhaoyue Wang,Veer Kapadia,Vishaka Rajani,Aren A. Babikian,Xujie Si,Mahsa Golchoubian,Raquel Urtasun,Igor Gilitschenski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"场景编排器"(scenario orchestrator)，在闭环仿真中持续监测联合交通状态，并通过重定时、重规划路线、角色再分配等最小干预手段实时调整非自车参与者，以保持预设交互意图；同时区分"场景是否实现"与"策略是否失败"两个评价维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预先脚本化的仿真场景在自车以闭环方式响应后，可能无法保持原本设计的交互意图；脚本参与者无法适应意外自车行为，而学习型交通参与者虽具反应性，却不能可靠地实现特定场景意图。因此需要一种能主动"守护"场景意图的闭环编排机制。

Method: 每步求解一个带约束的优化问题，在保持目标交互的前提下最小化干预量并保证交通可行性，对非自车参与者执行re-timing、re-routing与角色再分配；测试判定分为两级：先判断目标交互是否发生，再评估自车策略响应。

Result: 在CARLA中对多个场景族与多种驾驶策略进行实验，编排方法比脚本化基线与学习型参与者级基线更可靠地实现目标交互，尤其在初始条件和自车行为变化时表现更稳健。

Conclusion: 闭环场景编排可作为受控、可复现的自动驾驶策略评测工具，并将场景实现与策略失败解耦，提升仿真的评测效度。

Abstract: Scenario-based simulation is central to evaluating autonomous-driving policies, but a scenario scripted before execution (i.e., with predetermined, fixed actor routes and speeds) may not preserve its intended interaction once the tested ego policy responds in closed loop. Scripted scenarios cannot adapt to unanticipated ego behavior, while learned traffic actors may remain reactive without reliably realizing a particular scenario intent. We introduce a scenario orchestrator that continuously monitors the joint traffic state and adapts non-ego actors through re-timing, re-routing, and role reassignment for creating a specific scenario intent. At each step, the orchestrator solves a constrained optimization problem that preserves the intended interaction while limiting interventions and maintaining traffic feasibility. We further distinguish scenario realization from policy failure, allowing a test to first be judged based on whether the intended interaction occurred and then by how the ego policy responded. Experiments in the CARLA simulator across multiple scenario families and driving policies show that orchestration realizes targeted interactions more reliably than scripted and learned actor-level baselines, particularly under variations in initial conditions and ego behavior. These results support closed-loop scenario orchestration as a tool for controlled and reproducible policy evaluation.

</details>


### [168] [On Agentic Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09892)
*Chen Chen,Omid Bagherieh,Naveen Nikamanth Appiah Balaji,Jesse T. Gonzalez,Negin Heravi,Fahad Islam,Xueting Li,Jean-Baptiste Regli,Kejia Ren,Petr Švec,David J. Yoon,Matthias Müller,Vladlen Koltun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"agentic manipulation policy"(AMP)范式：用通用AI模型直接闭环指挥机械臂，无需示教或任务特化训练。AMP1统一harness在单臂/双臂/移动操作上零样本达SOTA：RoboLab-120成功率85.6%（VLA 49.2%、WAM 42.9%）；108次真机试验成功104次（12任务、3台机器人），失败率较VLA/WAM降20x/15x；加入持久记忆后双臂任务失败率再降4.6x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作研究以"专家策略"为主，依赖大规模示教数据训练VLA或WAM，泛化性与鲁棒性受限。作者假设：通用AI模型作为闭环agent直接操控机器人，可能比专门训练的专家策略更可靠、更通用，并试图系统性地验证这一假设。

Method: 构建参考harness AMP1：通用AI模型（含视觉输入与工具调用）直接输出机械臂控制指令，随任务推进自适应调整动作；同一套代码不经修改即可用于单臂、双臂与移动平台，基础版本不含抓取/推/放置等原语技能。扩充版本引入跨episode持久记忆存储（memory representation），使agent从历史经验中学习。

Result: RoboLab-120零样本成功率85.6%，显著高于领先VLA(49.2%)与WAM(42.9%)；真机108次试验零样本成功104次（12个任务、3台机器人实例），失败率相对强VLA/WAM基线降低20x与15x；agent自发形成搜索隐藏物体、以物为工具、相机自标定等策略；加入记忆模块后RoboDojo双臂任务失败率较零样本下降4.6x。

Conclusion: AMPs在泛化性与鲁棒性上明显优于专家式操作策略，且具备跨形态通用性与经验学习潜力。但核心控制依赖通用AI模型（黑盒、推理时延与安全性未充分讨论），"agent即策略"能否替代示范学习仍是开放的对照问题，可作为通用agent系统用于具身操作的重要基线参考。

Abstract: Recent work on robot manipulation has focused on specialist policies trained on demonstrations. We show that generalist AI models can control manipulators more reliably than specialist policies such as vision-language-action models (VLAs) and world-action models (WAMs). We systematically analyze agentic manipulation policies (AMPs), in which a generalist AI model commands the manipulator directly, adapting its actions as the task unfolds. We present AMP1, a reference harness that runs unchanged across single-arm, bimanual, and mobile manipulation. AMP1 achieves state-of-the-art results on the RoboLab-120 and CaP-Gym benchmarks, with no demonstrations or task-specific training. On RoboLab-120, AMP1 reaches an 85.6\% success rate zero-shot, compared to 49.2\% and 42.9\% for leading VLA and WAM policies. The agent succeeds zero-shot in 104 of 108 real-world trials spanning twelve tasks and three robot instances, reducing failure rates by 20x and 15x relative to strong VLA and WAM baselines. The basic harness supplies no grasping, pushing, or placing skills; the agent develops its own strategies, such as searching for hidden objects, using objects as tools, and calibrating its cameras. With the addition of a persistent memory store, the agent can also learn from experience: an augmented harness with a memory representation that is carried across episodes lowers the failure rate on RoboDojo bimanual manipulation tasks by 4.6x relative to zero-shot deployment. Our analysis suggests that AMPs are substantially more general and robust than specialist manipulation policies, providing a promising foundation for robot manipulation.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Dynamic GPU Voxel Grids and Voxel-sequential Processing in NanoVDB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08665)
*Eftychios Sifakis,Francis Williams,Christopher Horvath,Rohan Sawhney,Mark Harris,Jonathan Swartz,Ken Museth*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: NanoVDB 新增动态 GPU 拓扑算子（膨胀、合并、剪枝、跨分辨率粗化/细化）和体素顺序访问范式，使稀疏网格支持动态仿真并以接近峰值带宽执行点式/模板算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NanoVDB 的线性化内存布局与 fVDB 的 Index-Grid 已带来良好 GPU 性能与紧凑存储，但拓扑假定为静态，无法用于仿真与动态几何处理。

Method: 位并行动态拓扑算子（形态学膨胀、网格合并、剪枝、跨分辨率粗化与细化）；将活跃体素流式展开为扁平线性序列的体素顺序访问范式与配套数据结构，减少树遍历。

Result: 数毫秒内重建含数亿体素的网格；复杂模板算子接近峰值内存带宽，存储开销极小；在三维重建、流体仿真、大规模网格体素化基准上验证。

Conclusion: 解除 NanoVDB 的只读约束，使其可作为动态 GPU 仿真与几何操作的高性能主表示。

Abstract: We introduce two fundamental advances to the NanoVDB data structure: dynamic GPU-native topological updates, and a voxel-sequential access paradigm that dramatically accelerates point-wise and stencil-based operators on sparse grids. NanoVDB's linearized memory layout has delivered strong GPU performance, and the Index-Grid concept introduced in the fVDB framework [Williams et al 2024] provided topology/data separation and optimal compactness of storage in sparse machine learning workloads. However, all these prior iterations of NanoVDB assume a static topology that has precluded use of this data structure in simulation and dynamic geometric processes. We eliminate this limitation by introducing a suite of dynamic topology operators -- morphological dilation, grid merging, pruning, and cross-resolution coarsening and refinement. These operators use bit-parallel algorithms to reconstruct grids containing hundreds of millions of voxels in just a few milliseconds. We also introduce a voxel-sequential access paradigm and data structure for GPU kernels operating on sparse data. We stream active voxels as a flat linear sequence, minimizing or eliminating the need for tree traversal in the process. This achieves compute density and regularity that allow even complex stencil-based operators to approach peak memory bandwidth, with minimal storage overhead. Together, these contributions remove the read-only constraint from NanoVDB, establishing it as a high-performance primary representation for dynamic GPU simulation and geometry manipulation. We evaluate the efficacy of our newly introduced operators in benchmarks sourced from applications such as 3D reconstruction, fluid simulation, and large-scale mesh voxelization.

</details>


### [170] [Segmenting Geometric Textures of 3D Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08897)
*Weidan Xiong,Kaijie Qu,Shihao Feng,Shunqi Fan,Rongyu Xiong,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出面向三维网格中层几何纹理的分割方法：构建几何纹理分割基准，微调SAM3并基于DINO特征相似度聚类纹理区域，实现2D到3D提升，并提供交互式编辑与检索工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D分割方法聚焦高层语义部件，难以捕捉中层纹理；VLM图像分割在3D几何纹理上表现不足。

Method: 新几何纹理分割基准 + 微调SAM3 + DINO特征相似度聚类 + 2D→3D提升 + 交互式编辑/检索工具。

Result: 在几何纹理分割上显著优于现有方法，并支持模型编辑与基于几何/纹理相似度的检索应用。

Conclusion: 为3D几何纹理分割提供了基准、方法与工具链，可服务文物保护和3D打印等应用。

Abstract: Recognizing geometric texture patterns on 3D models is critical for applications such as heritage protection, model editing, and 3D printing. Existing 3D model segmentation methods predominantly focus on identifying high-level semantic parts, thereby failing to capture middle-level textures. While recent Vision-Language Models can perform image segmentation with appropriate text prompts, they still struggle to recognize 3D geometric textures. In this work, we present a method that segments 3D mesh models based on middle-level geometric textures. We further introduce a new geometric texture segmentation benchmark and fine-tune SAM3 accordingly. The segmented texture regions are then clustered based on DINO feature similarity and lifted from 2D to 3D. Additionally, we develop an interactive tool to enable both 3D model editing based on our segmentation results and model retrieval from the dataset based on geometric and texture similarity. Experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing methods in segmenting geometric textures and can facilitate various applications.

</details>
